```cpp
#pragma once

// ToDisplay (design M3 D3-D5): unpacked planes (R8_UINT/R16_UINT storage images read without a
// format) -> RGBA16F linear-light display image at frame size. shaders/to_display.slang implements
// the formulas of display.cpp (toDisplayReference) in float32.

#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <span>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/command_recorder.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/compute_pipeline.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "dvv/media/types.h"

namespace dvv::media::detail {

// Push constants of shaders/to_display.slang. The design's "float matrix[9]; float offset[3]" is
// stored as three float4 rows (matrix row r, then offset r), and planeLayout as a uint4, so every
// member is 16-byte aligned in the shader's push-constant block (arrays would get a 16-byte stride).
struct ToDisplayPush {
    std::array<float, 12> affine{};          // rows: m[3r], m[3r+1], m[3r+2], offset[r]
    std::array<std::uint32_t, 4> planeLayout{};  // component k: log2SubX | log2SubY << 4 | binding << 8
    std::uint32_t width = 0;                 // output = frame size
    std::uint32_t height = 0;
    std::uint32_t componentCount = 0;        // 1..4 (alpha ignored)
    std::uint32_t transfer = 0;              // 0 sRGB/unspecified, 1 BT.1886, 2 linear, 3 PQ, 4 HLG
    std::uint32_t bt2020ToBt709 = 0;         // 0 or 1
    std::array<std::uint32_t, 3> pad{};      // explicit, to 96 bytes
};
static_assert(sizeof(ToDisplayPush) == 96);
static_assert(offsetof(ToDisplayPush, planeLayout) == 48 && offsetof(ToDisplayPush, width) == 64 &&
              offsetof(ToDisplayPush, componentCount) == 72 &&
              offsetof(ToDisplayPush, bt2020ToBt709) == 80 && offsetof(ToDisplayPush, pad) == 84);

inline constexpr std::uint32_t TO_DISPLAY_GROUP = 8;       // [numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
inline constexpr std::uint32_t TO_DISPLAY_BINDINGS = 5;    // planes 0..3, output 4
inline constexpr VkFormat DISPLAY_IMAGE_FORMAT = VK_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT;

// For a frame of `format` (original or unpacked: the components are the same) and a resolved
// colour. Errc::Internal for an unknown format or an empty frame.
[[nodiscard]] Result<ToDisplayPush> toDisplayPush(FormatId format, const ColourParams& resolved,
                                                  std::uint32_t width, std::uint32_t height);

// The ToDisplay pipeline (5 storage images, 96 bytes of push constants). Errc::Unsupported when
// R8_UINT or R16_UINT lacks STORAGE_IMAGE or STORAGE_READ_WITHOUT_FORMAT, or R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT
// lacks STORAGE_IMAGE ("device <id> cannot read <format> storage images without a format (M3 R1)");
// Errc::Internal when the shader's reflected push block is not 96 bytes.
[[nodiscard]] Result<std::shared_ptr<gpu::ComputePipeline>> createToDisplayPipeline(
    gpu::ExecutionContext& context);

// Three distinct 1x1 R16_UINT storage images for unused plane bindings (an image may appear only
// once per dispatch). Made once per store.
struct ToDisplayDummies {
    std::array<std::shared_ptr<gpu::GpuImage>, 3> images;
};
[[nodiscard]] Result<ToDisplayDummies> createToDisplayDummies(gpu::ExecutionContext& context);

// Records one dispatch. Checks first (Errc::Internal, nothing recorded): planes.size() equals the
// format's componentCount; plane k has componentSize(format, k, w, h) and unpackedImageFormat of
// component k; output is w x h DISPLAY_IMAGE_FORMAT. Bindings: k < planes.size() -> planes[k],
// otherwise the next dummy; binding 4 -> output.
[[nodiscard]] Result<void> recordToDisplay(gpu::CommandRecorder& recorder,
                                           const gpu::ComputePipeline& pipeline,
                                           const ToDisplayPush& push, FormatId format,
                                           std::span<gpu::GpuImage* const> planes,
                                           gpu::GpuImage& output, const ToDisplayDummies& dummies);

}  // namespace dvv::media::detail
```

**Behaviour:**
- **`toDisplayPush(format, resolved, w, h)`:** `a = displayAffine(format, resolved)`; `affine[4r +
  i] = a.matrix[3r + i]` (i < 3), `affine[4r + 3] = a.offset[r]`; for `k < componentCount`:
  `planeLayout[k] = log2SubX | log2SubY << 4 | k << 8` (unpacked frames hold one plane per
  component in component order, so the binding of component k is k); unused entries 0;
  `componentCount = descriptor(format).componentCount`; `transfer`: Srgb/Unspecified 0, Bt1886 1,
  Linear 2, Pq 3, Hlg 4; `bt2020ToBt709 = resolved.primaries == Primaries::Bt2020`.
- **`createToDisplayPipeline`:** (1) the format checks above through `context.formatFeatures(f)`
  (`VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_IMAGE_BIT`, `VK_FORMAT_FEATURE_2_STORAGE_READ_WITHOUT_FORMAT_BIT`);
  the dummies' format R16_UINT is among them. (2) The reflected `pushConstantBuffer` block size of
  `dvv_media_shaders::toDisplay().reflectionJson` must be 96 (as M2's unpack check). (3)
  `context.createComputePipeline(dvv_media_shaders::toDisplay(), sizeof(ToDisplayPush),
  {.storageImages = TO_DISPLAY_BINDINGS})`.
- **Dummies:** `createImage({.width = 1, .height = 1, .format = VK_FORMAT_R16_UINT, .usage =
  VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT, .name = "to-display dummy <i>"})` × 3. They are never read (the shader
  reads only components `< componentCount`); the dispatch moves them to `GENERAL` on first use.
- **Converter `ToDisplay`** (in `makeGpuConverters`, after the M2 converters; holds the pipeline, the
  dummies, the context and the cpu executor as `shared_ptr`s):
  - `from() = {.unpacked = true, .api = Api::VkImage, .kind = MemKind::DeviceLocal}`,
    `to() = DISPLAY_STATE = {.unpacked = false, .api = Api::VkImage, .kind = MemKind::DeviceLocal,
    .display = true}`, `outputLayout() = VkOptimal`, `fidelity() = Derived`, `cost(desc) = 1.0 ×
    geometry(unpackedFormat(desc.format), w, h)->bytes`, `name() = "ToDisplay"`.
  - `run`: `input = std::get<VkImageB>(input_.binding)`; `resolved =
    resolveColour(input_.desc).params`; `push = co_try(toDisplayPush(desc.format, resolved, w, h))`;
    `output = co_try(context_->createImage({w, h, DISPLAY_IMAGE_FORMAT, STORAGE | TRANSFER_SRC,
    "display <origin>:<index>"}))`; one submission whose record function captures the pipeline, the
    `VkImageB` copy (its plane `shared_ptr`s), the output, the dummies and the push, and stores
    `recordToDisplay(…)`'s result in a captured `shared_ptr<Result<void>>` (as GpuUnpack does);
    `co_try(co_await context_->submit(*cpu_, …))`; `co_try(*checked)`; `co_return
    StoreAccess::makeFrame<VkPlanesFrame>(context.store, input_.desc, displayFormat(),
    displayColour(), context.output.domain, {output}, 1)`.
  - Errors: `createImage`/`submit` errors unchanged (OutOfMemory reaches the M2 evict-and-retry rule;
    `DeviceLost` passes through), recorder checks `Internal`.
- **GpuUnpack:** its `VkPlanesFrame` now gets `input_.desc.colour` as the colour (no other change).
- **`StateKey`** gains `bool display = false;` as its **last** member (positional initialisers in
  M2's planner tests stay valid). `DISPLAY_STATE` as above.
- **Store rules** (`frame_store.cpp`; keep every M2 rule otherwise):
  - The colour check before planning: a request whose format is the display format must have colour
    `displayColour()` (else `Unsupported "the display view has colour <displayColour id>, not <id>"`);
    any other request keeps M2's rule (`want.colour != rec.desc.colour` → `Unsupported "no colour
    conversion: …"`). This is the one colour-changing edge (D6).
  - `stateOf(want)`: `isDisplay(want.format)` → `{.unpacked = false, .api = want.api, .kind =
    want.domain.kind, .display = true}` (then the domain rule of M2 applies); otherwise as M2.
  - Planned views of pending entries: `format = state.display ? displayFormat() : (state.unpacked ?
    rec.unpacked : rec.desc.format)`, `colour = state.display ? displayColour() : rec.desc.colour`.
  - `acquireOriginal`: an origin whose `desc.format` is the display format → `Internal "the display
    format is never an original"` (before planning).
  - A host-only store registers no ToDisplay (M2's `createWith` drops converters with a memory kind the
    store has no tier for) and has no device domain, so a display view is `Unsupported` (M2's domain
    rule: "no converter path").
  - `FrameStoreOptions::maxPlanDepth` default 6 (design D6: Produce + HostToStaging + Upload +
    GpuUnpack + ToDisplay).
- **Synchronisation:** the plane images are bound through `[format("unknown")] RWTexture2D<uint>`,
  which dvv-gpu binds as writable storage images (M2 note); ToDisplay only reads them. Every dispatch
  starts with the recorder's automatic global barrier, so a concurrent Readback or another ToDisplay of
  the same planes is ordered by submission order. The sync-validation runs (`DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync`)
  are the proof (zero messages).

**Code** (binding: the shader must equal the CPU reference's formulas of Task 2 step by step):

```slang
// ToDisplay (design M3 D3-D5): one invocation per output pixel reads the code values of the
// unpacked planes (nearest chroma: x >> log2SubX), applies the affine map of displayAffine(),
// clamps, applies the transfer (sRGB/BT.1886/linear, or PQ/HLG tone mapped to SDR with the BT.2390
// EETF, 1000 -> 100 nits), converts BT.2020 primaries to BT.709 when asked, clamps and stores linear
// RGBA16F. Must match dvv::media::toDisplayReference (display.cpp) formula by formula.
struct ToDisplayPush
{
    float4 row0;          // R' = dot(row0.xyz, c) + row0.w
    float4 row1;          // G'
    float4 row2;          // B'
    uint4 planeLayout;    // component k: log2SubX | log2SubY << 4 | binding << 8
    uint width;           // output size = frame size
    uint height;
    uint componentCount;  // 1..4 (alpha ignored)
    uint transfer;        // 0 sRGB/unspecified, 1 BT.1886, 2 linear, 3 PQ, 4 HLG
    uint bt2020ToBt709;   // 1: BT.2020 -> BT.709 primaries in linear light
    uint pad0;
    uint pad1;
    uint pad2;
};

[[vk::push_constant]] ConstantBuffer<ToDisplayPush> pc;

[[vk::binding(0, 0)]] [format("unknown")] RWTexture2D<uint> plane0;
[[vk::binding(1, 0)]] [format("unknown")] RWTexture2D<uint> plane1;
[[vk::binding(2, 0)]] [format("unknown")] RWTexture2D<uint> plane2;
[[vk::binding(3, 0)]] [format("unknown")] RWTexture2D<uint> plane3;
[[vk::binding(4, 0)]] [format("rgba16f")] RWTexture2D<float4> display;

static const float PQ_M1 = 0.1593017578125;
static const float PQ_M2 = 78.84375;
static const float PQ_C1 = 0.8359375;
static const float PQ_C2 = 18.8515625;
static const float PQ_C3 = 18.6875;
static const float HLG_A = 0.17883277;
static const float HLG_B = 0.28466892;
static const float HLG_C = 0.55991073;
static const float SOURCE_PEAK_NITS = 1000.0;
static const float TARGET_PEAK_NITS = 100.0;

uint readPlane(uint binding, uint2 at)
{
    switch (binding)
    {
    case 0: return plane0[at];
    case 1: return plane1[at];
    case 2: return plane2[at];
    default: return plane3[at];
    }
}

float codeValue(uint k, uint2 pixel)
{
    const uint layout = pc.planeLayout[k];
    const uint2 at = uint2(pixel.x >> (layout & 15u), pixel.y >> ((layout >> 4u) & 15u));
    return float(readPlane((layout >> 8u) & 15u, at));
}

float srgbDecode(float x)
{
    x = clamp(x, 0.0, 1.0);
    return x <= 0.04045 ? x / 12.92 : pow((x + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
}

float pqToNits(float e)
{
    e = clamp(e, 0.0, 1.0);
    const float p = pow(e, 1.0 / PQ_M2);
    const float num = max(p - PQ_C1, 0.0);
    return 10000.0 * pow(num / (PQ_C2 - PQ_C3 * p), 1.0 / PQ_M1);
}

float nitsToPq(float nits)
{
    const float y = clamp(nits / 10000.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    const float p = pow(y, PQ_M1);
    return pow((PQ_C1 + PQ_C2 * p) / (1.0 + PQ_C3 * p), PQ_M2);
}

float eetf(float e)
{
    const float source = nitsToPq(SOURCE_PEAK_NITS);
    const float maxLum = nitsToPq(TARGET_PEAK_NITS) / source;
    const float ks = 1.5 * maxLum - 0.5;
    const float e1 = min(e / source, 1.0);
    float e2 = e1;
    if (e1 >= ks)
    {
        const float t = (e1 - ks) / (1.0 - ks);
        const float t2 = t * t;
        const float t3 = t2 * t;
        e2 = (2.0 * t3 - 3.0 * t2 + 1.0) * ks + (t3 - 2.0 * t2 + t) * (1.0 - ks) +
             (-2.0 * t3 + 3.0 * t2) * maxLum;
    }
    return e2 * source;
}

float pqToLinear(float e) { return pqToNits(eetf(e)) / TARGET_PEAK_NITS; }
float nitsToLinear(float nits) { return pqToLinear(nitsToPq(nits)); }

float hlgInverseOetf(float e)
{
    return e <= 0.5 ? e * e / 3.0 : (exp((e - HLG_C) / HLG_A) + HLG_B) / 12.0;
}

float3 toLinear(float3 v)
{
    switch (pc.transfer)
    {
    case 1u: return float3(pow(v.x, 2.4), pow(v.y, 2.4), pow(v.z, 2.4));
    case 2u: return v;
    case 3u: return float3(pqToLinear(v.x), pqToLinear(v.y), pqToLinear(v.z));
    case 4u:
    {
        const float3 e = float3(hlgInverseOetf(v.x), hlgInverseOetf(v.y), hlgInverseOetf(v.z));
        const float ys = 0.2627 * e.x + 0.6780 * e.y + 0.0593 * e.z;
        const float gain = SOURCE_PEAK_NITS * pow(ys, 0.2);
        return float3(nitsToLinear(gain * e.x), nitsToLinear(gain * e.y), nitsToLinear(gain * e.z));
    }
    default: return float3(srgbDecode(v.x), srgbDecode(v.y), srgbDecode(v.z));
    }
}

[shader("compute")]
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void main(uint3 id: SV_DispatchThreadID)
{
    if (id.x >= pc.width || id.y >= pc.height)
    {
        return;
    }
    float3 c = float3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    c.x = codeValue(0u, id.xy);
    if (pc.componentCount >= 3u)
    {
        c.y = codeValue(1u, id.xy);
        c.z = codeValue(2u, id.xy);
    }
    float3 v = float3(pc.row0.x * c.x + pc.row0.y * c.y + pc.row0.z * c.z + pc.row0.w,
                      pc.row1.x * c.x + pc.row1.y * c.y + pc.row1.z * c.z + pc.row1.w,
                      pc.row2.x * c.x + pc.row2.y * c.y + pc.row2.z * c.z + pc.row2.w);
    v = clamp(v, 0.0, 1.0);
    float3 rgb = toLinear(v);
    if (pc.bt2020ToBt709 != 0u)
    {
        rgb = float3(1.660491 * rgb.x - 0.587641 * rgb.y - 0.072850 * rgb.z,
                     -0.124550 * rgb.x + 1.132900 * rgb.y - 0.008349 * rgb.z,
                     -0.018151 * rgb.x - 0.100579 * rgb.y + 1.118730 * rgb.z);
    }
    display[id.xy] = float4(clamp(rgb, 0.0, 1.0), 1.0);
}
```

Use the `format("unknown")` spelling M2 Task 5 confirmed (or its `SLANGC_OPTIONS` fallback). If slangc
reports an implicit conversion warning (promoted to an error), add the explicit casts; never change
the formulas. `pow(0, y)` for `y > 0` is 0 on every target; every `pow` base here is ≥ 0.

C++ cannot mix positional and designated initialisers, so write them all designated (`ColourParams{.matrix = ColourMatrix::Custom, .range = ColourRange::Limited, .kr = 0.212f, .kb = 0.087f}`).

**Notes:**
- Design D3, D4, D5, D6, §4.2 (push constants), §7 (display rows), §8.1 dvv-media store/gpu rows,
  §8.3 (ToDisplay vs CPU reference, store e2e), E2, R1, R8.
- Note (plan choice): the push-constant layout keeps the design's 96 bytes and content but stores the
  affine as three `float4` rows (matrix row + offset) and `planeLayout` as a `uint4`, because arrays in
  a Slang push-constant block get a 16-byte element stride (the same reason as M2's `uint3 slots`).
- Note (plan choice): `VkPlanesFrame` takes the view's colour (display frames have `displayColour()`,
  planes keep the frame's colour) and computes `bytes()` from the view format's geometry, which equals
  M2's per-plane sum for unpacked formats and gives `w·h·8` for display frames.
- Note (plan choice): `StateKey` gets a trailing `display` flag instead of a three-valued format
  state, so M2's positional `StateKey` initialisers and converters stay unchanged.
- Note (plan choice): the PQ/HLG tolerance adds an absolute floor of 1e-5 to the design's relative
  1e-3, because half floats below 6.1e-5 are subnormal and cannot meet a relative bound.
- Fallback R1 (typed `r8ui`/`r16ui` pipelines) is not built: Task 0 recorded the feature on NVIDIA,
  and Task 1 made it required.

### Task 4: rawreel_core skeleton and pure logic

**Goal:** Create the wx-free `rawreel_core` library with the pure, unit-tested logic the player and the
GUI decide with: the fit-to-window view transform (D23), the player logic (clock, stall,
step/seek, loop, prefetch window, in-flight cap), the reduced command table with the key normaliser
and menu labels, the format-dialog model, one-file drop routing, and the Session value types
(`session_types.h`, `MAX_STREAMS = 1`). Add `rawreel_core_tests` under every preset (design v2 D2,
D11, D17, D21, D23, §4.3).

**Files** (rawreel):
- `src/core/view.h`, `src/core/view.cpp`, `tests/test_view.cpp`: see the **`view` section** below
  (`MAX_STREAMS`, `PointD`, `ViewTransform::fit`; fit only).
- Create `src/core/player.h`, `src/core/player.cpp`: `Clock`, `PlayerConfig`, `PlayerStatus`,
  `PlayerLogic`.
- Create `src/core/commands.h`, `src/core/commands.cpp`: `Command`, `Key`, `KeyChord`, `KeyRoute`,
  `CommandInfo`, `commandTable`, `commandForKey`, `normaliseKey`, `keyText`, `menuLabel`.
- Create `src/core/format_choice.h`, `src/core/format_choice.cpp`: `FormatChoice`,
  `choiceFromCandidate`, `choiceFromInfo`, `applyChoice`, `validateChoice`.
- `src/core/drop.h`, `src/core/drop.cpp`, `tests/test_drop.cpp`: see the **`drop` section** below
  (`fileUri`, `DropPlan`, `routeDrop`; one stream).
- Create `src/core/session_types.h` (header only): the Session value types and interfaces.
- Modify `CMakeLists.txt`: target `rawreel_core` (block below), outside the `if(NOT DVV_SANITIZE)`
  block that guards `rawreel_gui`.
- Create `tests/core_main.cpp` (doctest main ending with `dvv::finishWithLeakCheck`, like M2's
  `tests/cli_main.cpp`), `tests/test_player.cpp`, `tests/test_commands.cpp`,
  `tests/test_format_choice.cpp`, `tests/test_session_types.cpp`; modify `tests/CMakeLists.txt`.
- Not created (cut by U-SCOPE, design v2 §13): `src/core/recovery.{h,cpp}` and
  `tests/test_recovery.cpp` (`RecoveryPolicy` → v1.x).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: dvv-media (`Rational`, `FrameDesc`, `ColourParams`, `ColourMatrix::Custom` and
  `validateColour` of Task 2, `ResolvedColour` of Task 2's `display.h`, `SourceInfo`, `Candidate`,
  `Ambiguous`, `SourceRequest`, `ReadLease`, `findFormat`, `descriptor`, `geometry`,
  `utf8FromPath`, `pathFromUtf8`), dvv-base (`Result`, `makeError`, `Errc`, `Uri`, `parseUri`,
  `formatUri`, `percentEncode`), dvv-gpu (`ExecutionContext` forward use in `ISessionEvents`),
  dvv-platform (`PixelSize`). Because rawreel's build compiles the dvv-media tree, this task runs
  after Task 2 is committed.
- Produces (namespace `rawreel`; binding names and semantics; Tasks 5-12 rely on them): the headers
  of the `view` and `drop` sections below, and:

```cpp
// src/core/player.h — pure; no threads, no clock of its own.
using Clock = std::chrono::steady_clock;
struct PlayerConfig {
    std::optional<std::int64_t> length;     // frames of the shortest stream; nullopt = unbounded
    dvv::media::Rational fps{25, 1};        // stream 1's fps
    std::uint32_t window = 8;               // W: prefetch indices ahead of the position
    std::uint32_t inflight = 4;             // K: at most this many requested and not yet answered
};
struct PlayerStatus {
    std::int64_t position = -1;             // the last shown index (-1: none since the last reset)
    std::optional<std::int64_t> length;
    bool playing = false, loop = false, stalled = false;
    double targetFps = 0, actualFps = 0;
    std::uint64_t shown = 0, stalls = 0;
};
class PlayerLogic {
public:
    explicit PlayerLogic(PlayerConfig config);           // no stream set: nothing to show
    void reset(PlayerConfig config, std::int64_t position);  // new stream set; pauses; drops readiness
    void play(Clock::time_point now);
    void pause();
    void stop();                                         // pause + seek(0)
    void step(std::int64_t delta);                       // pauses; clamps; sets the direction
    void seek(std::int64_t index);                       // pauses; clamps; sets the direction
    void setLoop(bool loop);
    void setWindow(std::uint32_t window);                // plan addition (OutOfMemory halving)
    // All streams of `index` are ready. True when the index is kept (inside keeps()); false: the
    // caller releases the leases. Always clears the outstanding mark.
    bool frameReady(std::int64_t index, Clock::time_point now);
    void frameFailed(std::int64_t index);  // pauses when the failed index is due (P3)
    void tick(Clock::time_point now);
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::int64_t> takeShow();      // index to show now (once)
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::int64_t> takeRequests();    // to acquire now (cap, window)
    [[nodiscard]] std::pair<std::int64_t, std::int64_t> keepRange() const;  // raw, may wrap
    [[nodiscard]] bool keeps(std::int64_t index) const;  // plan addition: keepRange, modulo when looping
    [[nodiscard]] std::optional<Clock::time_point> nextDeadline() const;
    [[nodiscard]] PlayerStatus status(Clock::time_point now) const;
};
```

```cpp
// src/core/commands.h — wx-free (D17), the reduced table of design v2 §4.3
enum class Command : std::uint16_t {
    Open, OpenUri, CloseStreams, Quit,
    PlayPause, Stop, StepBack, StepForward, StepBack10, StepForward10, First, Last, Loop,
    Format, Reopen, GpuInfo, Diagnostics,
};
inline constexpr std::size_t COMMAND_COUNT = static_cast<std::size_t>(Command::Diagnostics) + 1;
// The full key set stays (the normaliser is kept as designed; Plus, Minus, Escape and F11 have no
// command in v1.0 and serve v1.x zoom and fullscreen keys).
enum class Key : std::int32_t {   // letters and digits: their ASCII upper-case code
    Space = ' ', Plus = '+', Minus = '-',
    Left = 0x10000, Right, Up, Down, Home, End, Escape, F11,
};
struct KeyChord {
    std::int32_t key = 0;
    bool ctrl = false, shift = false, alt = false;
    bool operator==(const KeyChord&) const = default;
};
[[nodiscard]] constexpr KeyChord chord(Key key, bool ctrl = false, bool shift = false) noexcept {
    return {static_cast<std::int32_t>(key), ctrl, shift, false};
}
enum class KeyRoute : std::uint8_t { Accelerator, CharHook };
struct CommandInfo {
    Command command;
    std::string_view id, menu, label, help;
    std::optional<KeyChord> key;
    KeyRoute route;
};
[[nodiscard]] std::span<const CommandInfo> commandTable() noexcept;   // in Command order
// CharHook commands only; nullopt when `text_focus` (an editable wxTextEntry has focus) or no match.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<Command> commandForKey(const KeyChord& chord, bool text_focus) noexcept;
// wx 3.3 key code + modifiers -> KeyChord (below). Unknown codes keep their value.
[[nodiscard]] KeyChord normaliseKey(std::int32_t keycode, bool ctrl, bool shift, bool alt) noexcept;
// "Ctrl+O", "Ctrl+Shift+O", "F11", "Space", "Shift+Left", "+", "Esc", "1", "L".
[[nodiscard]] std::string keyText(const KeyChord& chord);
// Accelerator with a key: "<label>\t<keyText>"; CharHook: "<label> (<keyText>)"; no key: "<label>".
[[nodiscard]] std::string menuLabel(const CommandInfo& info);
```

```cpp
// src/core/format_choice.h — the format dialog's model (D21)
struct FormatChoice {
    std::string format = "yuv420p";          // catalogue name or "@<UTF-8 path to .json>"
    std::uint32_t width = 1920, height = 1080;
    dvv::media::Rational fps{25, 1};
    dvv::media::ColourParams colour{};       // Unspecified fields: the source decides
    bool operator==(const FormatChoice&) const = default;
};
[[nodiscard]] FormatChoice choiceFromCandidate(const dvv::media::Candidate& candidate);
[[nodiscard]] FormatChoice choiceFromInfo(const dvv::media::SourceInfo& info);
// Removes fmt, size, w, h, fps, matrix, kr, kb, range, transfer, primaries from the URI's query,
// keeps every other parameter in order, then appends fmt, size (WxH), fps (N or N/D), and the
// colour fields that are not Unspecified: matrix (with kr and kb for Custom), range, transfer,
// primaries. Formats with dvv::formatUri. Errc::Format for an unparsable URI or an invalid choice.
[[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<std::string> applyChoice(std::string_view uri, const FormatChoice& choice);
// Errc::Format: catalogue name unknown; geometry() of the format at the size fails; "@" with an
// empty path; width or height 0; fps num <= 0 or den <= 0; validateColour() fails.
[[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<void> validateChoice(const FormatChoice& choice);
```

```cpp
// src/core/session_types.h (complete; header only)
#pragma once

// Value types and interfaces of the Session (design M3 v2 §4.3). Leases are shared, so the
// presenter thread can keep using a FrameSet while a newer one arrives; events never carry strong
// references to managed objects (D10).

#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "core/player.h"
#include "core/view.h"  // MAX_STREAMS (= 1, the view section)
#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/media/display.h"
#include "dvv/media/frame_store.h"
#include "dvv/media/source.h"
#include "dvv/media/source_factory.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {
class ExecutionContext;
}

namespace rawreel {

struct StreamInfo {
    std::uint32_t slot = 0;
    std::string resolvedUri;
    dvv::media::SourceInfo info;
    dvv::media::ResolvedColour colour;
};
// One shown frame: the display views of every stream for one index (one stream in v1.0; the array
// keeps the v1.x tile shape, D2).
struct FrameSet {
    std::int64_t index = -1;
    std::uint32_t count = 0;
    std::array<std::shared_ptr<const dvv::media::ReadLease>, MAX_STREAMS> views{};
    std::uint64_t epoch = 0;   // the Session's stackEpoch (D7)
};
struct SessionStatus {
    PlayerStatus player;
    std::uint32_t streams = 0;
    std::uint64_t epoch = 0;
    bool gpuAvailable = false;     // a stack is installed
    std::string bottleneck;
    std::uint32_t inflight = 0;    // request tasks running
    std::uint32_t readyAhead = 0;  // ready-map entries after the position
};  // written at the end of each pump(); tests wait on inflight/readyAhead

class IDisplaySink {  // thread-safe, non-blocking (the viewport's DisplayState)
public:
    virtual ~IDisplaySink() = default;
    virtual void show(FrameSet frames) = 0;
    virtual void clear() = 0;  // drops every lease it holds
};
class ISessionEvents {  // called on the Session executor; must not block (GUI: CallAfter)
public:
    virtual ~ISessionEvents() = default;
    virtual void streamsChanged(std::vector<StreamInfo> streams) = 0;  // incl. warnings
    virtual void formatNeeded(std::uint32_t slot, dvv::media::SourceRequest request,
                              dvv::media::Ambiguous candidates, std::string reason) = 0;
    // At start, after a loss (expired weak_ptr: no GPU stack) and after every reopen().
    virtual void stackChanged(std::weak_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context,
                              std::uint64_t epoch) = 0;
    // Non-fatal: infobar + log. Errc::DeviceLost -> the GUI shows the Reopen button.
    virtual void error(dvv::Error error) = 0;
};  // removed from the first M3 design: probed, exportFinished, deviceLostLimit
class IClock {
public:
    virtual ~IClock() = default;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual Clock::time_point now() const = 0;
};
class ITimer {  // one pending deadline; schedule() replaces it
public:
    virtual ~ITimer() = default;
    virtual void schedule(Clock::time_point when, std::move_only_function<void()> callback) = 0;
    // Binding: blocks until a running callback has returned; must not be called from inside a
    // callback. ThreadTimer's destructor implies cancel().
    virtual void cancel() = 0;
};

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- CMake:

target_link_libraries(rawreel_core PUBLIC dvv::media)

The `view` section (header, behaviour, unit cases and steps of `src/core/view.{h,cpp}` and
`tests/test_view.cpp`; fit only):


#### View: fit to window (`src/core/view.{h,cpp}`; design D2, D23, §4.3, §8.1 core-unit row)

**Goal of this section:** the view math of the one-stream, fit-only player. `ViewTransform::fit`
places the image in the viewport at `zoom = min(W/w, H/h)` (up- and down-scaling, no clamp), centred,
with the image's top-left corner on a **whole viewport pixel**, so an integer zoom maps every image
pixel to exactly zoom × zoom viewport pixels. Nothing else: zoom steps, pan, tiles, region zoom, grid
snapping and overlays are v1.x (design §13) and are not written.

**Files** (rawreel):
- Create `src/core/view.h` (complete header below) and `src/core/view.cpp` (`ViewTransform::fit` and
  the accessors).
- Create `tests/test_view.cpp` (`rawreel_core_tests`, `TEST_SUITE("core")`).
- `src/core/view.cpp` is in `rawreel_core`'s source list and `test_view.cpp` in
  `rawreel_core_tests`'s list (Task 4's CMake blocks).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: dvv-platform `dvv::platform::PixelSize` (`dvv/platform/native_window.h`:
  `std::uint32_t width, height`, `empty()`, `operator==`). It reaches `rawreel_core` through
  `dvv::media`. If the build reports it missing, add `dvv::platform` to `rawreel_core`'s
  `target_link_libraries`.
- Produces (`src/core/view.h`, complete, binding):

```cpp
#pragma once

// The fit-to-window view of design M3 v2 (D23). Viewport sizes are physical pixels, image sizes
// image pixels; pixel (i, j) covers [i, i+1) x [j, j+1). The compositor maps output pixel (px, py)
// to the image point (originX + (px + 0.5) / zoom, originY + (py + 0.5) / zoom) (design §4.4).

#include <cstdint>

#include "dvv/platform/native_window.h"

namespace rawreel {

inline constexpr std::uint32_t MAX_STREAMS = 1;  // D2: one stream in v1.0; v1.x tiles raise it

struct PointD {
    double x = 0, y = 0;
    bool operator==(const PointD&) const = default;
};

class ViewTransform {  // value type
public:
    ViewTransform() = default;  // viewport 0x0, image 0x0, zoom 0, origin (0, 0)

    // zoom = min(W/w, H/h) with no clamp. Width-limited iff W·h <= H·w (64-bit integers; a tie is
    // width-limited). Width-limited: zoom = W/w, x offset 0, y offset = (H·w − h·W) / (2·w);
    // height-limited: zoom = H/h, y offset 0, x offset = (W·h − w·H) / (2·h); offsets by integer
    // division (whole viewport pixels). origin = −offset / zoom on each axis.
    // An empty viewport or image -> zoom 0, origin (0, 0) (only the background is drawn).
    [[nodiscard]] static ViewTransform fit(dvv::platform::PixelSize viewport,
                                           dvv::platform::PixelSize image);

    [[nodiscard]] double zoom() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] PointD origin() const noexcept;  // the image point at the viewport's (0, 0)
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::platform::PixelSize viewport() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::platform::PixelSize image() const noexcept;

    bool operator==(const ViewTransform&) const = default;

private:
    dvv::platform::PixelSize viewport_{};
    dvv::platform::PixelSize image_{};
    double zoom_ = 0;
    PointD origin_{};
};

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Later tasks rely on: Task 7's `ViewportRenderer::frame` computes
  `ViewTransform::fit(info.extent, display image size)` and passes `zoom()`, `origin().x` and
  `origin().y` as the push constants `zoom`, `originX`, `originY`. Task 10's `player` scenario checks
  `lastRecorded().zoom == ViewTransform::fit(extent, {64, 48}).zoom()`. `session_types.h`
  (`FrameSet::views`) uses `MAX_STREAMS`.

**Behaviour** (`fit`, binding; the unit cases compare with `==`, so use exactly these expressions):
1. Store `viewport` and `image` as given. If `viewport.empty() || image.empty()`, return with
   `zoom_ = 0` and `origin_ = {0, 0}` (no division).
2. `const std::uint64_t W = viewport.width, H = viewport.height, w = image.width, h = image.height;`
   (all four are 64-bit before any product; `(2^32 − 1)²` fits in 64 bits).
3. `if (W * h <= H * w)` (width-limited): `zoom_ = static_cast<double>(W) / static_cast<double>(w)`,
   `offX = 0`, `offY = (H * w - h * W) / (2 * w)`. Else (height-limited):
   `zoom_ = static_cast<double>(H) / static_cast<double>(h)`, `offY = 0`,
   `offX = (W * h - w * H) / (2 * h)`. The subtraction is never negative in its branch.
4. `origin_ = {-static_cast<double>(offX) / zoom_, -static_cast<double>(offY) / zoom_}`.
5. The accessors return the stored values. No other function is declared.

**Tests first** (`tests/test_view.cpp`; every case name starts with `view: ` so
`--test-case="view:*"` selects them). Helpers in the test file:
`offsetX(t) = -t.origin().x * t.zoom()`, `offsetY(t)` likewise;
`column(t, px) = std::floor(t.origin().x + (px + 0.5) / t.zoom())`, `row(t, py)` likewise (the
compositor's pixel rule of design §4.4). `static_assert(MAX_STREAMS == 1);` at file scope.
1. `view: pillarbox upscale (37x23 in 211x97)` — `t = fit({211, 97}, {37, 23})`:
   `t.zoom() == 97.0 / 23.0`; `t.origin().x == -27.0 / (97.0 / 23.0)`; `t.origin().y == 0.0`;
   `std::abs(offsetX(t) - 27.0) <= 1e-9` and `offsetY(t) == 0.0` (the expected offsets (27, 0):
   `W·h = 4853 > H·w = 3589`, so height-limited, `(4853 − 3589) / 46 = 27`, 27.48 floored);
   `t.viewport() == PixelSize{211, 97}`; `t.image() == PixelSize{37, 23}`.
2. `view: letterbox upscale (64x48 in 400x400)` — `t.zoom() == 400.0 / 64.0` (6.25);
   `t.origin().x == 0.0`; `t.origin().y == -50.0 / (400.0 / 64.0)` (−8); `std::abs(offsetY(t) -
   50.0) <= 1e-9`.
3. `view: 1:1 and the tie` — `fit({37, 23}, {37, 23})`: `zoom() == 1.0`, `origin() == PointD{0, 0}`
   (`W·h == H·w`: width-limited, both offsets 0).
4. `view: downscale (1920x1080 in 640x480)` — `zoom() == 640.0 / 1920.0`; `origin().x == 0.0`;
   `origin().y == -60.0 / (640.0 / 1920.0)`; `std::abs(offsetY(t) - 60.0) <= 1e-9`.
5. `view: integer zoom covers whole pixels (37x23 in 74x80)` — `zoom() == 2.0`;
   `origin().x == 0.0`; `origin().y == -8.5` (offset 17). For every `px` in `[0, 74)`:
   `column(t, px) == px / 2` (integer division). For every `py` in `[0, 80)`: `py < 17` or
   `py >= 63` → `row(t, py) < 0` or `row(t, py) >= 23`; otherwise `row(t, py) == (py - 17) / 2`.
   So each image pixel covers exactly 2×2 viewport pixels (the compositor golden 74×80 of §8.3
   relies on it).
6. `view: an odd remainder is floored (10x5 in 30x20)` — `zoom() == 3.0`; offset y `(200 − 150) / 20
   = 2` (2.5 floored): `origin().y == -2.0 / 3.0`; rows `py` in `[2, 17)` give
   `row(t, py) == (py - 2) / 3`, rows 0, 1, 17, 18 and 19 are outside `[0, 5)`; every column
   `px` in `[0, 30)` gives `column(t, px) == px / 3`.
7. `view: 64-bit products (70000x70000 in 70000x1)` — `W·h = 4900000000` overflows 32 bits:
   `zoom() == 1.0 / 70000.0`; `origin().y == 0.0`; `origin().x == -34999.0 / (1.0 / 70000.0)`
   (height-limited; offset `(4900000000 − 70000) / 140000 = 34999`, 34999.5 floored). A 32-bit
   implementation computes `W·h = 605032704` and offset 4321, so it fails this case.
8. `view: empty sizes` — `ViewTransform{}`: `zoom() == 0.0`, `origin() == PointD{0, 0}`,
   `viewport() == PixelSize{0, 0}`, `image() == PixelSize{0, 0}`. `fit({0, 0}, {37, 23})`,
   `fit({211, 0}, {37, 23})` and `fit({211, 97}, {0, 23})`: `zoom() == 0.0`,
   `origin() == PointD{0, 0}`, and `viewport()`/`image()` equal the arguments.

First failure: the build fails (no `core/view.h`). Then, with a stub `fit` that returns
`ViewTransform{}`, all 8 cases fail (case 8 fails on `viewport() == PixelSize{211, 0}`).

**Steps** (inside Task 4's "tests first" and "implement" steps; run from the workspace root):
- [ ] **V1: Tests first.** Write `view.h` (above), a stub `view.cpp` (`fit` returns
  `ViewTransform{}`, accessors return the members) and `tests/test_view.cpp`. Run
  `Push-Location rawreel; cmake --preset vs2026; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 -R "rawreel\.core_unit" --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`.
  Expected: builds; the 8 `view:` cases fail.
- [ ] **V2: Implement** `fit` per Behaviour 1-5 and rerun the V1 command. Expected: the 8 `view:`
  cases pass. (The Per-task checks and the commit are Task 4's final steps; the `vs2026` Release
  and `vs2026-asan` runs of the Task 6 checkpoint check the exact float expressions in other
  configurations. U-WIN dropped the earlier V3 Linux run of them.)

**Expected results:** 8 `view:` cases in `rawreel.core_unit`, green under `vs2026` Debug (Release and
`vs2026-asan` at the Task 6 checkpoint).

**Notes:**
- Design D23 (fit only; the renderer computes it from the presenter's extent), §4.3 `ViewTransform`
  comment (integer offsets, arbiter v2 FIX 8), §8.1 core-unit row, §8.3 goldens 74×80 and 211×97.
- Note (plan choice): `PointD` and `ViewTransform` get a defaulted `operator==`, and
  `ViewTransform` default-constructs to all zero (zoom 0 = nothing drawn). The design's comment
  leaves both open.
- Note (plan choice): the unit cases compare `zoom()` and `origin()` with `==` against the same
  double expressions as Behaviour 3-4 (IEEE division is exact-rounded on every toolchain of the
  matrix); the offsets are checked within 1e-9 because `−origin·zoom` rounds.
- Do not add any v1 view function (`TILE_GAP`, `MIN_ZOOM`/`MAX_ZOOM`, `PixelRect`, tiles,
  `imageAt`, `viewportAt`, `zoomedAt`, `withZoom`, `panned`, `fitted`, `resized`, `nextZoomStep`,
  `WHEEL_NOTCH_FACTOR`, `snapToGrid`, `OverlayState`, `PIXEL_GRID_ZOOM`): they are v1.x (§13).

The `drop` section (header, behaviour, unit cases and steps of `src/core/drop.{h,cpp}` and
`tests/test_drop.cpp`; one stream):


#### Drop: one stream (`src/core/drop.{h,cpp}`; design D2, D21, §4.3, §7 row "Several files dropped")

**Goal of this section:** the wx-free rule for dropped (or several selected) files in the
one-stream player. The first file opens and replaces the stream; the rest are counted as ignored.

**Files** (rawreel):
- Create `src/core/drop.h` (complete header below) and `src/core/drop.cpp`.
- Create `tests/test_drop.cpp` (`rawreel_core_tests`, `TEST_SUITE("core")`).
- `src/core/drop.cpp` is in `rawreel_core`'s source list and `test_drop.cpp` in
  `rawreel_core_tests`'s list (Task 4's CMake blocks).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: dvv-base `dvv::percentEncode(std::string_view text, std::string_view keep = "/:\\")`
  and `dvv::parseUri` (`dvv/base/uri.h`); dvv-media `dvv::media::utf8FromPath` (M2; Task 4's Step 1
  checks the header that declares it).
- Produces (`src/core/drop.h`, complete, binding):

```cpp
#pragma once

// Drop routing for one stream (design M3 v2 D2, D21).

#include <cstddef>
#include <filesystem>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <string>

namespace rawreel {

// "file:" + percentEncode(utf8FromPath(path)) with dvv-base's default keep set "/:\\".
[[nodiscard]] std::string fileUri(const std::filesystem::path& path);

// The first file opens (it replaces the stream); `ignored` = the number of other files (the GUI
// logs "<ignored> files ignored: one stream in v1.0"). No files -> uri nullopt, ignored 0.
struct DropPlan {
    std::optional<std::string> uri;
    std::size_t ignored = 0;
    bool operator==(const DropPlan&) const = default;
};
[[nodiscard]] DropPlan routeDrop(std::span<const std::filesystem::path> files);

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Later tasks rely on: Task 10's `MainFrame::openFiles(files)` (drop target on the viewport and on
  the player panel, File → Open): `plan = routeDrop(files)`; `plan.uri` → `dvv::parseUri(*plan.uri)`
  (an error → log + infobar) → `session.open(0, {.uri = *uri})` with no `start` (frame 0, §4.3);
  `plan.ignored > 0` → `DVV_LOG_INFO("{} files ignored: one stream in v1.0", plan.ignored)`. Task 10
  owns that GUI code; this section owns only the rule.

**Behaviour:**
- `fileUri(p) = "file:" + dvv::percentEncode(dvv::media::utf8FromPath(p))` (default keep set).
- `routeDrop(files)`: `files.empty()` → `DropPlan{}`; otherwise
  `DropPlan{.uri = fileUri(files.front()), .ignored = files.size() - 1}`. No file is opened, read or
  checked here: an unknown size or format is the Session's `formatNeeded` (D21).

**Tests first** (`tests/test_drop.cpp`; case names start with `drop: `; `routeDrop({a, b})` below
means a `std::vector<std::filesystem::path>{a, b}` passed as the span, because `std::span` has no
initializer-list constructor in C++23):
1. `drop: fileUri round trips` — for `std::filesystem::path(u8"clip ä.yuv")`,
   `std::filesystem::path("a?b#c%d&e.yuv")` and an absolute path (`#if defined(_WIN32)`
   `std::filesystem::path(std::string("C:") + "\\clips\\x.yuv")`, else
   `std::filesystem::path("/data/x.yuv")`): `auto uri = dvv::parseUri(fileUri(p))`; `REQUIRE(uri)`;
   `uri->scheme == "file"`; `uri->path == dvv::media::utf8FromPath(p)`; and `fileUri(p)` starts with
   `"file:"` and contains no `?` or `#` after it (they are percent-encoded).
2. `drop: one file opens` — `routeDrop({a})` with `a = "clip_16x16_gray.yuv"` →
   `== DropPlan{fileUri(a), 0}`.
3. `drop: the first of several opens, the rest are ignored` — `routeDrop({a, b, c})` →
   `== DropPlan{fileUri(a), 2}`; `routeDrop({})` → `== DropPlan{}` (`!uri`, `ignored == 0`).

First failure: the build fails (no `core/drop.h`). With stubs (`fileUri` returns `""`, `routeDrop`
returns `DropPlan{}`), cases 1, 2 and 3 fail (case 3 on the three-file plan).

**Steps** (inside Task 4's "tests first" and "implement" steps; run from the workspace root):
- [ ] **D1: Tests first.** Write `drop.h`, stub `drop.cpp` and `tests/test_drop.cpp`. Run
  `Push-Location rawreel; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 -R "rawreel\.core_unit" --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`.
  Expected: the 3 `drop:` cases fail.
- [ ] **D2: Implement** `fileUri` and `routeDrop`; rerun the D1 command. Expected: the 3 `drop:`
  cases pass. (The Per-task checks and the commit are Task 4's final steps.)

**Expected results:** 3 `drop:` cases in `rawreel.core_unit`, green under `vs2026` Debug (Release and
`vs2026-asan` at the Task 6 checkpoint).

**Notes:**
- Design D2 (one stream; `slot >= 1` → `Unsupported` in the Session), D21 (an open replaces the
  stream), §4.3 `drop.h`, §7 ("first file opens, log 'N files ignored'"), §8.1 (`routeDrop`: one
  file, several → ignored count).
- Note (plan choice): `DropPlan` gets a defaulted `operator==` for the assertions.
- Note (plan choice): the Windows test path is built as `std::string("C:") + "\\clips\\x.yuv"` so the
  source holds no literal machine-style path (privacy lint).
- Removed from v1 (do not write): `OpenAction`, `DropPlan::opens`/`closeOthers`, the `tile` and
  `stream_count` parameters, "at most 3 streams" (v1.x tiles, §13).

**Behaviour** (the view and drop rules are in their sections above):
- **PlayerLogic** (state: `position_` = last shown index or −1, optional `wanted_` = the index to show
  as soon as it is ready, `playing_`, `loop_`, `stalled_`, `direction_` ±1, optional `deadline_`, sets
  `ready_`, `outstanding_`, `failed_`, optional `show_`, the times of playback shows of the last
  second, counters). `period = fps.den / fps.num` seconds as `Clock::duration` (truncated to the
  clock tick). `bounded` = `length` set; `len` = its value; with `len == 0` every operation is a no-op.
  - `clampIndex(i)`: bounded → `clamp(i, 0, len − 1)`; else `max(i, 0)`.
  - `base()` = `wanted_` or `position_`. `next()` = `wanted_` if set, else `position_ + 1`; at
    `position_ + 1 == len`: loop → 0, else none.
  - `reset(c, p)`: config = c; clear `ready_`, `outstanding_`, `failed_`, `show_`, shows, `stalled_`,
    `deadline_`; `playing_ = false`; `position_ = −1`; `wanted_ = clampIndex(p)`; `direction_ = +1`.
    `loop_` is kept.
  - `play(now)`: nothing without a position or wanted index; when bounded, not looping, nothing
    wanted and `position_ == len − 1`, `wanted_ = 0` (restart from the beginning); `playing_ = true`,
    `direction_ = +1`, `stalled_ = false`, `deadline_ = now + period`.
  - `pause()`: `playing_ = false`; `deadline_` cleared; if `stalled_`: `stalled_ = false` and
    `wanted_` cleared (it came from playback).
  - `stop()`: `pause(); seek(0)`.
  - `step(d)`: `pause()`; `d == 0` → nothing; target `clampIndex(base() + d)`; `direction_ = d > 0 ?
    +1 : −1`; then as seek's last part.
  - `seek(i)`: `pause()`; `t = clampIndex(i)`; when `t != base()`, `direction_ = t > base() ? +1 :
    −1`; `failed_.erase(t)`; if `t == position_` and nothing is wanted: nothing; else `wanted_ = t` and,
    if `t` is ready, show it now (no playback time recorded).
  - `setLoop(b)`: `loop_ = b`. `setWindow(w)`: `config.window = max(w, 1)`.
  - `frameReady(i, now)`: erase `i` from `outstanding_`; if `!keeps(i)` return false; insert into
    `ready_`; if `wanted_ == i`: show `i` (a playback show when `playing_`: record `now`); if it was
    stalled: `stalled_ = false`, `deadline_ = now + period` (re-anchor, no catch-up). Return true.
  - `frameFailed(i)`: erase `i` from `outstanding_`; insert it into `failed_`; if `wanted_ == i` (a
    seek/step target, or the frame a stalled playback waits for): `pause()` and clear `wanted_` (the
    position stays the last shown frame); otherwise nothing more: playback continues up to `i − 1`.
    Failed indices are not requested again until a seek or step targets them or a reset.
  - `tick(now)`: returns unless `playing_`, `!stalled_` and `deadline_ <= now`. `n = next()`; none →
    `pause()` (end without loop; `position_` stays). `n` in `failed_` → `pause()` (position stays, no
    show, no stall counted). `n` ready → show `n` (playback, time `now`),
    then `deadline_ += period`, and if that is `<= now`, `deadline_ = now + period` (late tick:
    re-anchor). `n` not ready → `wanted_ = n`, `stalled_ = true`, `++stalls`.
  - show `i`: `position_ = i`, `wanted_` cleared, `show_ = i`, `++shown`; then `ready_` entries with
    `!keeps(e)` are erased (the caller mirrors this for its leases with `keeps()`).
  - `takeShow()`: returns `show_` and clears it (a later show overwrites an untaken one).
  - `takeRequests()`: `b = base()`; none or `b < 0` → empty. Candidates in order: `b` when it is
    `wanted_` (not shown yet), then `b + j·direction_` for `j = 1..window`; bounded and looping: taken
    modulo `len` (non-negative); bounded and not looping: stop at the first index outside `[0, len)`;
    unbounded: stop below 0. Skip an index that is ready, outstanding, failed, equal to `position_` or
    already taken. Take while `outstanding_.size() < inflight`; mark each outstanding.
  - `keepRange()`: `b = base()`; direction +1 → `{b − 2, b + window}`; −1 → `{b − window, b + 2}`.
    `keeps(i)`: `i == position_`, or `lo <= i <= hi`; bounded and looping: `i + k·len` in `[lo, hi]`
    for some integer `k` (always true when `hi − lo + 1 >= len`).
  - `nextDeadline()`: `deadline_` when playing and not stalled, else nullopt (a stalled player waits
    for `frameReady`, never for the timer).
  - `status(now)`: `position = position_`; `loop = loop_ && bounded`; `targetFps = num / den`;
    `actualFps` = playback shows with time in `(now − 1 s, now]`; the rest from the state.
- **Command table** (binding; `commandTable()` returns these in `Command` order):

| Command | id | menu | label | key | route |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Open | open | File | Open... | Ctrl+O | Accelerator |
| OpenUri | open_uri | File | Open URI... | Ctrl+U | Accelerator |
| CloseStreams | close_streams | File | Close stream | Ctrl+W | Accelerator |
| Quit | quit | File | Quit | Ctrl+Q | Accelerator |
| PlayPause | play_pause | Playback | Play/Pause | Space | CharHook |
| Stop | stop | Playback | Stop | – | Accelerator |
| StepBack | step_back | Playback | Step back | Left | CharHook |
| StepForward | step_forward | Playback | Step forward | Right | CharHook |
| StepBack10 | step_back_10 | Playback | Step back 10 | Shift+Left | CharHook |
| StepForward10 | step_forward_10 | Playback | Step forward 10 | Shift+Right | CharHook |
| First | first | Playback | First frame | Home | CharHook |
| Last | last | Playback | Last frame | End | CharHook |
| Loop | loop | Playback | Loop | L | CharHook |
| Format | format | View | Format... | – | Accelerator |
| Reopen | reopen | View | Reopen | – | Accelerator |
| GpuInfo | gpu_info | Help | GPU info | – | Accelerator |
| Diagnostics | diagnostics | Help | Diagnostics | – | Accelerator |

  Each entry has a one-sentence `help` (status-bar text, e.g. "Open a raw video file"; Reopen:
  "Rebuild the GPU stack and reopen the stream at its frame"). Menus in order: File, Playback, View,
  Help (Task 9 builds them). Removed from the first M3 design (v1.x, design v2 §4.3): AddStream,
  Export, ZoomFit, Zoom100, ZoomIn, ZoomOut, Fullscreen, Escape, ToolPan, ToolPipette, ToolSelect,
  ZoomToSelection, Grid, GridSize; the Tools menu goes with them.
- **normaliseKey(code, ctrl, shift, alt)** over wx 3.3's `wxKeyCode` values (wx/defs.h; written as
  named constants in `commands.cpp`, because `rawreel_core` has no wx; Task 10 adds `static_assert`s
  against `WXK_*`): `WXK_ESCAPE 27` → `Escape`; `WXK_SPACE 32` → `Space`; `WXK_END 312`, `WXK_HOME
  313`, `WXK_LEFT 314`, `WXK_UP 315`, `WXK_RIGHT 316`, `WXK_DOWN 317` → the arrow, Home and End keys;
  `WXK_F11 350` → `F11`; `'='` (61), `'+'` (43), `WXK_ADD 335`, `WXK_NUMPAD_ADD 388` → `Plus` with
  `shift = false`; `'-'` (45), `WXK_SUBTRACT 337`, `WXK_NUMPAD_SUBTRACT 390` → `Minus`;
  `WXK_NUMPAD0..9` (324..333) → `'0'..'9'`; `'a'..'z'` → upper case; anything else unchanged.
  `ctrl`, `alt` and (except for Plus/Minus) `shift` are passed through.
- **commandForKey(c, text_focus)**: `text_focus` → nullopt; else the CharHook entry whose key equals
  `c` exactly (modifiers included), or nullopt.
- **keyText**: `Ctrl+`, `Shift+`, `Alt+` in that order, then `Space`, `+`, `-`, `Left`, `Right`,
  `Up`, `Down`, `Home`, `End`, `Esc`, `F11`, or the character.
- **FormatChoice**:
  - `choiceFromCandidate(c)`: `format` = `descriptor(*c.guess.format).name` when set, else
    `"yuv420p"`; size from the guess when both are non-zero, else 1920×1080; `fps` = the guess's or
    25/1; colour `{}`.
  - `choiceFromInfo(i)`: `fmt` of `parseUri(i.resolvedUri)` when it starts with `@` (a custom JSON
    format), else `descriptor(i.desc.format).name`; size and fps from `i`; `colour =
    colourParams(i.desc.colour)`.
  - `applyChoice` as in the header; colour names: matrix `bt601 bt709 bt2020 rgb custom`, range
    `limited full`, transfer `srgb bt1886 linear pq hlg`, primaries `bt601-525 bt601-625 bt709
    bt2020`; `kr`/`kb` as `std::format("{}", value)`; fps `"N"` when `den == 1`, else `"N/D"`.
  - `validateChoice` as in the header (`geometry` of the catalogue format at the size; `"@path"` only
    checks the size and fps, the JSON is loaded when the URI is opened).

**Tests first** (`TEST_SUITE("core")`; `t0 = Clock::time_point{} + std::chrono::hours(1)`, `P =
40 ms` for 25 fps; `cfg = {.length = 100, .fps = {25, 1}, .window = 8, .inflight = 4}`; a test
helper `Feeder { std::set<std::int64_t> pending; }` with `take(p)` = `p.takeRequests()` recorded in
`pending` (every `takeRequests() == …` check below goes through it) and `fill(p, now, except = {})`
= repeat { `take(p)`; deliver `p.frameReady(i, now)` for every pending `i` not in `except` and
remove it } until a round delivers nothing; "`fill`" below means `feeder.fill(p, <current time>)`):

`tests/test_player.cpp` (the ten behaviour cases of design §4.3 plus case 11 (P3), one TEST_CASE each):
1. `play shows the next frames at 1/fps`: `reset(cfg, 0)`; `takeRequests() == {0,1,2,3}`; `fill`;
   `takeShow() == 0`; `play(t0)`; `nextDeadline() == t0 + P`; for k = 1..30: `fill(p, t0 + k·P −
   1 ms)`, `tick(t0 + k·P)`, `takeShow() == k`; then `status(t0 + 30P)`: `shown == 31`, `stalls == 0`,
   `actualFps == 25`, `playing`.
2. `a missing frame stalls without skipping and re-anchors`: as 1 but `fill(…, except {5})`; k = 1..4
   show 1..4; `tick(t0 + 5P)` → no show, `stalled`, `stalls == 1`, `nextDeadline() == nullopt`;
   `tick(t0 + 6P)` → nothing, `stalls == 1`; `frameReady(5, t0 + 6P + 10 ms)` returns true and
   `takeShow() == 5`, not stalled, `nextDeadline() == t0 + 6P + 10 ms + P`; `tick` 1 ms before that →
   nothing; at it → 6.
3. `the end pauses or loops`: length 10, `reset(cfg, 8)`, `fill`, `takeShow() == 8`; `play(t0)`;
   `tick(t0+P)` → 9; `tick(t0+2P)` → nothing, `!playing`, `position == 9`. Then (no loop, at 9)
   `play(t1)` restarts: `fill(p, t1)` → `takeShow() == 0`. With `setLoop(true)` and a fresh
   `reset(cfg, 8)`: requests `{8, 9, 0, 1}`; after `fill` and `play`, ticks show 9, then 0, then 1.
4. `step and seek pause and show the target when ready`: `reset(cfg, 3)`, `fill`, `play(t0)`,
   `fill`, `tick(t0+P)` → 4; `seek(20)` → `!playing`, no show, `position == 4`,
   `takeRequests() == {20, 21, 22, 23}`; `frameReady(20, …)` → `takeShow() == 20`; `fill`;
   `step(−1)` → `takeRequests() == {19, 18, 17, 16}` (backward); `frameReady(19)` → show 19;
   `step(+1)` → show 20 at once (still kept and ready). `reset(cfg, 50); seek(1000)` →
   `takeRequests() == {99}`; `reset(cfg, 50); seek(−5)` → `takeRequests() == {0}`.
5. `requests respect the cap, the window and the direction`: `reset(cfg, 50)`: `{50,51,52,53}`, then
   `{}`; `frameReady(50)` → `{54}`; `frameReady(51..54)` → `{55,56,57,58}`; no index is ever returned
   twice while outstanding or ready; `reset(cfg, 2)`, `fill`, `step(−1)` → `{1, 0}`;
   `reset(cfg, 97)` → `{97, 98, 99}`.
6. `a failed frame pauses at the last shown frame` (every `fill` skips 12): `reset(cfg, 10)`, `fill`,
   `play(t0)`, `fill`, `tick(t0+P)` → 11; `tick(t0+2P)` → stalled; `frameFailed(12)` → `!playing`,
   `!stalled`, `position == 11`, no show; `takeRequests()` never contains 12; after `seek(12)` it does.
7. `an unbounded stream has no end and ignores loop`: `{.length = nullopt}`; `setLoop(true)` →
   `status().loop == false`; `reset(cfg, 0)`; `seek(1'000'000)` → requests start 1'000'000,
   1'000'001; after `fill` and `play`, ticks show 1'000'001, 1'000'002.
8. `actualFps counts the last second`: as case 1 for 50 frames → `status(t0 + 50P).actualFps == 25`;
   `pause()`; `status(t0 + 50P + 500 ms).actualFps == 13`.
9. `reset drops readiness and outstanding marks`: `reset(cfg, 0)`, `takeRequests() == {0,1,2,3}`,
   `frameReady(0)`; `reset(cfg, 0)` → `takeShow() == nullopt`, `takeRequests() == {0,1,2,3}`.
10. `a looping short clip never stalls`: length 4, window 8, inflight 4, `setLoop(true)`,
    `reset(cfg, 0)`, `fill`, `play(t0)`; for k = 1..20: `tick(t0 + k·P)`, `takeShow() == k % 4`,
    `takeRequests().empty()`; `keeps(i)` for i in 0..3 throughout; `stalls == 0`.
11. `a failure ahead of the position pauses when it is due`: `reset(cfg, 4)`, `fill(…, except {12})`,
    `takeShow() == 4`, `play(t0)`; `frameFailed(12)` → still playing; for k = 1..7: `fill(…, except
    {12})`, `tick(t0 + k·P)` → `takeShow() == 4 + k`; `tick(t0 + 8P)` → no show, `!playing`,
    `!stalled`, `position == 11`, `stalls == 0`.

`tests/test_commands.cpp`:

`tests/test_format_choice.cpp`:
1. `choiceFromCandidate`: guess `{yuv420p, 33, 17, nullopt}` → `{"yuv420p", 33, 17, {25,1}, {}}`;
   an empty guess → the defaults `{"yuv420p", 1920, 1080, {25,1}, {}}`; guess with fps 50/1 → fps kept.

   resolvedUri "file:clip.yuv?fmt=v210&size=34x17&fps=50&range=limited&matrix=bt709"}` →

3. `applyChoice`: `("file:clip.yuv?impl=raw&w=3&h=4&range=full", {"v210", 34, 17, {50,1}, {Bt709,
   Limited}})` → `parseUri(result)` has path `clip.yuv` and params exactly `{impl=raw, fmt=v210,
   size=34x17, fps=50, matrix=bt709, range=limited}` in that order; a Custom colour adds `matrix=custom,

   input `"clip.yuv"` (bare path) gives scheme `file` and the same path; `"bad:%zz"` → `Format`.
4. `validateChoice`: `{"v210", 33, 17}` → `Format` containing "multiple of 2"; `{"nope"}` → `Format`;
   fps `{0,1}` → `Format`; `{"@"}` → `Format`; `{"@x.json", 0, 5}` → `Format`; Custom kr 0.6 kb 0.5 →
   `Format`; `{"yuv420p", 33, 17, {25,1}}` ok.

`tests/test_session_types.cpp`: `FrameSet{}` has `index −1`, `count 0`, `epoch 0` and empty views;

The wx-free core of the one-stream player: ViewTransform::fit (fit to window with whole-pixel
offsets); PlayerLogic (fps clock, stall instead of skip, step, seek, loop, prefetch window with an
in-flight cap, modulo when looping); the command table with key normaliser and menu labels; the
format-dialog model with applyChoice; one-file drop routing; and the Session value types.

player, 5 command, 4 format-choice, 3 drop and 1 session-types cases (32), green under `vs2026`
Debug; `FORMAT-OK`; privacy lint clean. (Release, `vs2026-asan` and clang-tidy: Task 6 checkpoint.)

**Commit:** `feat: add rawreel_core with the view, player, command and format-choice logic`.

**Notes:**
- Design v2 D2 (`MAX_STREAMS = 1`), D11, D17 (reduced table), D18 (menus), D21, D23 (fit only),
  §4.3, §8.1 core-unit row, §8.4 layer 1. Cut by U-SCOPE (§13): `RecoveryPolicy` (D9), zoom, pan,
  tiles, region zoom, grid snapping, overlays, the removed commands, multi-file/tile drop routing,
  the probe and export value types.
- Note (plan choice): `PlayerLogic` gains `setWindow` (the Session halves W on OutOfMemory, §5.2) and
  `keeps(index)` (keepRange "taken modulo length" cannot be expressed as one pair); `frameReady`
  returns whether the index was kept, so the Session releases leases the player does not keep and the
  in-flight count never sticks.
- Note (plan choice): `keepRange` follows the direction (`[b − 2, b + W]` forward, `[b − W, b + 2]`
  backward); the design's pair is the forward case.
- Note (plan choice): `PlayerStatus::position` is the last shown index; a pending seek target shows
  once it is ready. `play()` at the last frame without loop restarts from 0.
- Note (plan choice): failed indices are not requested again until a seek or step targets them or a
  reset; otherwise a read error would be retried and reported on every pump.
- `steadyClock()` and `makeThreadTimer()` come with `timer.h` in Task 5.
- Note (plan choice): the command ids, labels, help texts and menu assignment are this plan's; the
  design fixes only the commands, keys and routes. `Stop`, `Format`, `Reopen`, `GpuInfo` and
  `Diagnostics` have no key. `Reopen` sits in the View menu after `Format...` (menus are built in
  table order, so a File entry would follow `Quit`); its label equals the infobar button's.
  `CloseStreams` keeps its name but reads "Close stream" (one stream).
- `rawreel_core` duplicates the small colour-name table of dvv-media's URI parser, which is private.

# M3 plan, part 2: Tasks 5-12

Conventions for every task below (as M2, narrowed to Windows by U-WIN; see Global Constraints):
- Run commands from the workspace root, the folder that contains `rawreel-workspace`.
- Use the PowerShell tool on Windows. WSL is used only for clang-format and the checkpoint build,
  as `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && <command>'`, also from the
  workspace root. No test runs in WSL. Run git from PowerShell only, except the read-only
  `git ls-files` and `git diff --name-only` of the format and tidy commands.
- Every commit ends with the trailer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
  Nothing is pushed. All work is on branch `m3` (Task 0).
- A tool call stops after 10 minutes. Long Windows runs use `run_in_background`; the checkpoint's
  WSL build may run in parallel with the Windows presets.
- `<GPU env>` = `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'`
  (Windows, NVIDIA).
- Inner loop (Windows, foreground): `<GPU env>; Push-Location rawreel; cmake --build --preset vs2026;
  ctest --preset vs2026 -E "\.package$" --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`.
- Privacy lint after every commit: `cmake -DREPO=<repo> -DMODE=commits
  "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake`.
  Expected: `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.
- **Per-task checks** (once, on the task's final commit; Global Constraints has the commands):
  - Windows: `<GPU env>; Push-Location <repo>; cmake --preset vs2026 | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`
  - clang-format on the changed files (`<base>` = the repository's head before the task):
    `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd <repo> && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <base> -- "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r clang-format --dry-run --Werror && echo FORMAT-OK'`
  - the privacy lint. No Release, no ASan, no clang-tidy, no WSL build (U-PROC).
- **Debug race check** (risky tasks only; in place of the earlier TSan ×N runs, U-WIN, U-PROC): the

  after the checkpoint's ASan workflow; no AddressSanitizer report. The CTest entries carry the ASan

**Seams with part 1 (Tasks 0-4).** This part uses these names exactly as the design (§4) gives

### Task 5: Session: stream, player, sink, events

**Goal:** Build the Session actor of design D10/§5.2 in `rawreel_core` for one stream: the timer
thread (`ThreadTimer`), serialised opens with the D21 rules (slot 0 only; an open replaces the
stream and takes an optional start position), the playback pump with prefetch, read-error and
out-of-memory handling, the `status()` snapshot and `shutdown()`. Device loss, the GPU stack and
`reopen()` come in Task 6. The headless integration suite (design v2 §8.2 cases 1-6, 8, 11, 12,
13) passes in `vs2026` Debug and ten times in a row in Debug (U-PROC); `vs2026-asan` and the ten
runs in a row under `vs2026-asan` (the Windows race check that replaces TSan under U-WIN) run at the
Task 6 checkpoint. The Linux branch of `ThreadTimer` is written as portable code and only compiled
(by the Task 6 checkpoint build, which is the Linux sanity build).

**Files** (rawreel):
- Create `src/core/timer.h`, `src/core/timer.cpp`: `steadyClock()`, `makeThreadTimer()`

    `whenAll`, `syncWait`, `parseUri`/`formatUri`, `DVV_LOG_*`, `LifetimeRegistry`.
- **PlayerLogic contract this task relies on** (design §4.3 cases 5, 6, 9; Step 2 checks Task 4's
  code against it):
  - (P1) `reset(config, p)` drops readiness and outstanding marks; `p` (clamped into
    `[0, length)`) becomes the index to show: `takeShow()` returns `p` once `frameReady(p, now)`
    has arrived, and `takeRequests()` starts with `p` when it is not ready.
  - (P2) `frameReady(i, now)` and `frameFailed(i)` clear `i`'s outstanding mark for every `i`;
    `frameReady` returns false and records nothing else when `!keeps(i)`; a repeated
    `frameReady(i)` changes nothing.
  - (P3) as Task 4's `frameFailed`/`tick` Behaviour.
  - (P4) the Session tests membership only with `player.keeps(i)`, never with `keepRange()` itself.
