```cpp
#pragma once

// The media clock's time sources (design M3 D11, §4.3): the steady clock and ThreadTimer, a timer
// with one pending deadline on its own std::jthread. Tests use fakes (tests/session_fixture.h).

#include <memory>

#include "core/session_types.h"
#include "dvv/base/error.h"

namespace rawreel {

[[nodiscard]] std::shared_ptr<IClock> steadyClock();
// ThreadTimer (ITimer): schedule() replaces the pending deadline; the callback runs once on the
// timer's own thread when the deadline has passed and must only post work (it must not throw).
// cancel() blocks until a running callback has returned and throws std::logic_error when called
// from inside a callback; the destructor cancels. Windows waits on a high-resolution waitable timer,
// Linux on a condition variable. Errc::Internal when the Windows timer or event cannot be created
// or the thread cannot start.
[[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<std::shared_ptr<ITimer>> makeThreadTimer();

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Produces (`src/core/session.h`, complete for this task; Task 6 adds the members marked there):

```cpp
#pragma once

// The Session (design M3 D10, §4.3, §5): a wx-free actor that owns the streams, the media stack,
// the player and the jobs. Every command is thread-safe and non-blocking: it posts to the Session's
// SerialExecutor and returns; commands run in call order. Frames go to an IDisplaySink, discrete
// events to ISessionEvents (both called on the Session executor), continuous state is polled with
// status().

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "core/session_types.h"
#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/executor.h"
#include "dvv/base/task.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/device_id.h"
#include "dvv/media/frame_store.h"
#include "dvv/media/source_factory.h"
#include "dvv/registry/component_registry.h"

namespace dvv::gpu {
class ExecutionContext;
class GpuRegistry;
}  // namespace dvv::gpu

namespace rawreel {

namespace detail {
class SessionCore;
}  // namespace detail

struct MediaStack {  // what device loss replaces (design D7)
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context;  // null: host-only (tests)
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::media::FrameStore> store;
    // The display view of a stream (GPU: displayView(deviceDomain), host fixture: planesView(desc,
    // HostPlanes, host)); evaluated once per stream at attach.
    std::function<dvv::media::ViewSpec(const dvv::media::FrameDesc&)> viewFor;
};
using StackFactory =
    std::move_only_function<dvv::Result<MediaStack>(std::optional<dvv::gpu::DeviceId>)>;

struct SessionOptions {
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::ComponentRegistry> components;  // source providers
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::IExecutor> cpu, io;
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry> gpus;         // null: host-only
    std::optional<dvv::gpu::DeviceId> device;            // null: the best device
    bool presentation = false;                           // contexts with presentation (GUI)
    std::uint32_t inflight = 4;                          // PlayerConfig::inflight (K, D11)
    std::shared_ptr<IClock> clock;
    std::shared_ptr<ITimer> timer;
    std::shared_ptr<IDisplaySink> sink;
    std::shared_ptr<ISessionEvents> events;
    // Empty: the default (host-only store without gpus; Task 6 adds the GPU stack). The first
    // stack is made in create() on the caller's thread; later ones on `io` (Task 6).
    StackFactory makeStack{};
};

// The stage with the most converter nanoseconds between two snapshots (design D20): "Produce" ->
// "disk", "HostToStaging" -> "copy", "Upload" -> "upload", "GpuUnpack" -> "unpack", "ToDisplay" ->
// "display"; "" when none of them grew.
[[nodiscard]] std::string bottleneckStage(const dvv::media::StoreStats& before,
                                          const dvv::media::StoreStats& after);
// Help -> Diagnostics text (design X13): one line of store counters, one per tier, one per
// converter, one per outstanding lease.
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> diagnosticsLines(
    const dvv::media::StoreStats& stats, std::span<const dvv::media::LeaseInfo> leases);

class Session {
public:
    // Errc::Internal when components, cpu, io, clock, timer, sink or events is missing or
    // inflight is 0; errors of the SerialExecutor, the SourceFactory and the first makeStack pass
    // through (the GUI turns them into the M1b startup failure, exit 4).
    [[nodiscard]] static dvv::Result<std::unique_ptr<Session>> create(SessionOptions options);
    Session(const Session&) = delete;
    Session& operator=(const Session&) = delete;
    Session(Session&&) = delete;
    Session& operator=(Session&&) = delete;
    // Expects a completed shutdown(); otherwise it logs a warning and runs it (syncWait). Never
    // destroy a Session on a thread of `cpu` or `io`.
    ~Session();

    // Commands: thread-safe, non-blocking, executed in call order on the Session executor;
    // refused once shutdown() has started.
    // Slot 0 only (D2, D21; slot >= 1 -> error Unsupported "one stream in v1.0"); replaces the
    // stream. `start`: the index to show first (clamped into [0, length − 1]); none -> frame 0
    // (design v2 §4.3 "Start position").
    void open(std::uint32_t slot, dvv::media::SourceRequest request,
              std::optional<std::int64_t> start = {});
    void closeStreams();
    void play();
    void pause();
    void togglePlay();
    void stop();
    void setLoop(bool loop);
    void step(std::int64_t delta);
    void seek(std::int64_t index);
    void logDiagnostics();  // Help -> Diagnostics: diagnosticsLines() to the log (Info)
    // Task 6: reopen, reportDeviceLost.

    [[nodiscard]] SessionStatus status() const;  // snapshot; any thread
    // Stops the timer, refuses new commands, waits for every running task (requests, opens, and
    // later stack builds), clears the sink, detaches the stream and releases the
    // stack on the executor. Await it once; a second call returns at once.
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::Task<void> shutdown();

private:
    explicit Session(std::shared_ptr<detail::SessionCore> core) noexcept;
    template <class F>
    void command(F f);

    std::shared_ptr<detail::SessionCore> core_;
};

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- CMake: `rawreel_core` gains the two sources (it already links `dvv::media` PUBLIC, which brings
  `dvv::gpu` and `dvv::registry`). CTest `rawreel.core_session` = `rawreel_core_tests
  --test-suite=session`, `TIMEOUT 600`, `ENVIRONMENT DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail`, the
  `ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION "${DVV_TEST_ENVIRONMENT}"` of the other entries.
- Produces for later tasks: `detail::SessionCore` with the members of the Code below (`opens_`,
  `open_running_`, `stack_`, `stack_epoch_`, `streams_`, `stream_set_`, `generation_`, `player_`,
  `ready_`, `oom_retried_`, `oom_window_`, `stopping_`; `startNextOpen`, `openDone`,
  `attachSource(slot, source, start)`, `requestDone`, `requestFailed`, `installStack`, `pump`,
  `publishStatus`, `spawn`, `taskFinished`, `shutdownOnExecutor`; the emitters `emitError`,
  `emitStreamsChanged`, `emitFormatNeeded`, `emitStackChanged`); Task 6 extends it in
  `session.cpp`. The test support files (`session_fixture.*`, `test_source_provider.*`) are reused
  by Task 6.

**Behaviour:**
- **Threads (design §5.1).** All `SessionCore` state is owned by the Session executor (a
  `SerialExecutor` over `cpu`); nothing in it is locked except the status snapshot
  (`status_mutex_`, written at the end of each `pump()`) and `stopped_` (atomic, read by
  `~Session`). Commands post a lambda holding `shared_ptr<SessionCore>`. Every coroutine that
  awaits something that does not resume on the Session executor (`SourceFactory::open`, the I/O
  hop) continues with `co_await resumeOn(executor)` before it touches state. `FrameStore::acquire`
  is called with `resume_on = executor` (M2 D12), so `whenAll` of acquisitions resumes on the
  executor. Events and sink calls happen only on the executor; no event is emitted once
  `stopping_` is set.
- **create:** validate (Internal naming the missing member); `makeSerialExecutor(cpu)`;
  `SourceFactory::create(*components, io)`; when `makeStack` is empty: `gpus == nullptr` → the
  host-only factory (below), `gpus != nullptr` → Internal "no default GPU stack factory yet" (Task
  6 replaces this branch with `gpuStackFactory`); run `makeStack(device)` on the calling thread
  (plan choice, Notes);
  a stack without `store` or `viewFor` → Internal; post `installStack` (epoch 1, `stackChanged`).
- **Host-only factory:** `FrameStore::create({.cpu, .io})`; `viewFor = [host](desc) { return
  planesView(desc, Api::HostPlanes, host); }` with `host = store->hostDomain()`; `context` null.
- **open(slot, request, start)** (§5.2 step 1): push `OpenJob{slot, request, start}` onto the FIFO;
  `startNextOpen()` starts the next job only when none runs: `slot >= MAX_STREAMS` (any slot ≥ 1) →
  `error(Unsupported "one stream in v1.0")`; else spawn `openSource` with the current `streamSet`.
  On completion (executor): `streamSet` changed → drop silently (the source dies here); an error
  for a `file:` URI without `fmt` whose code is `Unsupported` or `Format`, or an `Ambiguous` →
  `formatNeeded(slot, request, candidates or {}, reason)` (reason: the error message, or "the format
  of '<uri>' is ambiguous"); other errors → `error(...)` unchanged; a source →
  `attachSource(job.slot, source, job.start)`. Then the next job starts.
- **attachSource(slot, source, start)** (D21; in this task a stack always exists, Task 6 adds the
  no-stack rule): any size, format or colour replaces the stream (there are no size rules between
  slots in v1.0). `store->attach(source)` (an error → `error`, nothing changes), then
  `Stream{source, info copy, resolveColour(desc), origin, viewFor(desc)}` replaces slot 0 (the old
  origin is detached). Then `streamsReplaced(start)`: `++streamSet`, ready map cleared, OOM state
  cleared, `resetPlayer(start ? *start : 0, keep_playing = true)`, `sink.clear()`,
  `streamsChanged(streams)`.
- **closeStreams:** empty the FIFO, `++streamSet`, detach the origin, drop the stream, ready map,
  player; `sink.clear()`; `streamsChanged({})`.
- **resetPlayer(position, keep_playing)** (design v2 §4.3 "Start position"): `config =
  makeConfig()`; `player.reset(config, clamp(position, 0, length − 1))` (only `max(position, 0)`
  when the stream is unbounded); `player.setLoop(loop_)`; `play(now)` when `keep_playing` and the
  replaced player was playing (a first stream, or a stream after `closeStreams()`, starts paused).
  Callers: `streamsReplaced(start)` with `start` or 0; the out-of-memory retry with the current
  position (`player.status(now).position`, 0 when −1).
- **makeConfig (D11):** `fps` of the stream (slot 0); `length` = its `frameCount` (nullopt when
  unbounded); `inflight = options.inflight`; `window = clamp(budget / (2 · 8 · w · h · streams),
  2, 8)` with `streams = 1`, in 64-bit integers, where `budget` is the `TierStats::budget` of the
  store tier whose `domain == streams[0].view.domain` (the host tier when none matches), capped by
  the OOM window when set.
- **Commands** `play`, `pause`, `togglePlay` (by `status(now).playing`), `stop`, `setLoop` (also
  stored in `loop_`), `step`, `seek`: forwarded to the player when one exists; ignored otherwise.
- **pump()** (§5.2 steps 2-4, after every command, task completion and tick): when not stopping
  and a player and a stack exist: every index of `takeRequests()` → `startRequest(i)` (one detached
  task: `whenAll` of `store->acquire({origin_s, i}, view_s, executor)` for every stream, tagged
  `{streamSet, epoch, generation}`); `takeShow()` → `sink.show(FrameSet{i, n, leases, epoch})` from
  the ready map; ready-map entries are released when `!player.keeps(i)`; the bottleneck is updated (once per second); `timer.schedule(nextDeadline())` when the
  deadline changed (`timer.cancel()` when there is none). Then always `publishStatus()`.
- **Request results** (executor): stale tag or stopping → dropped (the leases die here). The first
  error in slot order: `OutOfMemory` the first time for this index → OOM window
  `max(2, window / 2)`, `++generation` (results of the old window become stale),
  `resetPlayer(<current position>, true)`,
  then every ready-map entry with `player.keeps(i)` is announced again with `frameReady`
  (Note); a second `OutOfMemory` for the index and every other error → `player.frameFailed(i)` and
  `error(cause.wrap(cause.code, "frame <i> of '<resolvedUri of that stream>'"))`. `DeviceLost` is an
  ordinary error in this task (Task 6: the device-lost sequence). All ok → `player.frameReady(i, now)`; the leases
  are stored (as `shared_ptr<const ReadLease>`) only when `frameReady` returned true and the index is
  not yet in the map.
- **status():** `SessionStatus` under `status_mutex_`: `player` (`PlayerStatus{}` without a player,
  position −1), `streams`, `epoch` (the current `stackEpoch`), `gpuAvailable` (a stack is
  installed: `stack_.store != nullptr`; the host-only stack counts), `bottleneck`, `inflight`
  (request tasks running), `readyAhead` (ready entries at
  distance 1..window ahead of the position, modulo the length when looping).
- **Bottleneck (D20):** once per second of the Session clock: the store's `stats()` is compared
  with the previous snapshot through `bottleneckStage`; `status.bottleneck` is that name while the
  player is playing and `actualFps < 0.95 · targetFps`, else empty.
- **logDiagnostics:** `diagnosticsLines(store->stats(), store->outstandingLeases())`, each line
  through `DVV_LOG_INFO`; "store: none" without a stack. Lines: `store: frames F, leases L, pins P,
  hits H, misses M, creations C, evictions E, failures X`; per tier `tier <Host|HostVisible|
  DeviceLocal>: <bytes> / <budget> bytes, <views> views`; per converter `converter <name>: <runs>
  runs, <bytes> bytes, <ms> ms` (ms with one decimal); per lease `lease <origin>:<index> <view
  format name> <Read|Pin> at <file>:<line>`.
- **shutdown()** (§5.5 Session part): see Code. Afterwards `stopped()` is true and
  `LifetimeRegistry::liveCount()` no longer counts anything the Session made.

**Code** (binding: the threading handshake of design §5.1-§5.2).

`src/core/timer.cpp` (complete):

```cpp
#include "core/timer.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <cstdint>
#include <exception>
#include <format>
#include <functional>
#include <mutex>
#include <optional>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <stop_token>
#include <thread>
#include <utility>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <windows.h>
#endif

#include "dvv/base/log.h"

namespace rawreel {
namespace {

class SteadyClock final : public IClock {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] Clock::time_point now() const override { return Clock::now(); }
};

// What both platform variants share: the pending deadline and callback, guarded by mutex_.
class ThreadTimerBase : public ITimer {
public:
    void schedule(Clock::time_point when, std::move_only_function<void()> callback) override {
        std::move_only_function<void()> replaced;
        {
            std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
            replaced = std::exchange(callback_, std::move(callback));
            deadline_ = when;
            ++generation_;
        }
        wake();
    }  // `replaced` is destroyed here, outside the lock

    void cancel() override {
        if (std::this_thread::get_id() == threadId()) {
            throw std::logic_error("ITimer::cancel() called from inside a timer callback");
        }
        std::move_only_function<void()> dropped;
        {
            std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);
            dropped = std::exchange(callback_, {});
            deadline_.reset();
            ++generation_;
            // Binding (design §4.3): a running callback has returned before cancel() does.
            idle_.wait(lock, [this] { return !running_; });
        }
        wake();
    }

protected:
    virtual void wake() noexcept = 0;  // makes the thread read the deadline again
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::thread::id threadId() const noexcept = 0;

    // Called by the timer thread with `lock` held; returns with it held. Runs the callback outside
    // the lock when the deadline has passed. True when the deadline was due.
    bool fireIfDue(std::unique_lock<std::mutex>& lock) {
        if (!deadline_ || Clock::now() < *deadline_) {
            return false;
        }
        std::move_only_function<void()> callback = std::exchange(callback_, {});
        deadline_.reset();
        running_ = true;
        lock.unlock();
        if (callback) {
            callback();
        }
        callback = {};  // destroyed before running_ is cleared
        lock.lock();
        running_ = false;
        idle_.notify_all();
        return true;
    }

    std::mutex mutex_;
    std::condition_variable idle_;
    std::optional<Clock::time_point> deadline_;
    std::move_only_function<void()> callback_;
    std::uint64_t generation_ = 0;
    bool running_ = false;
    bool stop_ = false;  // Windows only
};

#if defined(_WIN32)

// A CREATE_WAITABLE_TIMER_HIGH_RESOLUTION timer (no process-wide timeBeginPeriod, design D11) plus
// an auto-reset event that schedule(), cancel() and the destructor set.
class WaitableThreadTimer final : public ThreadTimerBase {
    struct Private {
        explicit Private() = default;
    };

public:
    static dvv::Result<std::shared_ptr<ITimer>> create() {
        HANDLE timer = CreateWaitableTimerExW(nullptr, nullptr, CREATE_WAITABLE_TIMER_HIGH_RESOLUTION,
                                              TIMER_ALL_ACCESS);
        if (timer == nullptr) {
            return dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Internal,
                                  std::format("CreateWaitableTimerExW failed (error {})", GetLastError()));
        }
        HANDLE event = CreateEventW(nullptr, FALSE, FALSE, nullptr);
        if (event == nullptr) {
            const DWORD code = GetLastError();
            CloseHandle(timer);
            return dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Internal,
                                  std::format("CreateEventW failed (error {})", code));
        }
        try {
            return std::shared_ptr<ITimer>(std::make_shared<WaitableThreadTimer>(Private{}, timer, event));
        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
            // The constructor did not finish (the thread did not start), so no destructor runs.
            CloseHandle(event);
            CloseHandle(timer);
            return dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Internal,
                                  std::format("the timer thread did not start: {}", error.what()));
        }
    }

    WaitableThreadTimer(Private /*key*/, HANDLE timer, HANDLE event)
        : timer_(timer), event_(event), thread_([this] { run(); }) {}
    WaitableThreadTimer(const WaitableThreadTimer&) = delete;
    WaitableThreadTimer& operator=(const WaitableThreadTimer&) = delete;
    ~WaitableThreadTimer() override {
        if (thread_.joinable()) {
            cancel();
            {
                std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
                stop_ = true;
            }
            wake();
            thread_.join();
        }
        CloseHandle(event_);
        CloseHandle(timer_);
    }

private:
    void wake() noexcept override { SetEvent(event_); }
    [[nodiscard]] std::thread::id threadId() const noexcept override { return thread_.get_id(); }

    void run() {
        std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);
        while (!stop_) {
            if (fireIfDue(lock)) {
                continue;
            }
            DWORD timeout = INFINITE;
            bool armed = false;
            if (deadline_) {
                using Ticks = std::chrono::duration<std::int64_t, std::ratio<1, 10'000'000>>;
                const std::int64_t ticks = std::max<std::int64_t>(
                    std::chrono::ceil<Ticks>(*deadline_ - Clock::now()).count(), 1);
                LARGE_INTEGER due{};
                due.QuadPart = -ticks;  // relative, in 100 ns units
                armed = SetWaitableTimerEx(timer_, &due, 0, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, 0) != FALSE;
                if (!armed) {  // never seen; keeps the timer working at millisecond precision
                    timeout = static_cast<DWORD>(std::min<std::int64_t>(ticks / 10'000 + 1, 0x7FFFFFFF));
                }
            }
            lock.unlock();
            const std::array<HANDLE, 2> handles{event_, timer_};
            // A schedule()/cancel() between unlock and wait has set the event, so nothing is lost.
            WaitForMultipleObjects(armed ? 2U : 1U, handles.data(), FALSE, timeout);
            lock.lock();
        }
    }

    HANDLE timer_;
    HANDLE event_;
    std::thread thread_;  // last: starts after the handles exist
};

#else

class CvThreadTimer final : public ThreadTimerBase {
public:
    CvThreadTimer() : thread_([this](const std::stop_token& stop) { run(stop); }) {}
    CvThreadTimer(const CvThreadTimer&) = delete;
    CvThreadTimer& operator=(const CvThreadTimer&) = delete;
    ~CvThreadTimer() override {
        cancel();
        thread_.request_stop();  // wakes the stop_token waits; the jthread joins below
    }

private:
    void wake() noexcept override { changed_.notify_all(); }
    [[nodiscard]] std::thread::id threadId() const noexcept override { return thread_.get_id(); }

    void run(const std::stop_token& stop) {
        std::unique_lock lock(mutex_);
        while (!stop.stop_requested()) {
            if (fireIfDue(lock)) {
                continue;
            }
            const std::uint64_t seen = generation_;
            const auto changed = [&] { return generation_ != seen; };
            if (deadline_) {
                const Clock::time_point due = *deadline_;
                changed_.wait_until(lock, stop, due, changed);
            } else {
                changed_.wait(lock, stop, changed);
            }
        }
    }

    std::condition_variable_any changed_;
    std::jthread thread_;  // last: destroyed (joined) first
};

#endif

}  // namespace

std::shared_ptr<IClock> steadyClock() {
    return std::make_shared<SteadyClock>();
}

dvv::Result<std::shared_ptr<ITimer>> makeThreadTimer() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
    return WaitableThreadTimer::create();
#else
    try {
        return std::shared_ptr<ITimer>(std::make_shared<CvThreadTimer>());
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        return dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Internal,
                              std::format("the timer thread did not start: {}", error.what()));
    }
#endif
}

}  // namespace rawreel
```

`src/core/session.cpp`, the threading core (binding; the bodies marked "described" follow
Behaviour; Task 6 adds to the places marked `Task 6:`):

```cpp
#include "core/session.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <coroutine>
#include <cstdint>
#include <deque>
#include <exception>
#include <format>
#include <map>
#include <mutex>
#include <optional>
#include <set>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <variant>
#include <vector>

#include "core/player.h"
#include "dvv/base/executors.h"
#include "dvv/base/log.h"
#include "dvv/base/uri.h"
#include "dvv/base/when_all.h"
#include "dvv/media/display.h"
#include "dvv/media/pixel_format.h"

namespace rawreel {
namespace detail {

struct Stream {
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::media::ISource> source;  // released on a device loss (design v2 D7)
    dvv::media::SourceInfo info;
    dvv::media::ResolvedColour colour;
    dvv::media::OriginId origin = 0;  // in the current stack's store
    dvv::media::ViewSpec view{};      // stack.viewFor(info.desc)
};

struct OpenJob {  // Task 6 adds the rebuild flag (design v2 §5.3 step 3)
    std::uint32_t slot = 0;
    dvv::media::SourceRequest request;
    std::optional<std::int64_t> start;  // design v2 §4.3 "Start position"
};

// The requests of one stream set on one stack (design §5.2); `generation` changes when the
// out-of-memory rule restarts the window.
struct Tag {
    std::uint64_t streamSet = 0;
    std::uint64_t epoch = 0;
    std::uint64_t generation = 0;
    bool operator==(const Tag&) const = default;
};

struct RequestSlot {
    dvv::media::OriginId origin = 0;
    dvv::media::ViewSpec view{};
};

using Leases = std::array<std::shared_ptr<const dvv::media::ReadLease>, MAX_STREAMS>;
using OpenResult =
    dvv::Result<std::variant<std::unique_ptr<dvv::media::ISource>, dvv::media::Ambiguous>>;

class SessionCore;
// The detached task bodies (defined after the class; they use only its public members).
dvv::Task<void> openSource(std::shared_ptr<SessionCore> core, OpenJob job,
                           std::uint64_t stream_set);
dvv::Task<void> requestFrames(std::shared_ptr<SessionCore> core,
                              std::shared_ptr<dvv::media::FrameStore> store, std::int64_t index,
                              Tag tag, std::vector<RequestSlot> slots);

class SessionCore final : public std::enable_shared_from_this<SessionCore> {
public:
    SessionCore(SessionOptions options, std::shared_ptr<dvv::SerialExecutor> executor,
                std::unique_ptr<dvv::media::SourceFactory> factory) noexcept
        : options_(std::move(options)), executor_(std::move(executor)), factory_(std::move(factory)) {}

    // --- any thread ---
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::IExecutor& executor() const noexcept { return *executor_; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool stopped() const noexcept { return stopped_.load(std::memory_order_acquire); }
    [[nodiscard]] SessionStatus status() const {
        std::lock_guard lock(status_mutex_);
        return status_;
    }

    // --- Session executor only, from here on ---
    [[nodiscard]] bool stopping() const noexcept { return stopping_; }
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::media::SourceFactory& factory() const noexcept { return *factory_; }

    void installStack(MediaStack stack) {  // Task 5: the first stack; Task 6: the full version
        stack_ = std::move(stack);
        ++stack_epoch_;
        emitStackChanged();
    }

    void open(std::uint32_t slot, dvv::media::SourceRequest request,
              std::optional<std::int64_t> start) {
        opens_.push_back(OpenJob{slot, std::move(request), start});
        startNextOpen();
    }
    void closeStreams();  // described
    void play() { if (player_) player_->play(clock().now()); }
    void pause() { if (player_) player_->pause(); }
    void togglePlay();    // described
    void stop() { if (player_) player_->stop(); }
    void setLoop(bool loop) { loop_ = loop; if (player_) player_->setLoop(loop); }
    void step(std::int64_t delta) { if (player_) player_->step(delta); }
    void seek(std::int64_t index) { if (player_) player_->seek(index); }
    void logDiagnostics() const;  // described

    void onTick() {
        scheduled_.reset();  // the deadline fired; pump() schedules the next one
        if (!stopping_ && player_) {
            player_->tick(clock().now());
        }
        pump();
    }

    void pump() {
        // Task 6: the lost-context check goes here.
        if (!stopping_ && player_ && stack_.store) {
            for (const std::int64_t index : player_->takeRequests()) {
                startRequest(index);
            }
            if (const std::optional<std::int64_t> index = player_->takeShow()) {
                show(*index);
            }
            std::erase_if(ready_, [this](const auto& entry) { return !player_->keeps(entry.first); });
            updateBottleneck(clock().now());  // described
            scheduleTimer(player_->nextDeadline());
        } else {
            scheduleTimer(std::nullopt);
        }
        publishStatus();
    }

    // A detached task (design §5.2 step 5): counted until it has finished on the executor.
    void spawn(dvv::Task<void> body, bool request);
    void taskFinished(bool request) {
        --tasks_;
        if (request) {
            --request_tasks_;
        }
        if (tasks_ == 0 && drain_waiter_) {
            // Through the executor, not inline: the finishing task's frame is still on the stack.
            executor_->post([handle = std::exchange(drain_waiter_, {})] { handle.resume(); });
        }
        pump();
    }

    void openDone(OpenJob job, std::uint64_t stream_set, OpenResult opened) {
        open_running_ = false;
        if (stopping_) {
            return;
        }
        if (stream_set == stream_set_) {
            if (!opened) {
                dvv::Error error = std::move(opened).error();
                if (needsFormat(job.request, error)) {
                    std::string reason = error.message;
                    emitFormatNeeded(job.slot, std::move(job.request), {}, std::move(reason));
                } else {
                    emitError(std::move(error));
                }
            } else if (auto* ambiguous = std::get_if<dvv::media::Ambiguous>(&*opened)) {
                std::string reason = std::format("the format of '{}' is ambiguous",
                                                 dvv::formatUri(job.request.uri));
                emitFormatNeeded(job.slot, std::move(job.request), std::move(*ambiguous),
                                 std::move(reason));
            } else {
                attachSource(job.slot,
                             std::shared_ptr<dvv::media::ISource>(std::move(std::get<0>(*opened))),
                             job.start);
            }
        }  // else closeStreams() ran meanwhile: the source dies here, nothing is reported
        startNextOpen();
    }

    void requestDone(std::int64_t index, const Tag& tag,
                     std::vector<dvv::Result<dvv::media::ReadLease>> results) {
        if (stopping_ || !player_ || tag != currentTag()) {
            return;  // stale: the leases are released here, on the executor
        }
        for (std::size_t slot = 0; slot < results.size(); ++slot) {
            if (!results[slot]) {
                requestFailed(index, slot, tag, std::move(results[slot]).error());
                return;
            }
        }
        const bool kept = player_->frameReady(index, clock().now());  // P2
        if (kept && !ready_.contains(index)) {
            Leases leases{};
            for (std::size_t slot = 0; slot < results.size(); ++slot) {
                leases[slot] = std::make_shared<const dvv::media::ReadLease>(std::move(*results[slot]));
            }
            ready_.emplace(index, std::move(leases));
        }
    }

    // Binding order (design §5.5, Session part).
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::Task<void> shutdownOnExecutor() {
        if (stopping_) {
            co_return;  // a second shutdown(): the first one finishes the work
        }
        stopping_ = true;
        options_.timer->cancel();  // blocks only while a callback is posting a tick
        scheduled_.reset();
        co_await dvv::resumeOn(*executor_);  // FIFO: ticks posted before cancel() returned run first
        opens_.clear();
        ++stream_set_;  // running opens and requests become stale
        ready_.clear();
        options_.sink->clear();
        publishStatus();
        if (tasks_ > 0) {
            co_await DrainAwaiter{this};
        }
        MediaStack stack = std::exchange(stack_, MediaStack{});
        for (const Stream& stream : streams_) {
            if (stack.store && stream.origin != 0) {
                stack.store->detach(stream.origin);
            }
        }
        streams_.clear();
        player_.reset();
        stack = MediaStack{};  // the Session's context reference is released here, on the executor
        factory_.reset();
        publishStatus();
        stopped_.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    }

private:
    struct DrainAwaiter {
        SessionCore* core;
        [[nodiscard]] bool await_ready() const noexcept { return core->tasks_ == 0; }
        void await_suspend(std::coroutine_handle<> handle) const noexcept {
            core->drain_waiter_ = handle;
        }
        void await_resume() const noexcept {}
    };

    [[nodiscard]] IClock& clock() const noexcept { return *options_.clock; }
    [[nodiscard]] Tag currentTag() const noexcept { return {stream_set_, stack_epoch_, generation_}; }

    void startNextOpen() {
        // Task 6: every stack build runs as a step of an open job (design v2 §5.3 step 3).
        while (!open_running_ && !opens_.empty() && !stopping_) {
            OpenJob job = std::move(opens_.front());
            opens_.pop_front();
            if (job.slot >= MAX_STREAMS) {  // D2: slot 0 only
                emitError(dvv::Errc::Unsupported, "one stream in v1.0");
                continue;
            }
            open_running_ = true;
            spawn(openSource(shared_from_this(), std::move(job), stream_set_), false);
        }
    }

    void startRequest(std::int64_t index) {
        std::vector<RequestSlot> slots;
        slots.reserve(streams_.size());
        for (const Stream& stream : streams_) {
            slots.push_back({stream.origin, stream.view});
        }
        spawn(requestFrames(shared_from_this(), stack_.store, index, currentTag(), std::move(slots)),
              true);
    }

    void show(std::int64_t index) {
        const auto entry = ready_.find(index);
        if (entry == ready_.end()) {
            DVV_LOG_ERROR("session: frame {} was due but is not ready", index);
            return;
        }
        FrameSet frames;
        frames.index = index;
        frames.count = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(streams_.size());
        frames.views = entry->second;
        frames.epoch = stack_epoch_;
        ++shown_;
        options_.sink->show(std::move(frames));
    }

    void scheduleTimer(std::optional<Clock::time_point> deadline) {
        if (deadline == scheduled_) {
            return;
        }
        scheduled_ = deadline;
        if (!deadline) {
            options_.timer->cancel();
            return;
        }
        options_.timer->schedule(*deadline, [weak = weak_from_this()] {
            // Timer thread: it only posts (design §5.1). shutdown() cancels the timer before the
            // core can go away, so this never holds the last reference.
            if (const std::shared_ptr<SessionCore> core = weak.lock()) {
                try {
                    core->executor().post([core] { core->onTick(); });
                } catch (const std::exception& error) {
                    DVV_LOG_ERROR("session: a timer tick was lost: {}", error.what());
                }
            }
        });
    }

    void requestFailed(std::int64_t index, std::size_t slot, const Tag& tag, dvv::Error error) {
        static_cast<void>(tag);  // Task 6: DeviceLost -> deviceLost(tag.epoch, error)
        if (error.code == dvv::Errc::OutOfMemory && oom_retried_.insert(index).second) {
            // Design §5.2: halve the window and request the index again, once. Results of the old
            // window carry the old generation and are dropped.
            oom_window_ = std::max<std::uint32_t>(2, config_.window / 2);
            ++generation_;
            resetPlayer(std::max<std::int64_t>(player_->status(clock().now()).position, 0), true);
            const Clock::time_point now = clock().now();
            for (const auto& [ready, leases] : ready_) {
                if (player_->keeps(ready)) {
                    player_->frameReady(ready, now);
                }
            }
            return;
        }
        player_->frameFailed(index);
        const std::string where =
            std::format("frame {} of '{}'", index, streams_[slot].info.resolvedUri);
        emitError(error.wrap(error.code, where));
    }

    void attachSource(std::uint32_t slot, std::shared_ptr<dvv::media::ISource> source,
                      std::optional<std::int64_t> start);                                  // described
    void streamsReplaced(std::optional<std::int64_t> start);                               // described
    void resetPlayer(std::int64_t position, bool keep_playing);                            // described
    [[nodiscard]] PlayerConfig makeConfig() const;                                         // described
    void updateBottleneck(Clock::time_point now);                                          // described
    void publishStatus();                                                                  // described
    [[nodiscard]] static bool needsFormat(const dvv::media::SourceRequest& request,
                                          const dvv::Error& error) {
        return request.uri.scheme == "file" && !request.uri.param("fmt").has_value() &&
               (error.code == dvv::Errc::Unsupported || error.code == dvv::Errc::Format);
    }

    // Events: only on the executor, never while stopping (described: emitError(Errc, string),
    // emitStreamsChanged, emitFormatNeeded, emitStackChanged with weak_ptr(stack_.context) and
    // stack_epoch_).
    void emitError(dvv::Error error) {
        if (!stopping_) {
            options_.events->error(std::move(error));
        }
    }
    void emitError(dvv::Errc code, std::string message) {
        emitError(dvv::makeError(code, std::move(message)).error());
    }
    void emitFormatNeeded(std::uint32_t slot, dvv::media::SourceRequest request,
                          dvv::media::Ambiguous candidates, std::string reason);
    void emitStreamsChanged();
    void emitStackChanged();

    SessionOptions options_;
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::SerialExecutor> executor_;
    std::unique_ptr<dvv::media::SourceFactory> factory_;

    MediaStack stack_;
    std::uint64_t stack_epoch_ = 0;
    std::vector<Stream> streams_;
    std::uint64_t stream_set_ = 0;
    std::uint64_t generation_ = 0;
    std::optional<PlayerLogic> player_;
    PlayerConfig config_{};
    bool loop_ = false;
    std::map<std::int64_t, Leases> ready_;
    std::set<std::int64_t> oom_retried_;
    std::optional<std::uint32_t> oom_window_;
    std::deque<OpenJob> opens_;
    bool open_running_ = false;
    std::uint32_t tasks_ = 0;          // every detached task
    std::uint32_t request_tasks_ = 0;  // SessionStatus::inflight
    std::coroutine_handle<> drain_waiter_;
    std::optional<Clock::time_point> scheduled_;
    std::uint64_t shown_ = 0;
    std::string bottleneck_;
    bool stopping_ = false;
    std::atomic<bool> stopped_{false};

    mutable std::mutex status_mutex_;
    SessionStatus status_;
};

namespace {

// Fire-and-forget coroutine (like M1b's FrameJob): starts at once, frees itself at the end.
struct Detached {
    struct promise_type {
        Detached get_return_object() const noexcept { return {}; }
        [[nodiscard]] std::suspend_never initial_suspend() const noexcept { return {}; }
        [[nodiscard]] std::suspend_never final_suspend() const noexcept { return {}; }
        void return_void() const noexcept {}
        // Only an allocation failure of resumeOn below gets here: the counters would never
        // reach zero and shutdown() would hang, so stop the process instead.
        void unhandled_exception() const noexcept { std::terminate(); }
    };
};

Detached runDetached(std::shared_ptr<SessionCore> core, dvv::Task<void> body, bool request) {
    try {
        co_await std::move(body);
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        DVV_LOG_ERROR("session: a task failed: {}", error.what());
    } catch (...) {
        DVV_LOG_ERROR("session: a task failed with an unknown exception");
    }
    // The bookkeeping runs on the executor; resumeOn never resumes inline.
    co_await dvv::resumeOn(core->executor());
    core->taskFinished(request);
}

}  // namespace

void SessionCore::spawn(dvv::Task<void> body, bool request) {
    ++tasks_;
    if (request) {
        ++request_tasks_;
    }
    runDetached(shared_from_this(), std::move(body), request);
}

dvv::Task<void> openSource(std::shared_ptr<SessionCore> core, OpenJob job,
                           std::uint64_t stream_set) {
    OpenResult opened{};
    try {
        opened = co_await core->factory().open(job.request);  // `job` lives in this frame
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        opened = dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Internal,
                                std::format("opening '{}' failed: {}",
                                            dvv::formatUri(job.request.uri), error.what()));
    }
    co_await dvv::resumeOn(core->executor());  // the factory may resume on the I/O pool
    core->openDone(std::move(job), stream_set, std::move(opened));
}

dvv::Task<void> requestFrames(std::shared_ptr<SessionCore> core,
                              std::shared_ptr<dvv::media::FrameStore> store, std::int64_t index,
                              Tag tag, std::vector<RequestSlot> slots) {
    std::vector<dvv::Result<dvv::media::ReadLease>> results;
    try {
        std::vector<dvv::Task<dvv::Result<dvv::media::ReadLease>>> acquires;
        acquires.reserve(slots.size());
        for (const RequestSlot& slot : slots) {
            acquires.push_back(store->acquire({slot.origin, index}, slot.view, core->executor()));
        }
        // Every acquire resumes on the executor (resume_on), so whenAll does too.
        results = co_await dvv::whenAll(std::move(acquires));
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        // Thrown either before any suspension (on the executor, inside pump) or by whenAll after
        // every child resumed on the executor: either way this runs on the executor.
        results.clear();
        results.push_back(dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Internal,
                                         std::format("frame {}: {}", index, error.what())));
    }
    core->requestDone(index, tag, std::move(results));
}

}  // namespace detail

template <class F>
void Session::command(F f) {
    core_->executor().post([core = core_, f = std::move(f)]() mutable {
        if (core->stopping()) {
            return;  // refused once shutdown() has started
        }
        f(*core);
        core->pump();
    });
}

namespace {
dvv::Task<void> shutdownCore(std::shared_ptr<detail::SessionCore> core) {
    co_await dvv::resumeOn(core->executor());
    co_await core->shutdownOnExecutor();
}
}  // namespace

dvv::Task<void> Session::shutdown() {
    return shutdownCore(core_);
}

Session::~Session() {
    if (core_ && !core_->stopped()) {
        DVV_LOG_WARN("session: destroyed without shutdown(); shutting down now");
        try {
            dvv::syncWait(shutdownCore(core_));
        } catch (const std::exception& error) {
            DVV_LOG_ERROR("session: shutdown failed: {}", error.what());
        }
    }
}

void Session::open(std::uint32_t slot, dvv::media::SourceRequest request,
                   std::optional<std::int64_t> start) {
    command([slot, request = std::move(request), start](detail::SessionCore& core) mutable {
        core.open(slot, std::move(request), start);
    });
}
// closeStreams, play, pause, togglePlay, stop, setLoop, step, seek and logDiagnostics are
// one-line command() calls of the same form. status() returns core_->status().

}  // namespace rawreel
```

`Session::create` (described in Behaviour) posts the first stack:
`core->executor().post([core, stack = std::move(stack)]() mutable { core->installStack(std::move(stack)); core->pump(); });`
`requestFailed`'s retry loop calls `frameReady` for entries kept by the ready map, so the player
does not request them again (Note).

**Test support** (`tests/test_source_provider.{h,cpp}`, `tests/session_fixture.{h,cpp}`):
- **Test provider** `TestSourceProvider` (component id `test`, kind Source, version 1, empty
  schema, `probe` always available, `DVV_REGISTER(dvv::media::ISourceProvider, TestSourceProvider)`
  in the `.cpp` of the test executable; built like M2's `tests/fake_providers.cpp`). Schemes
  `slow` and `fail`; `match` → confidence 100 for them. Both are opaque schemes for
  `dvv::parseUri`, so `open` parses `parseUri("synthetic:" + uri.body)` to read the parameters:
  `w` (32), `h` (16), `fmt` (`yuv420p`), `frames` (40), `fps` (25), and
  - `slow:` — `index=N`, `gate=<name>`: `produce(N)` waits (on the I/O thread, at most 10 s, then
    `Internal "gate <name> timed out"`) until `releaseGate(name)`; `opengate=<name>`: `open()`
    waits the same way before it returns.
  - `fail:` — `index=N`, `errc=io|devicelost|oom`, `times=K` (0 = always, default 0):
    `produce(N)` returns `Io "injected read error"`, `DeviceLost "injected device loss"` or
    `OutOfMemory "injected out of memory"` for its first K calls (an atomic counter per source).
  The source (`TestSource final : ISource, Managed<TestSource>`, created with
  `SourceFactory::issueKey<TestSource>(std::move(ticket))`) has `SourceInfo{desc{fmt, colour 0, w,
  h}, fps, frames, RandomCheap, deterministic, resolvedUri = formatUri(request.uri), provider
  "test"}`. `produce(i)`: `co_await resumeOn(ctx.io())`, the gate/failure rule, then a host frame
  of `geometry(fmt, w, h).bytes` filled by `pack(CodeValues)` with
  `testCode(fmt, c, x, y, i) = (7x + 13y + 29c + 3i) mod 2^bits` on each component's own grid
  (`componentSize`), then `ctx.publish(frame)`.
  Header (complete):

```cpp
#pragma once
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string_view>
#include "dvv/media/types.h"
namespace rawreel::testing {
// Named gates of slow: sources (process-wide). closeGate() makes waiters block; releaseGate() lets
// every current and later waiter pass. gateWaiters() counts threads waiting right now.
void closeGate(std::string_view name);
void releaseGate(std::string_view name);
[[nodiscard]] std::size_t gateWaiters(std::string_view name);
// The code value a slow:/fail: source writes for component c at (x, y) of its grid, frame `index`.
[[nodiscard]] std::uint16_t testCode(dvv::media::FormatId format, std::size_t component,
                                     std::uint32_t x, std::uint32_t y, std::int64_t index);
}  // namespace rawreel::testing
```

- **Fixture:** `FakeClock` (`now()`, `advance(d)`; starts at `Clock::time_point{} + 1h`);
  `FakeTimer` (`schedule` stores one `(when, callback)`; `cancel` drops it; `advance(d)` moves the
  clock and, when the stored deadline is due, takes the callback out and runs it on the calling
  thread, which posts the tick exactly as `ThreadTimer` does; all under a mutex);
  `RecordingSink` (records each `show`: index, count, epoch, the first view's `key().origin`, and
  whether every `views[s]->key().index == index`; keeps only the latest FrameSet; `clear()` drops
  it and counts;
  `waitShown(index, 5 s)` on a condition variable); `RecordingEvents` (every event appended to a
  vector under a mutex, with `waitFor(predicate, 5 s)`; stores `stackChanged` as `(expired?,
  epoch)`, the last weak context, errors as `dvv::Error`); `SessionFixture` (cpu pool of 4, I/O
  pool of 4, `ComponentRegistry::create(Capabilities{})`, fakes, a counting host-only `makeStack`
  that records its `DeviceId` argument, `Session::create`; helpers `open(slot, uri)` (parseUri +
  `SourceRequest`), `waitStatus(predicate, 5 s)` (polls `status()` every millisecond until the
  deadline: a condition wait, never a fixed sleep), `settle(min_ready)` = `waitStatus(inflight == 0
  && readyAhead >= min_ready)`, `tick()` = `timer.advance(40 ms)`; the destructor awaits
  `shutdown()` if a test did not, then drops everything and waits up to 5 s for
  `LifetimeRegistry::instance().liveCount() == 0`).

**Tests first** (`tests/test_session.cpp` in doctest suite `session`; `tests/test_timer.cpp`
without a suite). Default streams: `A = synthetic:gradient?w=32&h=16&fmt=yuv420p&frames=40&fps=25`,
`B = synthetic:noise?w=32&h=16&fmt=nv12&frames=40&fps=25`. The host budget gives `W = 8`.
- `timer: steadyClock is the steady clock`: two `now()` calls are non-decreasing and within 1 s of
  `Clock::now()`.
- `timer: fires once after the deadline`: schedule `now + 30 ms`; the callback stores
  `Clock::now()` into a promise; the future is ready within 2 s; the stored time `>= deadline` and
  `< deadline + 1 s`; after `cancel()` the call count is 1.
- `timer: schedule replaces the pending deadline`: schedule `A` at `now + 10 s`, then `B` at
  `now + 20 ms`; `B` runs; after `cancel()`, `A` never ran.
- `timer: cancel drops the pending callback`: schedule `A` at `now + 50 ms`, `cancel()`, schedule
  `B` at `now + 60 ms`; after `B` ran, `A` count is 0.
- `timer: cancel waits for a running callback`: the callback sets `entered`, waits on a latch,
  sets `returned`; the test waits for `entered`, starts a thread that calls `cancel()` and then
  checks `returned == true`, counts the latch down, joins: the check passed.
- `timer: cancel inside a callback throws`: the callback calls `cancel()` in `try`; it records
  `std::logic_error`.
- `timer: a past deadline fires at once`: schedule `now − 1 s`; it runs within 1 s.
- `timer: destruction cancels`: schedule `now + 50 ms` with a callback setting a shared flag;
  destroy the timer at once; the flag is false (the thread is joined, so it can never run).
- `session: create checks its options`: each of components, cpu, io, clock, timer, sink, events
  missing → `Errc::Internal`; `inflight = 0` → `Internal`.
- `session: one stream shows its frames` (§8.2 case 1): open A into slot 0; `streamsChanged` with
  1 stream (slot 0, A's resolved URI); FrameSet 0 shown with `count == 1`; play 10 frames
  (`settle(min(8, remaining))`, `tick()`); every recorded FrameSet has `count == 1` and the view
  of its index; `status().gpuAvailable == true` (the host-only stack is installed).
- `session: play shows every index once, in order` (case 2): one stream A; after frame 0 is shown,
  `play()`; 30 × (`settle`, `tick()`); the shown history is exactly `0, 1, …, 30`;
  `status().player.stalls == 0`.
- `session: a slow frame stalls and re-anchors` (case 3): `slow:gradient?index=10&gate=g3`
  (gate closed first); play; for indices 1..9 `waitStatus(readyAhead >= needed)` then `tick()`;
  one more `tick()` → `status().player.stalled`, `stalls == 1`, position 9; `releaseGate("g3")` →
  10 is shown with the fake clock unchanged; `timer.advance(39 ms)` → position still 10;
  `timer.advance(1 ms)` → 11 (one period after the late show, no burst); no index skipped.
- `session: step, seek, home, end and loop` (case 4): paused at 0: `step(1)` → 1, `step(-1)` → 0,
  `seek(20)` → 20, `seek(0)` → 0, `seek(39)` → 39; `play()` at 39 without loop → stays paused at
  39; `setLoop(true)`, `play()`, `tick()` → 0 is shown; stepping while playing pauses.
- `session: slot 1 is refused and an open replaces the stream` (case 5): A in slot 0, frame 0
  shown; `open(1, B)` → one `error` with code `Unsupported` whose message contains
  `one stream in v1.0`, still 1 stream (A); `open(0, C)` with
  `C = synthetic:noise?w=64&h=16&fmt=nv12&frames=40&fps=25` (another size and format) →
  `streamsChanged` with 1 stream of 64x16 nv12, and every FrameSet recorded after that event has
  `count == 1` and C's origin (none of the old `streamSet`). Start position (design v2 §4.3):
  `open(0, A, 7)` → the first FrameSet after its `streamsChanged` has index 7, not playing;
  `open(0, A, 1000)` → index 39 (clamped to `length − 1`); `play()`, wait for position ≥ 2, then
  `open(0, C)` without a start → index 0 is shown and `status().player.playing` stays true (the
  replaced stream was playing).
- `session: a read error pauses at the last shown frame` (case 6): `fail:gradient?index=12&errc=io`;
  play with `settle`/`tick()` until the player stops advancing; position 11, not playing; exactly
  one `error` event whose `message` contains `frame 12` and whose cause is `Io`; 12 never shown.
- `session: an ambiguous raw file asks for the format` (case 8): write 3 × 867 bytes
  (yuv420p 33x17) to `<temp dir>/clip_33x17.yuv`; `open(0, file:<path>)` → `formatNeeded(0, …)`
  with one candidate (`raw`, guess 33x17 yuv420p), no `streamsChanged`; then
  `open(0, applyChoice(uri, choiceFromCandidate(candidate)))` → `streamsChanged` with 1 stream of
  3 frames.
- `session: shutdown with requests in flight` (case 11): `slow:gradient?index=1&gate=g11`; wait
  until `status().inflight >= 1`; start `syncWait(session->shutdown())` on a `std::jthread`;
  `releaseGate("g11")`; the thread finishes within 5 s; after the fixture is gone,
  `liveCount() == 0` within 5 s.
- `session: out of memory halves the window and retries once` (case 12): (a)
  `fail:gradient?index=5&errc=oom&times=1`: after the failure `settle` reaches `readyAhead == 4`
  and never more; playing on shows 5; no `error`. (b) `times=2`: an `error` containing `frame 5`
  with cause `OutOfMemory`; position stops at 4.
- `session: opens are serialised` (case 13): close gates `oa` and `ob`;
  `open(0, slow:gradient?opengate=oa)`, `open(0, slow:noise?opengate=ob)`; `releaseGate("ob")`,
  then `releaseGate("oa")`; the last `streamsChanged` has 1 stream whose `resolvedUri` contains
  `noise`, and the latest FrameSet's lease `key().origin` is that stream's (two `streamsChanged`
  in total). Also: `open(0, slow:gradient?opengate=oc)`, `closeStreams()`, `releaseGate("oc")` →
  no stream, no event after the `streamsChanged({})`.
- `session: bottleneckStage` (unit): two `StoreStats` with converter `GpuUnpack` +5 ms and
  `Produce` +2 ms → `"unpack"`; no growth → `""`; `ToDisplay` largest → `"display"`;
  `CpuUnpack` growth alone → `""`.
- `session: diagnosticsLines` (unit): a stats value with one tier, one converter and one lease
  gives 4 lines, the first starting with `store: frames`.
- First failure: the build fails (`core/session.h` missing). With a stub `create` that returns
  `Internal`, every Session case fails at `SessionFixture`; the timer cases fail on a stub that
  never fires.

  `(streamSet, epoch)`: the out-of-memory rule restarts the window without dropping the stream
  set, and results of the old window must not be counted twice. Ready entries survive the restart
  and are announced again to the player.

  (`resetPlayer(position, true)`), so the start-position rule and the retry share one function.

### Task 6: Session: GPU stack, device lost and reopen

**Goal:** Give the Session its default GPU stack and the device-lost path of design v2 §5.3. A loss
pauses, forgets the stream (keeping it as `lastStream`), releases the stack and reports
`error(DeviceLost)`, and nothing is rebuilt automatically. `reopen()` rebuilds the stack and reopens
`lastStream` at its kept position, paused. Every stack build after `create()` runs as a step of a job
in the open FIFO, so two `makeStack` calls never overlap and opens stay in call order. The §8.2
headless cases 7, 14, 15 and 16 pass in `vs2026` and `vs2026-asan` (×10 in Debug in the task, and
×10 under `vs2026-asan` at this task's checkpoint, the Windows race check that replaces TSan ×10
under U-WIN). The rawreel `gpu` Session cases (play
as display images, injected loss → live context count 0, reopen → count 1 and epoch 2) pass on
NVIDIA in `vs2026` and `vs2026-asan`. The task also creates `tests/gpu_support.*` for Task 7.

**Files** (rawreel only, branch `m3`; there is no dvv-media part):
- Modify `src/core/session.h`: add `Session::reopen()` and `Session::reportDeviceLost(epoch)`, the
  free function `gpuStackFactory`, and the updated comments on `SessionOptions::makeStack`,
  `Session::open` and `SessionStatus` use.
- Modify `src/core/session.cpp`: `OpenJob` gains `rebuild` and an optional request; add
  `LastStream`, `buildStack`, `stackBuilt`, `finishJob`, `releaseStack`, `deviceLost`, `reopen` and
  `reportDeviceLost`; change `startNextOpen`, `openSource`, `openDone`, `requestFailed`, `pump`,
  `closeStreams` and `publishStatus`; `create()` uses `gpuStackFactory` when `gpus` is set (Code below).
- Modify `tests/test_source_provider.h`, `tests/test_source_provider.cpp`: the `fail:` parameter
  `shots=K` (a per-URI count) plus `resetShots()` and `passGate()`.
- Modify `tests/session_fixture.h`, `tests/session_fixture.cpp`: a gated, counting `makeStack`;
  one shared sequence number for shows and events; a status probe for `error` events; a sink
  inspector.
- Modify `tests/test_session.cpp`: §8.2 cases 7, 14, 15 and 16, plus four cases for the same
  rules (healthy-stack reopen, reopen after `closeStreams`, shutdown during a stack build, stale
  reports).
- Create `tests/gpu_support.h`, `tests/gpu_support.cpp`: the GPU test helpers (Task 7 uses them).
- Create `tests/gpu_test_session.cpp`: the Session on a real GPU (including a small-budget
  eviction case).
- Modify `tests/CMakeLists.txt`: add `gpu_support.cpp`, `gpu_test_session.cpp`,

  do not change: the new cases are inside `rawreel.core_session` and `rawreel.gpu`.

- Produces (`src/core/session.h`, additions; the rest of the header stays as Task 5 left it):

```cpp
// SessionOptions::makeStack comment (replaces Task 5's):
    // Empty: the default — gpuStackFactory(gpus, cpu, io, presentation) when gpus is set, else the
    // host-only store. The first stack is made in create() on the caller's thread (a GPU startup
    // failure fails create()); every later stack is made on `io` as a step of an open job in the
    // open FIFO (design v2 §5.3 step 3), one at a time.

// In class Session:
    // Slot 0 only (D2, D21; slot >= 1 -> error Unsupported "one stream in v1.0"); replaces the
    // stream. Enqueues an open job; with no stack installed the job first builds one (§5.3).
    // `start`: the index to show after the attach (clamped); empty -> frame 0 (§4.3).
    void open(std::uint32_t slot, dvv::media::SourceRequest request,
              std::optional<std::int64_t> start = {});
    // Enqueues a rebuild job (§5.3 step 3): it releases the current stack if there is one (without
    // an error event), builds a new one on `io` and reopens the attached stream, or else the last
    // stream, at its kept position, paused. Ignored with one DVV_LOG_INFO line when a rebuild job
    // is already queued or running.
    void reopen();
    // The presenter of stack `epoch` failed with DeviceLost (GUI). Ignored for another epoch or
    // while no stack is installed.
    void reportDeviceLost(std::uint64_t epoch);

// Free function. The default GPU StackFactory. Device: the argument; else the first entry of
// rankedDevices() whose GpuInfo is usable() (and has optional.swapchain when `presentation`).
// Errors: an argument outside devices() -> Errc::Internal "GPU <n> does not exist"; none found ->
// Errc::Unsupported "no usable GPU device" (+ " that can present to a window" when
// `presentation`). Context: createContext(id, {defaultPipelineCacheFile("rawreel", info), or empty
// with a DVV_LOG_WARN line when that fails, presentation}); store: FrameStore::create({cpu, io,
// context}) with default budgets; viewFor: displayView(*store->deviceDomain()) for every
// FrameDesc. Errors of createContext and FrameStore::create pass through unchanged.
[[nodiscard]] StackFactory gpuStackFactory(std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry> gpus,
                                           std::shared_ptr<dvv::IExecutor> cpu,
                                           std::shared_ptr<dvv::IExecutor> io, bool presentation);
```

- Produces (event and status contract; Tasks 8, 9 and 10 rely on it):
  - **Loss.** The events come in this order on the Session executor: `streamsChanged({})`, then
    `stackChanged({}, 0)`, then `error` with `code == Errc::DeviceLost` and `message == "GPU device
    lost: <cause message>. Reopen to continue."` (`cause` = the triggering error). After the loss,
    `status().gpuAvailable == false`, `status().epoch == 0`, `status().streams == 0` and
    `status().player.position == -1` (no player).
  - **Reopen with a stack installed** (D25). The job sends `streamsChanged({})` and
    `stackChanged({}, 0)` with no `error`, then continues as below.
  - **Build.** On success the job sends `stackChanged(weak(context), e)` with `e` = the previous
    epoch + 1. When the job carries a request, the normal open path follows (`streamsChanged({s})`,
    or `formatNeeded`, or `error`).
    - A build failure sends `error` with `code == Errc::DeviceLost` and `message == "the GPU could
      not be started: <cause message>. Reopen to retry."`, with the cause chained. The DeviceLost
      code is what makes the infobar keep its Reopen button (§5.3 GUI rule).
  - **Startup.** `Session::create` with `gpus` set and an empty `makeStack` uses `gpuStackFactory`
    (Task 8's App). Its errors fail `create()`, and the App exits 4.
- Produces (`tests/gpu_support.h`, complete; Task 7 uses `makeGpuTestEnvironment`,
  `submitAndWait`, `readImage`, `liveCountOf`):

```cpp
#pragma once

// Helpers for rawreel's GPU tests (rawreel_gpu_tests). One GpuTestEnvironment per test case.

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/base/executors.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/gpu_registry.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/submission.h"
#include "dvv/registry/component_registry.h"

namespace rawreel::testing {

struct GpuTestEnvironment {  // members are released in reverse order
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuRegistry> registry;  // headless; validation from the environment
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::ThreadPool> cpu;             // 4 threads
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::ThreadPool> io;              // 4 threads
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::ComponentRegistry> components;
};
// REQUIREs every step (GpuRegistry::create({}), the pools, ComponentRegistry::create(Capabilities{})).
[[nodiscard]] GpuTestEnvironment makeGpuTestEnvironment();
// Live managed objects whose type name ends with `type` (sum over LifetimeRegistry::typeStats()).
[[nodiscard]] std::int64_t liveCountOf(std::string_view type);
// One submission of `record`, awaited with syncWait; resumes on `executor`.
[[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<std::uint64_t> submitAndWait(dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext& context,
                                                       dvv::IExecutor& executor,
                                                       dvv::gpu::RecordFunction record);
// The whole image copied into a HostReadback buffer (copyImageToBuffer, then invalidate), tightly
// packed, `bytes_per_texel` per texel.
[[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<std::vector<std::byte>> readImage(
    dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext& context, dvv::IExecutor& executor,
    const std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage>& image, std::uint32_t bytes_per_texel);

}  // namespace rawreel::testing
```

```cpp
// tests/session_fixture.h (additions):
```
