**Behaviour** (binding: design v2 §5.3, D7, D25, §4.3 "Start position"):
- **Epoch.** `stack_epoch_` goes +1 for every installed stack (the first is 1) and never goes back.
  FrameSets, request tags and `stackChanged` carry it. `status().epoch` is `stack_epoch_` while a
  stack is installed, else 0. `status().gpuAvailable` is `stack_.store != nullptr`.
- **Triggers** (all handled by `deviceLost(epoch, cause)` on the executor):
  - a request result whose first error, in slot order, has `code == DeviceLost`, called with the
    tag's epoch before the out-of-memory rule;
  - `reportDeviceLost(e)`, with cause `DeviceLost "the presenter reported a lost device"`;
  - at the start of `pump()`, when `stack_.context` is non-null and `stack_.context->lost()`, with
    cause `DeviceLost "the device was lost"` (the host-only stack has a null context and never
    triggers).

  `deviceLost` returns at once when stopping, when no stack is installed, or when the epoch is
  not `stack_epoch_`.
- **Loss (§5.3 steps 1-2)**, in this order:
  1. `player.pause()`, then `publishStatus()`, so the snapshot shows the position that `lastStream`
     keeps (Note).
  2. Count the cancelled opens: N = the queued jobs + 1 if a job is running. Empty the FIFO and
     clear `rebuild_running_`. When N > 0, log one Info line "session: N opens cancelled: GPU
     device lost".
  3. `releaseStack()` (below).
  4. Emit `error(cause.wrap(DeviceLost, "GPU device lost: <cause.message>. Reopen to continue."))`.

  Nothing else happens until the user acts. Play and the other player commands are ignored,
  because there is no player.
- **releaseStack()** (§5.3 step 1 without the FIFO and the error; it is also used by a rebuild of
  a healthy stack):
  0. Flush a pending show: `if (player_) { if (const auto i = player_->takeShow()) { show(*i); } }`.
     `PlayerLogic::tick` and `frameReady` move the position before `pump()` takes the show, and the
     `pump()` lost check runs before the show block. Without the flush the kept position would be
     an index the sink never received (arbiter plan-v2 FIX 1; Task 9's `lost` relies on "kept
     position == the last index shown").
  1. When a stream is attached: `player.pause()`, and `last_stream_ = {streams_[0].info.resolvedUri,
     player position}` (otherwise `last_stream_` stays as it is).
  2. `ready_.clear()`, `sink.clear()`, `++stream_set_`.
  3. Detach every origin from the store, drop the streams, the player and the OOM state, and emit
     `streamsChanged({})`.
  4. When not stopping, emit `stackChanged({}, 0)`.
  5. Move `stack_` into a local and release it there, on the executor.

  In-flight tasks keep their own store references. They drain with `DeviceLost` or a stale tag,
  and their results are dropped.
- **Open FIFO** (§5.3 step 3, binding). `OpenJob{slot, optional request, optional start, rebuild}`.
  Exactly one job runs at a time (`open_running_`). When a job *starts* (in `startNextOpen`):
  1. `slot >= MAX_STREAMS` → `error(Unsupported "one stream in v1.0")`, and the next job starts.
  2. `rebuild` → if a stack is installed, `releaseStack()` (no error; the FIFO is kept, so
     `reopen(); open(x)` still opens x afterwards). Then the request and start are cleared. When
     `last_stream_` exists, the request becomes `parseUri(last_stream_->resolvedUri)` and the
     start becomes its position. A parse error is emitted as `error`, and the job continues
     stack-only. `rebuild_running_ = job.rebuild`.
  3. No stack installed → spawn `buildStack` (hop to `io`, `makeStack(options.device)`, exceptions
     → `Internal "makeStack failed: <what>"`, hop back). On the executor, `stackBuilt`:
     - when stopping → end the job; the result is released there, on the executor;
     - a failure, or a stack without `store`/`viewFor` → `error(cause.wrap(DeviceLost, "the GPU
       could not be started: <cause.message>. Reopen to retry."))` and the job ends;
     - success → `installStack` (epoch +1, `stackChanged(weak(context), epoch)`), then continue
       at 4. When `stream_set_` changed while the stack was being built (a `closeStreams`), the
       request is dropped and the job ends.
  4. With a request → `openSource` → `openDone` → `attachSource(slot, source, start)` (Task 5:
     §5.2 step 1, Start position). Without one → the job ends.

  The job ends in `openDone` or in `finishJob()` (`open_running_ = false`, `rebuild_running_ =
  false`, `startNextOpen()`). A running open whose `stream_set` went stale (loss, close) releases
  its source and reports nothing (Task 5 rule, unchanged).
- **reopen()** enqueues `OpenJob{.rebuild = true}`. It is ignored, with one Info line "session:
  Reopen ignored: a rebuild is already queued or running", when `rebuild_running_` is set or a
  queued job has `rebuild`.
- **open() after a loss** (case 15) needs no special code. The job starts with no stack, so step 3
  builds one first.
- **closeStreams()** (Task 5) also resets `last_stream_`. A rebuild job then reopens nothing; with
  a stack installed it still replaces the stack (D25).
- **shutdown()** (Task 5's order, unchanged). The FIFO is cleared and the drain waits for a
  running `buildStack` as well. Its `stackBuilt` sees `stopping_` and releases the new stack on
  the executor.
- **Old-context release (binding).** The Session's context references are released only on its
  executor (`releaseStack`, `stackBuilt`, shutdown) or on `io` (inside `makeStack`, when
  `FrameStore::create` fails after `createContext`). Request tasks hold their own store reference
  and resume on the executor (`resume_on`). The GUI thread and the presenter thread are allowed
  (§5.3). No context is ever released on its own submit or waiter thread, so no destructor joins
  its own thread.
- **`create()`.** With `makeStack` empty and `gpus` set: `gpuStackFactory(gpus, cpu, io,
  presentation)` replaces Task 5's `Internal "no default GPU stack factory yet"`. Everything else
  stays as Task 5 has it.

**Code** (binding: the FIFO handshake and the loss sequence; additions and changes to
`src/core/session.cpp`):

```cpp
// --- types (replace Task 5's OpenJob) ---
struct OpenJob {
    std::uint32_t slot = 0;
    std::optional<dvv::media::SourceRequest> request;  // empty: a stack-only job
    std::optional<std::int64_t> start;                 // §4.3 Start position
    bool rebuild = false;                              // reopen() (§5.3 step 3)
};
struct LastStream {  // the stream forgotten by the last releaseStack()
    std::string resolvedUri;
    std::int64_t position = 0;
};
dvv::Task<void> buildStack(std::shared_ptr<SessionCore> core, OpenJob job, std::uint64_t stream_set);

// --- SessionCore members added (executor-owned) ---
//   std::optional<LastStream> last_stream_;
//   bool rebuild_running_ = false;   // the running job is a rebuild job
// Accessors for buildStack (public section): dvv::IExecutor& io() const { return *options_.io; }
//   dvv::Result<MediaStack> makeStack() { return options_.makeStack(options_.device); }
//   (only buildStack calls makeStack(), on io, while its job runs: never twice at once).

void SessionCore::open(std::uint32_t slot, dvv::media::SourceRequest request,
                       std::optional<std::int64_t> start) {
    opens_.push_back(OpenJob{slot, std::move(request), start, false});
    startNextOpen();
}

void SessionCore::reopen() {
    if (rebuild_running_ || std::ranges::any_of(opens_, &OpenJob::rebuild)) {
        DVV_LOG_INFO("session: Reopen ignored: a rebuild is already queued or running");
        return;
    }
    opens_.push_back(OpenJob{.rebuild = true});
    startNextOpen();
}

void SessionCore::reportDeviceLost(std::uint64_t epoch) {
    deviceLost(epoch,
               dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::DeviceLost, "the presenter reported a lost device").error());
}

void SessionCore::startNextOpen() {
    while (!open_running_ && !opens_.empty() && !stopping_) {
        OpenJob job = std::move(opens_.front());
        opens_.pop_front();
        if (job.slot >= MAX_STREAMS) {
            emitError(dvv::Errc::Unsupported, "one stream in v1.0");
            continue;
        }
        if (job.rebuild) {
            if (stack_.store) {
                releaseStack();  // D25: no error; the FIFO is kept
            }
            job.request.reset();
            job.start.reset();
            if (last_stream_) {
                auto uri = dvv::parseUri(last_stream_->resolvedUri);
                if (uri) {
                    job.request = dvv::media::SourceRequest{.uri = std::move(*uri)};
                    job.start = last_stream_->position;
                } else {
                    emitError(uri.error().wrap(uri.error().code, "the last stream cannot be reopened"));
                }
            }
        }
        open_running_ = true;
        rebuild_running_ = job.rebuild;
        if (!stack_.store) {
            spawn(buildStack(shared_from_this(), std::move(job), stream_set_), false);
        } else if (job.request) {
            spawn(openSource(shared_from_this(), std::move(job), stream_set_), false);
        } else {
            open_running_ = false;  // nothing to do (not reachable: a rebuild released the stack)
            rebuild_running_ = false;
        }
    }
}

void SessionCore::stackBuilt(OpenJob job, std::uint64_t stream_set, dvv::Result<MediaStack> stack) {
    if (stopping_) {
        finishJob();
        return;  // `stack` is released here, on the executor
    }
    if (!stack || !stack->store || !stack->viewFor) {
        const dvv::Error cause =
            stack ? dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Internal, "makeStack returned an incomplete stack").error()
                  : stack.error();
        DVV_LOG_WARN("session: the GPU could not be started: {}", cause.describe());
        emitError(cause.wrap(dvv::Errc::DeviceLost,
                             std::format("the GPU could not be started: {}. Reopen to retry.",
                                         cause.message)));
        finishJob();
        return;
    }
    installStack(std::move(*stack));  // ++stack_epoch_, stackChanged(weak(context), stack_epoch_)
    if (job.request && stream_set == stream_set_) {
        spawn(openSource(shared_from_this(), std::move(job), stream_set_), false);
        return;  // the job ends in openDone
    }
    finishJob();
}

void SessionCore::finishJob() {
    open_running_ = false;
    rebuild_running_ = false;
    startNextOpen();
}

// openDone (Task 5) changes: its first lines become
//     open_running_ = false;
//     rebuild_running_ = false;
// it reads the request as *job.request (an open job always has one), and attaches with
// attachSource(job.slot, std::move(source), job.start).
// openSource (Task 5) changes the same way: core->factory().open(*job.request) and
// dvv::formatUri(job.request->uri) (it is only spawned for a job with a request).

void SessionCore::deviceLost(std::uint64_t epoch, const dvv::Error& cause) {
    if (stopping_ || !stack_.store || epoch != stack_epoch_) {
        return;  // another stack's report, no stack, or this loss is handled already
    }
    DVV_LOG_WARN("session: the GPU device was lost (stack {}): {}", epoch, cause.describe());
    if (player_) {
        player_->pause();
    }
    publishStatus();  // the snapshot read during the error event shows the kept position
    const std::size_t cancelled = opens_.size() + (open_running_ ? 1U : 0U);
    opens_.clear();
    rebuild_running_ = false;  // a running job is dropped by the stream_set_ bump in releaseStack
    if (cancelled > 0) {
        DVV_LOG_INFO("session: {} opens cancelled: GPU device lost", cancelled);
    }
    releaseStack();
    emitError(cause.wrap(dvv::Errc::DeviceLost,
                         std::format("GPU device lost: {}. Reopen to continue.", cause.message)));
}

void SessionCore::releaseStack() {
    if (player_) {  // a show the player already counted reaches the sink first (kept == last shown)
        if (const std::optional<std::int64_t> index = player_->takeShow()) {
            show(*index);
        }
    }
    if (player_ && !streams_.empty()) {
        player_->pause();
        last_stream_ = LastStream{streams_.front().info.resolvedUri,
                                  player_->status(clock().now()).position};
    }
    ready_.clear();
    options_.sink->clear();
    ++stream_set_;  // in-flight results and a running open become stale
    for (const Stream& stream : streams_) {
        if (stream.origin != 0) {
            stack_.store->detach(stream.origin);
        }
    }
    streams_.clear();
    player_.reset();
    oom_retried_.clear();
    oom_window_.reset();
    emitStreamsChanged();  // {}
    if (!stopping_) {
        options_.events->stackChanged({}, 0);  // GUI: destroys presenter and renderer
    }
    MediaStack old = std::exchange(stack_, MediaStack{});
    old = MediaStack{};  // released here, on the executor (old-context release rule)
}

dvv::Task<void> buildStack(std::shared_ptr<SessionCore> core, OpenJob job, std::uint64_t stream_set) {
    // Context and store creation block (device creation, pipeline cache file), so they run on the
    // I/O pool and the Session executor never blocks (design v2 §5.1, §5.3 step 3).
    co_await dvv::resumeOn(core->io());
    dvv::Result<MediaStack> stack = dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Internal, "makeStack did not run");
    try {
        stack = core->makeStack();
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        stack = dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Internal, std::format("makeStack failed: {}", error.what()));
    }
    co_await dvv::resumeOn(core->executor());
    core->stackBuilt(std::move(job), stream_set, std::move(stack));
}

// pump(), first statement:
//     if (!stopping_ && stack_.context && stack_.context->lost()) {
//         deviceLost(stack_epoch_, dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::DeviceLost, "the device was lost").error());
//     }
// requestFailed(index, slot, tag, error), first statement:
//     if (error.code == dvv::Errc::DeviceLost) { deviceLost(tag.epoch, error); return; }
// closeStreams(): also `last_stream_.reset();`
// publishStatus(): epoch = stack_.store ? stack_epoch_ : 0; gpuAvailable = stack_.store != nullptr.
// Session::open/reopen/reportDeviceLost: one-line command() calls (Task 5 form).
```

`gpuStackFactory` follows its header comment. It returns a lambda that captures `gpus`, `cpu`,
`io` and `presentation` (not a context). A `FrameStore::create` failure returns its error, and the
local context is released inside `makeStack`, on `io`, which the release rule allows. The view
function is `[domain = *store->deviceDomain()](const dvv::media::FrameDesc&) { return
dvv::media::displayView(domain); }`. A store without a device domain → `Internal "the GPU store has
no device domain"`.

**Test support changes:**
- `fail:` gains `shots=K`: the first K `produce(index)` calls for this URI fail. The count is per
  URI text (`formatUri(request.uri)`), across every source instance opened from it, so it
  survives a reopen of the same URI. It is a process-wide map under a mutex, cleared by
  `resetShots()`. When `shots` is given, `times` is ignored. The design's `code=devicelost` is
  the existing `errc=devicelost`.
- `passGate(name, limit)` is the gate wait that `slow:` already uses, now public.
- `SessionFixture`, `RecordingSink` and `RecordingEvents` additions: as declared above. The
  RecordingSink inspector must not keep the `FrameSet`, because leases are released on the
  executor.
- `tests/gpu_support.cpp`:
  - `makeGpuTestEnvironment()` is `GpuRegistry::create({})`, `makeThreadPool(4)` twice and
    `ComponentRegistry::create(Capabilities{})`, each `REQUIRE`d.
  - `liveCountOf` sums `typeStats()` entries whose name ends with `type`.
  - `submitAndWait` is `syncWait(context.submit(std::move(record), executor))`, adapted to the
    real submit signature.
  - `readImage` uses a `HostReadback` buffer of `width · height · bytes_per_texel`,
    `copyImageToBuffer` in one submission, `invalidate`, then copies the bytes out.

**Tests first** (`tests/test_session.cpp`, suite `session`). Stream `A =
synthetic:gradient?w=32&h=16&fmt=yuv420p&frames=40&fps=25` and `B =
synthetic:noise?w=32&h=16&fmt=nv12&frames=40&fps=25`; the host budget gives W = 8. "Seq" means the
shared sequence number. **Headless stacks are host-only:** their `context` is null, so every
`stackChanged` weak pointer that `RecordingEvents` records is expired, and these cases match on the
epoch only. `stackChanged(live, N)` means a `stackChanged` with `epoch == N` (N ≥ 1);
`stackChanged(expired, 0)` means one with `epoch == 0`. "The loss" is the `stackChanged` with
epoch 0 that follows a trigger. Only the GPU cases (`gpu_test_session.cpp`) check the weak pointer
itself.
- `session: a device loss reports an error and reopen continues at the kept position` (§8.2 case
  7):
  - Open `fail:gradient?w=32&h=16&fmt=yuv420p&frames=40&index=12&errc=devicelost&shots=1`. Index
    12 lies beyond the first prefetch window, so the loss happens mid-play.
  - Wait until 0 is shown; check `status().gpuAvailable == true` and `status().epoch == 1`.
  - `play()`, then repeat (`settle`, `tick()`) until an `error` event arrives (at most 40 rounds).
  - Check the loss:
    - the error's `code == Errc::DeviceLost` and its message starts with `GPU device lost:` and
      ends with `Reopen to continue.`;
    - `P = error.status.player.position` (the probe), with `P >= 0`, and `P` equals the index of
      the last show of epoch 1 (the kept position is the last index the sink received);
    - the events after the last show hold `streamsChanged({})` and then `stackChanged(expired, 0)`,
      in that seq order, before the error;
    - `waitStatus(!gpuAvailable && epoch == 0 && streams == 0)`;
    - `stackCalls() == 1`;
    - after `tick()` ×3 no show has a seq greater than the loss's.
  - `reopen()`. Within 5 s:
    - `stackCalls() == 2`;
    - a `stackChanged(live, 2)`, then `streamsChanged` with one stream whose `resolvedUri` equals
      the old one;
    - `status().epoch == 2`, `status().player.position == P`, `!status().player.playing`;
    - a show of index P with epoch 2.
  - `play()`, then (`settle`, `tick()`) ×15. The shows of epoch 2 are consecutive from P, and 12
    is among them (the shot is used up).
  - `reportDeviceLost(2)` → `stackChanged(expired, 0)` and a second `DeviceLost` error. Then
    `reopen()` → `stackChanged(live, 3)`, `stackCalls() == 3`, the stream at the new kept
    position (no limit).
- `session: a late result and a stale report of an old stack are ignored` (§8.2 case 14). The
  late frame 3 is made to reach the visible path: if a stale result were accepted, the wanted
  index 3 would be shown at once. Only a separately gated stream `C14` can supply a valid 3.
  1. `closeGate("s14")`, `closeGate("s14b")`. Open
     `slow:gradient?w=32&h=16&fmt=yuv420p&frames=40&index=3&gate=s14`. Wait until 0 is shown and
     `gateWaiters("s14") == 1`.
  2. `reportDeviceLost(1)` → wait for the error. `reopen()` → wait until `status().epoch == 2` and
     index 0 is shown with epoch 2. (The reopened instance's frame 3 waits on `s14` as well.)
  3. `open(0, C14)` with `C14 = slow:noise?w=32&h=16&fmt=nv12&frames=40&index=3&gate=s14b`. Wait
     until a `streamsChanged` holds `C14` and 0 is shown with a seq greater than that event's
     (epoch 2). Then `seek(3)`: 3 is not ready, so nothing is shown yet.
  4. `releaseGate("s14")`. This releases frame 3 of both `slow:gradient` instances (the epoch-1
     one and the replaced epoch-2 one); both results are stale. Then `waitStatus(inflight == 1)`:
     only `C14`'s gated frame 3 is still running, so both stale results have been handled.
  5. Check:
     - no show of index 3 has a seq greater than the `streamsChanged` with `C14` (a stale result
       was not shown);
     - no show with epoch 1 has a seq greater than the loss's;
     - exactly one `error` event;
     - exactly one `stackChanged(expired, 0)`.
  6. `releaseGate("s14b")` → wait until 3 is shown with epoch 2.
  7. `reportDeviceLost(1)`, then `seek(1)` and wait until 1 is shown with epoch 2. This is a
     barrier, because commands run in order.
  8. Check: `status().epoch == 2`, still one `stackChanged(expired, 0)`, still one `error`.
- `session: an open with no stack builds one first` (§8.2 case 15):
  - Open A and wait until 0 is shown. `reportDeviceLost(1)` → wait for the error.
  - `open(0, B)`. Within 5 s:
    - `stackCalls() == 2`;
    - the seq of `stackChanged(live, 2)` is lower than the seq of the `streamsChanged` with B;
    - `status().player.position == 0` (a plain open starts at 0), not playing;
    - 0 is shown with epoch 2.
- `session: stack builds are serialised and a second reopen is coalesced` (§8.2 case 16):
  - Open A and wait until 0 is shown. `reportDeviceLost(1)` → wait for the error.
  - `closeGate(STACK_GATE)`, then `reopen(); reopen(); open(0, B)`. Wait until
    `gateWaiters(STACK_GATE) == 1`, then `releaseGate(STACK_GATE)`.
  - Wait until the last `streamsChanged` holds B and a show of 0 with epoch 2 has a seq greater
    than that `streamsChanged` (A's frame 0 on epoch 2 does not count). Check:
    - `stackCalls() == 2` (create plus one);
    - `stackMaxRunning() == 1`;
    - exactly one `stackChanged(live, …)` after the loss, with epoch 2;
    - a `streamsChanged` with A comes between it and the one with B;
    - `status().epoch == 2`, `status().streams == 1`;
    - one error event in total.
- `session: reopen with a healthy stack replaces it and keeps queued opens` (D25, §5.3 step 3
  (1)):
  - Open A and wait until 0 is shown. `seek(7)` and wait until 7 is shown.
  - `reopen()` → `streamsChanged({})`, `stackChanged(expired, 0)`, `stackChanged(live, 2)`,
    `streamsChanged` with A. Then 7 is shown with epoch 2, not playing, and there is no `error`
    event.
  - `reopen(); open(0, B)` → the final stream is B on epoch 3, a `streamsChanged` with A comes
    between `stackChanged(live, 3)` and the one with B, position 0, and still no `error`.
- `session: reopen after closeStreams rebuilds only the stack`:
  - Open A and wait until 0 is shown. `closeStreams()`, then `reopen()`.
  - Expect `stackChanged(expired, 0)`, then `stackChanged(live, 2)`.
  - Then `waitStatus(gpuAvailable && epoch == 2 && streams == 0)`, and no `streamsChanged` with a
    stream comes after the `closeStreams`.
- `session: shutdown while a stack is being built`:
  - Open A and wait until 0 is shown. `reportDeviceLost(1)` → error. `closeGate(STACK_GATE)`,
    then `reopen()`, and wait until `gateWaiters(STACK_GATE) == 1`.
  - Read `const auto clears = sink.clears();` (`RecordingSink`'s `clear()` count; Task 5's
    fixture counts it). Start `syncWait(session->shutdown())` on a `std::jthread`, then
    `waitFor(sink.clears() > clears)`: `shutdownOnExecutor` calls `sink->clear()` on the executor
    after it sets `stopping_`, so `stackBuilt` will see `stopping_`. Only then
    `releaseGate(STACK_GATE)` (otherwise `stackBuilt` can win the race and install epoch 2).
  - The thread finishes within 5 s, no `stackChanged(live, 2)` is recorded, and after the fixture
    is gone `liveCount() == 0` within 5 s.
- `session: a loss report without a stack or of another epoch is ignored`:
  - Open A and wait until 0 is shown. `reportDeviceLost(7)`, then `seek(2)` and wait until 2 is
    shown. Check: no `stackChanged(expired, 0)`, no error, epoch 1.
  - `reportDeviceLost(1)` → the error arrives. `reportDeviceLost(1)` again, then `open(0, B)` and
    wait for B. Check: exactly one error and one `stackChanged(expired, 0)`.

GPU (`tests/gpu_test_session.cpp`):
- Setup: `makeGpuTestEnvironment()`, then `Session::create({components, cpu, io, gpus =
  registry, device = {}, presentation = false, inflight = 4, steadyClock(), makeThreadTimer(),
  RecordingSink, RecordingEvents})` with an empty `makeStack`, so `gpuStackFactory` is used.
- The sink inspector checks that `views[0]->view().format == displayFormat()`, that `binding()`
  holds a `VkImageB` with `planeCount == 1`, and that `planes[0]->format() ==
  VK_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT`.
- Stream `S = synthetic:gradient?w=64&h=48&fmt=yuv420p10le&frames=120`.
- `gpu session: one stream plays as display images`:
  - Open S, `play()`, and wait until index 30 is shown (30 s deadline).
  - Every recorded show has `count == 1`, a matching index and `inspected == true`.
    `status().epoch == 1` and `gpuAvailable`.
  - After `shutdown()` and releasing the Session: `liveCountOf("ExecutionContext") == 0` and
    `registry->validationMessageCount() == 0`.
- `gpu session: an injected device loss reports an error and reopen continues`:
  - Open S, `play()`, and wait until 10 is shown. Then
    `dvv::gpu::testing::injectDeviceLost(*events.lastContext().lock())`; the temporary
    `shared_ptr` is dropped at once.
  - Within 5 s:
    - an `error` with `code == DeviceLost`;
    - a `stackChanged(expired, 0)`;
    - `status().gpuAvailable == false`.
  - `P` = the error's status position, and `P` equals the index of the last show of epoch 1 that
    the sink recorded (here the loss is usually found by the `pump()` check right after a tick, the
    path of the releaseStack flush). Within 5 s, `liveCountOf("ExecutionContext") == 0`. The
    context's destructor does not join its own thread: a self-join throws `std::system_error` and
    aborts the run.
  - `reopen()`. Within 30 s:
    - `status().epoch == 2` and `liveCountOf("ExecutionContext") == 1`;
    - a show of index P with epoch 2, not playing.
  - `play()` → within 30 s index P + 10 is shown with epoch 2, and every epoch-2 show is
    inspected.
  - After `shutdown()` and releasing the Session: count 0 and validation 0.
- `gpu session: a small-budget store evicts under in-flight GPU work` (M2 final review, M3
  adjustment 4: M2 has no test that evicts while GPU steps are in flight, and 4K playback evicts in
  every tier within about 35 to 80 frames):
  - Setup as above (`inflight = 4`, the Step commands set `DVV_VK_VALIDATION=sync`), but with the
    test's own `makeStack` instead of `gpuStackFactory`. It builds the `MediaStack` the way
    `gpuStackFactory` does (first usable device, `createContext(id, {})`, `viewFor` =
    `displayView(*store->deviceDomain())`), except that the store is
    `FrameStore::create(FrameStoreOptions{cpu, io, context, <device budget>, <staging budget>})`
    with small budgets (the M2 field names, e.g. `deviceBudget` and `stagingBudget`; check them in
    Step 1). The test keeps a `std::weak_ptr` to the store to read `stats()`.
  - Budgets: 3 frames of `S` per tier, from its geometry (64×48 `yuv420p10le`, 3 bytes per pixel):
    staging `3 · 9216 = 27648` bytes; device `3 · (9216 original + 9216 planes + 24576 display
    image) = 129024` bytes. Allocation rounding only makes eviction more likely. If the M2 budget
    rule fails a request instead of evicting when every resident frame is leased (an `error` with
    `OutOfMemory`), raise both budgets to 8 frames (K + window + 2) and record it in the commit
    body: 120 frames still exceed it.
  - Open S (120 frames, loop off), `play()`, and wait until index 119 is shown (60 s deadline).
  - Check:
    - every index 0..119 is shown exactly once, in order (tiny frames are never late on NVIDIA; a
      skipped or repeated index is a failure to investigate, not to relax);
    - every show is inspected (as above) and there is no `error` event;
    - `store->stats().evictions > 0`, read through the weak pointer before `shutdown()`;
    - after `shutdown()` and releasing the Session: `liveCountOf("ExecutionContext") == 0`,
      `registry->validationMessageCount() == 0`, and the CTest entry's exit leak check
      (`DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail`) passes.
  - A failure here that lies in the store (dvv-media) is reported to the controller, which decides
    the fix; a Session failure is fixed in this task.
- **First failure.** The build fails first (`reopen`, `reportDeviceLost` and `gpuStackFactory`
  are missing). With stubs (no-op commands, a factory returning `Internal`):
  - case 7 fails at the wait for `stackChanged(expired, 0)` (in Task 5 a `DeviceLost` request is
    an ordinary read error);
  - cases 14, 15, 16 and the D25 case fail at their first `stackChanged` wait;
  - the two `gpuStackFactory` GPU cases fail at `Session::create`;
  - the small-budget case uses its own `makeStack`, so it may already pass with the stubs (it
    guards M2's eviction under GPU load, not new Session code); record whether it did.

**Notes:**
- Design references: v2 D7, D8, D25, §4.3 (`open`/`reopen`/`reportDeviceLost`, Start position,
  `gpuAvailable`), §5.1, §5.2 step 1, §5.3 (binding: triggers, steps 1-3, the old-context release
  rule), §7 rows "Device lost seen by the Session" and "`makeStack` fails", §8.1 rawreel `gpu`
  row, §8.2 cases 7, 14, 15, 16. Arbiter corrections (a) (no FIFO emptying on a healthy rebuild)
  and (b) (stale report after reopen) are in cases 14 and D25 and in the Code.
- **Note (plan choice):** a failed stack build reports `Errc::DeviceLost`, with the real cause
  chained. §5.3's GUI rule shows Reopen only for `DeviceLost` errors, and the design requires
  that "the infobar keeps Reopen". The message says "Reopen to retry."
- **Note (plan choice):** the first stack is still made in `create()` on the caller's thread (Task
  5, inherited from the approved plan), so a GPU startup failure reaches the GUI synchronously
  (exit 4, design §7). Design v2 §5.1 lists "start" among the `io` builds. Only the stacks built
  after `create()` (Reopen, an open with no stack) run on `io` inside the FIFO. This deviation is
  recorded in the decisions log (M3-plan-v2-arbiter) and in Task 12's spec §5.2 text.
- **Note (plan choice):** `deviceLost` publishes the status once, after the pause and before the
  release. A `status()` read inside the `error` event (case 7's "position captured when the
  `error` event arrived") then shows exactly the position that `lastStream` keeps. The next
  `pump()` publishes the no-stack state.
- **Note (plan choice):** `status().epoch` is 0 while no stack is installed, matching
  `stackChanged({}, 0)`. `stack_epoch_` itself only grows.
- **Note (plan choice):** "N opens cancelled" is logged only when N > 0, and N counts the queued
  jobs plus a running one. A loss also clears `rebuild_running_`, so a Reopen right after a loss
  that hit a running rebuild is accepted. It waits in the FIFO until the dropped job's open
  finishes.
- **Note (plan choice):** `fail:`'s per-URI count is `shots=K` in a process-wide map cleared by the
  fixture. A map has the same lifetime as "the fixture's provider object" (it survives `makeStack`
  and new source instances) without relying on how the registry instantiates providers. The
  design's `code=` is the existing `errc=`.
- **Note (plan choice):** when `closeStreams()` runs while a rebuild job builds its stack, the
  new stack is installed (it is valid) and the job's request is dropped.
- LOG 18 (arbiter): the infobar's Reopen button stays enabled while a rebuild runs. Extra clicks
  are coalesced and logged (v1.x polish).
- Risks:
  - R2: a real loss can differ from injection. The waits are bounded (Task 1), and the `pump()`
    check is a backstop when a store error does not carry `DeviceLost` as the outer code.
  - R9: Session races. One executor, `(streamSet, epoch)` tags, one FIFO job at a time, Windows
    ASan ×10 at the checkpoint, Debug ×10 in the task (TSan returns with Linux; U-WIN accepted risk).
  - R10: a lease destructor on the GUI or presenter thread can release the last reference of a
    lost context. Its destructor does not wait for the GPU (Task 1).
  - R14: after a real TDR, `makeStack` can fail while old references drain. The error keeps
    Reopen, and the user retries.
- Removed against the old Task 7b: the dvv-media `ISource` contract comment and its two-store
  conformance case, `RecoveryPolicy`, the automatic rebuild with re-attached sources,
  `selectDevice`, `retryGpu`, `deviceLostLimit`, `was_playing_`, parked opens and the 7-limit and
  7-spacing cases.

### Task 7: Viewport compositor (one stream, fit)

**Goal:** Build the display end of design D3/D23 in `rawreel_render`: `DisplayState` (the
`IDisplaySink` the Session feeds and the presenter reads, with the binding `callbackMutex` rule),
the `composite.slang` compute shader with 32-byte push constants, `ViewportRenderer` (fits the one
display image into the presenter's extent, background around it, multi-tap downscale, sRGB encode
into an RGBA8 image the presenter blits 1:1; `lastRecorded()` for the selftests) and its CPU
reference `compositeReference`. The GPU output matches the reference (±1) on NVIDIA with zero
validation messages, including the 11×7 multi-tap golden (U-WIN: no lavapipe run).

**Files** (rawreel, branch `m3`):
- Create `src/render/display_state.h`, `src/render/display_state.cpp`: `CompositeParams`,
  `DisplaySnapshot`, `DisplayStats`, `DisplayState`.
- Create `src/render/composite_reference.h`, `src/render/composite_reference.cpp`:
  `CompositeConstants`, the flag and background constants, `compositeConstants`,
  `HostDisplayImage`, `compositePixel`, `compositeReference`.
- Create `src/render/viewport_renderer.h`, `src/render/viewport_renderer.cpp`: `RecordedInfo`,
  `ViewportRenderer`, `isSrgbFormat`.
- Create `shaders/composite.slang`.
- Modify `CMakeLists.txt`: the three `src/render/*.cpp` in `rawreel_render`; `rawreel_render`
  links `rawreel_core` PUBLIC (if Task 6 has not done it yet); `dvv_add_shaders(rawreel_render
  NAMESPACE rawreel_shaders SHADERS shaders/test_pattern.slang shaders/composite.slang)` (the M1b
  test pattern stays until Task 8 removes it).
- Create `tests/test_display_state.cpp` and `tests/test_composite_reference.cpp` (both in
  `rawreel_tests`, the M1b unit executable that links `rawreel_render`; CTest `rawreel.unit`) and
  `tests/gpu_test_compositor.cpp` (in `rawreel_gpu_tests`; CTest `rawreel.gpu`); modify
  `tests/CMakeLists.txt` (add the three sources to their executables).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - **Task 4** (`src/core/view.h`, `src/core/session_types.h`): `MAX_STREAMS` (= 1), `PointD`,
    `ViewTransform::fit(dvv::platform::PixelSize viewport, dvv::platform::PixelSize image)`,
    `zoom()`, `origin()`, `viewport()`, `image()`. Fit semantics (design §4.3, binding): `zoom =
    min(W/w, H/h)`; width-limited iff `W·h ≤ H·w` (64-bit integers); the image's top-left corner
    sits at a whole-pixel offset: 0 on the limiting axis, `(H·w − h·W) / (2·w)` (width-limited) or
    `(W·h − w·H) / (2·h)` (height-limited) on the other, by integer division; `origin = −offset /
    zoom`; an empty viewport or image gives zoom 0. `FrameSet{index = -1, count = 0,
    std::array<std::shared_ptr<const dvv::media::ReadLease>, MAX_STREAMS> views, epoch = 0}`,
    `IDisplaySink` (`show(FrameSet)`, `clear()`).
  - **Task 3:** display views made by a GPU `FrameStore` through ToDisplay: a `VkImageB` binding
    with one `R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT` plane in layout `GENERAL`, linear light, BT.709 primaries;
    `maxPlanDepth` 6.
  - **Task 2** (`dvv/media/display.h`): `displayView(Domain)`, `srgbEncode`, `srgbDecode`,
    `encodeSrgb8`, `halfToFloat`, `floatToHalf`.
  - **Task 6** (`tests/gpu_support.h`, namespace `rawreel::testing`): `GpuTestEnvironment{registry,
    cpu, io, components}`, `makeGpuTestEnvironment()`, `submitAndWait(ExecutionContext&,
    IExecutor&, RecordFunction) → Result<std::uint64_t>`, `readImage(ExecutionContext&, IExecutor&,
    const std::shared_ptr<GpuImage>&, std::uint32_t bytes_per_texel) → Result<std::vector<std::byte>>`
    (whole image, tightly packed); `rawreel_gpu_tests` already links `rawreel_core` and
    `dvv::media_sources` and compiles `gpu_support.cpp`. If Task 6 also has a helper that creates a
    context (or its default stack factory, old name `gpuStackFactory`), the fixture below may use

  - **M2 dvv-gpu:** `ComputeBindings{.storageImages}`, `ExecutionContext::createComputePipeline(
    shader, push_size, bindings)`, the `CommandRecorder::dispatch(pipeline, push_bytes,

- Produces (`src/render/display_state.h`, complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

// The viewport's shared state (design M3 v2 §4.4, D23): the latest FrameSet (the Session's
// IDisplaySink) and the composite parameters (GUI). Thread-safe and non-blocking.

#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <mutex>

#include "core/session_types.h"

namespace rawreel {

// Fit to window only (D23): the renderer computes the fit itself from the presenter extent and
// the display image size. This struct is where v1.x zoom/pan state returns.
struct CompositeParams {
    bool dark = false;  // background: dark (0.12) or light (0.85) sRGB-encoded grey
    bool operator==(const CompositeParams&) const = default;
};
struct DisplaySnapshot {  // one consistent read, taken under one lock
    FrameSet frames;
    CompositeParams params;
    std::uint64_t version = 0;  // +1 for every show(), clear() and setParams()
};
struct DisplayStats {
    std::uint64_t overwritten = 0;  // FrameSets replaced before any snapshot() read them
};

class DisplayState final : public IDisplaySink {
public:
    void show(FrameSet frames) override;
    void clear() override;  // drops every lease it holds
    void setParams(CompositeParams params);
    // show(), clear() and setParams() call the callback after every change while holding
    // callback_mutex_ (never mutex_). Binding: once setOnChanged({}) returns, no callback is running
    // and none will run. The callback must not block (the viewport calls Presenter::requestFrame).
    void setOnChanged(std::move_only_function<void()> callback);
    [[nodiscard]] DisplaySnapshot snapshot() const;
    [[nodiscard]] DisplayStats stats() const;

private:
    void notify();

    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    FrameSet frames_;
    CompositeParams params_{};
    std::uint64_t version_ = 0;
    mutable bool read_ = true;  // the current FrameSet was returned by snapshot()
    DisplayStats stats_;
    std::mutex callback_mutex_;
    std::move_only_function<void()> on_changed_;
};

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Produces (`src/render/composite_reference.h`, complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

// The compositor's push constants and its CPU reference (design M3 v2 §4.4). compositePixel()
// computes exactly what shaders/composite.slang computes, in the same order, in float32.

#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <span>

#include "dvv/platform/native_window.h"
#include "render/display_state.h"

namespace rawreel {

inline constexpr std::uint32_t COMPOSITE_DARK = 1;         // flags bit0: dark background
inline constexpr std::uint32_t COMPOSITE_SKIP_ENCODE = 2;  // flags bit1: the swapchain is _SRGB
inline constexpr std::uint32_t COMPOSITE_MAX_TAPS = 4;     // taps per axis below zoom 0.5
inline constexpr float BACKGROUND_DARK = 0.12F;            // sRGB-encoded grey of the background
inline constexpr float BACKGROUND_LIGHT = 0.85F;

struct CompositeConstants {  // = PushConstants of composite.slang
    std::uint32_t viewportW = 0, viewportH = 0, imageW = 0, imageH = 0;
    float zoom = 0.0F, originX = 0.0F, originY = 0.0F;
    std::uint32_t flags = 0;
};
static_assert(sizeof(CompositeConstants) == 32);

// The constants of one composite at `viewport` (design §4.4): view = ViewTransform::fit(viewport,
// image); viewportW/H = viewport; zoom/originX/originY = float(view.zoom()/origin()); imageW/H =
// image when view.zoom() > 0, else 0 (no frame or an empty size: background only); flags =
// COMPOSITE_DARK when params.dark, | COMPOSITE_SKIP_ENCODE when skip_encode.
[[nodiscard]] CompositeConstants compositeConstants(const CompositeParams& params,
                                                    dvv::platform::PixelSize viewport,
                                                    dvv::platform::PixelSize image,
                                                    bool skip_encode);

struct HostDisplayImage {  // host copy of a display view
    std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
    std::span<const std::uint16_t> rgba16f;  // half floats, row-major, width·height·4
};
// One output pixel (RGBA8, alpha 255) for these constants. When constants.imageW > 0, `image`
// is present with width == imageW and height == imageH; otherwise it is ignored.
[[nodiscard]] std::array<std::uint8_t, 4> compositePixel(const CompositeConstants& constants,
                                                         std::uint32_t px, std::uint32_t py,
                                                         const std::optional<HostDisplayImage>& image) noexcept;
// The CPU reference of one output pixel (design §4.4): compositePixel(compositeConstants(params,
// viewport, image ? {image->width, image->height} : {0, 0}, false), px, py, image). Empty `image`
// (no frame) → background.
[[nodiscard]] std::array<std::uint8_t, 4> compositeReference(
    std::uint32_t px, std::uint32_t py, dvv::platform::PixelSize viewport,
    const CompositeParams& params, std::optional<HostDisplayImage> image);

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Produces (`src/render/viewport_renderer.h`, complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>

#include <vulkan/vulkan_core.h>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/compute_pipeline.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/execution_context.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/presenter.h"
#include "dvv/gpu/resources.h"
#include "dvv/platform/native_window.h"
#include "render/display_state.h"

namespace rawreel {

// What the last recorded composite shows (design M3 v2 §4.4; selftests `player` and `lost`).
struct RecordedInfo {
    std::uint64_t epoch = 0;            // the renderer's epoch; 0 before the first record
    std::int64_t index = -1;            // the composited FrameSet's index; -1: background only
    dvv::platform::PixelSize extent{};  // the output extent of that record
    double zoom = 0.0;                  // ViewTransform::fit(extent, image size).zoom(); 0: no image
    bool operator==(const RecordedInfo&) const = default;
};

// Presenter-thread object, one per presenter (design §4.4, §5.3 GUI side): the composite
// pipeline, its RGBA8 output image (STORAGE | TRANSFER_SRC) and one 1x1 RGBA16F dummy bound when
// there is no frame. Created on the GUI thread, used on the presenter thread (frame()), destroyed
// on the GUI thread after the presenter. frame() and render() are never called concurrently;
// lastRecorded() may be called from any thread.
class ViewportRenderer {
    struct Private {
        explicit Private() = default;
    };

public:
    // `epoch`: the Session stackEpoch of `context` (ISessionEvents::stackChanged). Errors of
    // createComputePipeline and createImage pass through (DeviceLost on a lost context).
    [[nodiscard]] static dvv::Result<std::unique_ptr<ViewportRenderer>> create(
        std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context, std::uint64_t epoch);
    ViewportRenderer(Private key, std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context,
                     std::uint64_t epoch, std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ComputePipeline> pipeline,
                     std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage> dummy) noexcept;

    // The PresentFrame for the presenter: records the composite of snapshot.frames.views[0] with
    // ViewTransform::fit(info.extent, display image size) and snapshot.params. Records again only
    // when the snapshot version, the extent or the sRGB rule changed, or
    // !info.previousFrameCompleted; otherwise the same image and no commands. FrameSets of another
    // epoch (or without a usable display view) draw the background only. The record function holds
    // the FrameSet's leases until the submission has completed.
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<dvv::gpu::PresentFrame> frame(const dvv::gpu::PresentFrameInfo& info,
                                                            const DisplaySnapshot& snapshot);
    // Tests: always records, into a new image of `extent`, and returns it (no sRGB rule).
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<dvv::gpu::PresentFrame> render(dvv::platform::PixelSize extent,
                                                             const FrameSet& frames,
                                                             const CompositeParams& params);
    // The last recorded composite (frame() or render()); RecordedInfo{} before the first. Test use
    // only (selftests); read under recorded_mutex_.
    [[nodiscard]] RecordedInfo lastRecorded() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t epoch() const noexcept { return epoch_; }

private:
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage>> makeOutput(
        dvv::platform::PixelSize extent) const;
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::gpu::RecordFunction recordComposite(
        const std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage>& output, dvv::platform::PixelSize extent,
        const FrameSet& frames, const CompositeParams& params, bool skip_encode);

    std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context_;
    std::uint64_t epoch_;
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ComputePipeline> pipeline_;
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage> dummy_;
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage> output_;  // frame() only
    dvv::platform::PixelSize extent_{};
    std::uint64_t version_ = 0;
    bool skip_encode_ = false;
    bool logged_srgb_ = false;
    mutable std::mutex recorded_mutex_;
    RecordedInfo recorded_;
};

// B8G8R8A8_SRGB, R8G8B8A8_SRGB, A8B8G8R8_SRGB_PACK32.
[[nodiscard]] bool isSrgbFormat(VkFormat format) noexcept;

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- CMake: `rawreel_render` (STATIC, no wx) = the M1b sources + `src/render/display_state.cpp`,
  `src/render/composite_reference.cpp`, `src/render/viewport_renderer.cpp`; PUBLIC `rawreel_core
  dvv::gpu dvv::base`; generated header `rawreel_shaders/composite.h` with
  `rawreel_shaders::composite()`. CTest entries do not change.
- Produces for the later tasks:
  - **Task 8** (GUI core): `DisplayState` (`show`, `clear`, `setParams`, `setOnChanged`,
    `snapshot`), `CompositeParams{dark}` (the Viewport calls `setParams(CompositeParams{.dark =
    …})` once; no refit on resize, D23), `ViewportRenderer::create(context, epoch)` and
    `frame(info, snapshot)` (the presenter provider `[state, renderer](info) { auto s =
    state->snapshot(); return renderer->frame(info, s); }`), `isSrgbFormat`.
  - **Tasks 9 and 10** (selftests `lost` and `player`): `ViewportRenderer::lastRecorded()` →
    `RecordedInfo{epoch, index, extent, zoom}`; `lastRecorded().zoom` equals
    `ViewTransform::fit(lastRecorded().extent, image size).zoom()` exactly (the same double).
  - **M3.1 Task 11b** (perf, U-M31): `DisplayState::stats().overwritten`.
  - Task 12 (README): the known limit "below 1:8 the fit view still undersamples" follows from
    `COMPOSITE_MAX_TAPS = 4`.

**Behaviour:**
- **DisplayState** (Code). `stats().overwritten` counts a non-empty FrameSet replaced by `show()`
  before any `snapshot()` returned it (design E7, R13); `clear()` does not count. Every `show()`,
  `clear()` and `setParams()` raises `version` by 1, even when the params are equal. Replaced
  leases are released outside both mutexes.
- **compositeConstants:** as its header comment. `float` conversion happens only here; the shader
  receives these exact floats and `compositePixel` uses them, so neither recomputes the fit.
- **Pixel rule** (binding, design §4.4; shader and `compositePixel` identical, float32):
  1. Background `grey = srgbDecode(dark ? 0.12 : 0.85)` (linear), colour = (grey, grey, grey).
  2. When `imageW != 0 && imageH != 0 && zoom > 0`: `u = originX + (px + 0.5) / zoom`, `v = originY
     + (py + 0.5) / zoom`; when `0 ≤ u < imageW` and `0 ≤ v < imageH` the colour is the sample,
     else background.
  3. Sample: zoom ≥ 1 → texel `(floor u, floor v)`. zoom < 1 → `n = min(4, ceil(0.5 / zoom))` taps
     per axis, computed as `n = 1; while (n < 4 && float(n) · zoom < 0.5) ++n;`. For `b` in
     `[0, n)` (outer) and `a` in `[0, n)` (inner): `v_b = v + ((b + 0.5)/n − 0.5)/zoom`, `u_a = u +
     ((a + 0.5)/n − 0.5)/zoom`; the tap is the 4-texel bilinear at `(u_a − 0.5, v_b − 0.5)`: `x0 =
     floor(x)`, `fx = x − x0`, texel coordinates clamped into the image, `lerp(a, b, t) = a + t·(b −
     a)`, rows first (`top`, `bottom`), then `lerp(top, bottom, fy)`. The taps are summed in that
     order and the sum divided by `float(n·n)`. For 0.5 ≤ zoom < 1, n = 1 and the tap is the plain
     bilinear at `(u − 0.5, v − 0.5)`.
  4. Store RGBA with alpha 1: `srgbEncode` per channel unless `COMPOSITE_SKIP_ENCODE` (reference:
     `encodeSrgb8(c)`, or `round(255 · clamp(c, 0, 1))` when skipping).
- **ViewportRenderer::create:** pipeline `createComputePipeline(rawreel_shaders::composite(),
  sizeof(CompositeConstants), ComputeBindings{.storageImages = MAX_STREAMS + 1})` (binding 0 the
  display image, binding 1 the output); dummy `createImage({1, 1, VK_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT,
  VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT, "composite dummy"})`. Output images: `{w, h,
  VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM, STORAGE | TRANSFER_SRC, "viewport composite"}`.
- **Usable FrameSet:** `frames.epoch == epoch_`, `1 ≤ count ≤ MAX_STREAMS`, and `views[0]` is a
  valid lease whose binding is a `VkImageB` with one `R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT` plane of this context's
  device and generation (the epoch check runs first and is the one that separates stacks; the
  device/generation comparison only rejects another physical device, because v1.0 generations
  are all `FIRST_GENERATION`). Otherwise the composite is background only
  (the dummy is bound at binding 0, `compositeConstants(…, image = {0, 0}, …)`).
- **frame()** (Code): the sRGB rule (`isSrgbFormat(info.format)`) logs one warning per renderer
  ("viewport: the swapchain format is sRGB; the compositor skips its own encode") and sets
  `COMPOSITE_SKIP_ENCODE` (v1 arbiter LOG 13, v2 FIX 10). A new output image is made when the
  extent changes.
- **lastRecorded():** every record (`frame()` that records, and `render()`) stores `RecordedInfo{
  epoch_, usable ? frames.index : -1, extent, ViewTransform::fit(extent, image size).zoom()}`
  (image size `{0, 0}` when not usable, so zoom 0) under `recorded_mutex_`; `frame()` without a
  record leaves it unchanged.
- No new `Errc`: `create`, `frame` and `render` pass through the errors of `createComputePipeline`
  and `createImage` (`DeviceLost` on a lost context, `OutOfMemory`).

**Code** (binding: the shader, its reference, the Vulkan path and the DisplayState handshake).

`shaders/composite.slang` (complete):

```slang
// rawreel's viewport compositor (design M3 v2 D3, D23, §4.4). One thread per viewport pixel: the
// display image (RGBA16F, linear light, BT.709 primaries) fitted into the viewport with the
// background around it, then the sRGB encode into the RGBA8 output, which the presenter blits 1:1.
// compositePixel() in src/render/composite_reference.cpp computes the same, in the same order.

static const uint FLAG_DARK = 1;
static const uint FLAG_SKIP_ENCODE = 2;
static const uint MAX_TAPS = 4;

struct PushConstants
{
    uint viewportW;
    uint viewportH;
    uint imageW;
    uint imageH;
    float zoom;
    float originX;
    float originY;
    uint flags;
};

[[vk::push_constant]] ConstantBuffer<PushConstants> pc;

// Set 0, pushed per dispatch (M2 push descriptors). Bound images are writable for dvv-gpu; this
// shader only reads binding 0.
[[vk::binding(0, 0)]] [format("rgba16f")] RWTexture2D<float4> display;
[[vk::binding(1, 0)]] [format("rgba8")] RWTexture2D<float4> output;

// IEC 61966-2-1, as dvv::media::srgbEncode/srgbDecode (input clamped).
float srgbEncode(float x)
{
    x = clamp(x, 0.0, 1.0);
    return x <= 0.0031308 ? 12.92 * x : 1.055 * pow(x, 1.0 / 2.4) - 0.055;
}

float srgbDecode(float x)
{
    x = clamp(x, 0.0, 1.0);
    return x <= 0.04045 ? x / 12.92 : pow((x + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
}

float3 texel(int x, int y)
{
    uint2 p = uint2(clamp(x, 0, int(pc.imageW) - 1), clamp(y, 0, int(pc.imageH) - 1));
    return display[p].rgb;
}

float3 mix3(float3 a, float3 b, float t)
{
    return a + t * (b - a);
}

// The 4-texel bilinear around (u - 0.5, v - 0.5), edges clamped.
float3 bilinear(float u, float v)
{
    float x = u - 0.5;
    float y = v - 0.5;
    float x0 = floor(x);
    float y0 = floor(y);
    float fx = x - x0;
    float fy = y - y0;
    int ix = int(x0);
    int iy = int(y0);
    float3 top = mix3(texel(ix, iy), texel(ix + 1, iy), fx);
    float3 bottom = mix3(texel(ix, iy + 1), texel(ix + 1, iy + 1), fx);
    return mix3(top, bottom, fy);
}

float3 sampleDisplay(float u, float v)
{
    if (pc.zoom >= 1.0)
    {
        return texel(int(floor(u)), int(floor(v)));
    }
    // n = min(MAX_TAPS, ceil(0.5 / zoom)), without a division (exact at zoom 0.5, 0.25, ...).
    uint n = 1;
    while (n < MAX_TAPS && float(n) * pc.zoom < 0.5)
    {
        n = n + 1;
    }
    float nf = float(n);
    float3 sum = float3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    for (uint b = 0; b < n; b = b + 1)
    {
        float vb = v + ((float(b) + 0.5) / nf - 0.5) / pc.zoom;
        for (uint a = 0; a < n; a = a + 1)
        {
            float ua = u + ((float(a) + 0.5) / nf - 0.5) / pc.zoom;
            sum = sum + bilinear(ua, vb);
        }
    }
    return sum / float(n * n);
}

[shader("compute")]
[numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
void main(uint3 id: SV_DispatchThreadID)
{
    if (id.x >= pc.viewportW || id.y >= pc.viewportH)
    {
        return;
    }
    float grey = srgbDecode((pc.flags & FLAG_DARK) != 0 ? 0.12 : 0.85);
    float3 colour = float3(grey, grey, grey);
    if (pc.imageW != 0 && pc.imageH != 0 && pc.zoom > 0.0)
    {
        float u = pc.originX + (float(id.x) + 0.5) / pc.zoom;
        float v = pc.originY + (float(id.y) + 0.5) / pc.zoom;
        if (u >= 0.0 && v >= 0.0 && u < float(pc.imageW) && v < float(pc.imageH))
        {
            colour = sampleDisplay(u, v);
        }
    }
    if ((pc.flags & FLAG_SKIP_ENCODE) == 0)
    {
        colour = float3(srgbEncode(colour.r), srgbEncode(colour.g), srgbEncode(colour.b));
    }
    output[id.xy] = float4(colour, 1.0);
}
```

If Task 2's `srgbEncode`/`srgbDecode` constants differ from the ones above (thresholds, 1.055,
0.055, 2.4, 12.92), use Task 2's in the shader; the reference always calls Task 2's functions.

`src/render/composite_reference.cpp`, the reference (binding; plain `float`, never `double`, and
no `std::lerp`):

```cpp
namespace {

using Rgb = std::array<float, 3>;

float mix(float a, float b, float t) {
    return a + t * (b - a);  // the shader's mix3, per channel
}

Rgb texel(const HostDisplayImage& image, int x, int y) {
    const int cx = std::clamp(x, 0, static_cast<int>(image.width) - 1);
    const int cy = std::clamp(y, 0, static_cast<int>(image.height) - 1);
    const std::size_t base =
        (static_cast<std::size_t>(cy) * image.width + static_cast<std::size_t>(cx)) * 4;
    return {dvv::media::halfToFloat(image.rgba16f[base]),
            dvv::media::halfToFloat(image.rgba16f[base + 1]),
            dvv::media::halfToFloat(image.rgba16f[base + 2])};
}

Rgb bilinear(const HostDisplayImage& image, float u, float v) {
    const float x = u - 0.5F;
    const float y = v - 0.5F;
    const float x0 = std::floor(x);
    const float y0 = std::floor(y);
    const float fx = x - x0;
    const float fy = y - y0;
    const int ix = static_cast<int>(x0);
    const int iy = static_cast<int>(y0);
    const Rgb t00 = texel(image, ix, iy);
    const Rgb t10 = texel(image, ix + 1, iy);
    const Rgb t01 = texel(image, ix, iy + 1);
    const Rgb t11 = texel(image, ix + 1, iy + 1);
    Rgb out{};
    for (std::size_t c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {
        const float top = mix(t00[c], t10[c], fx);
        const float bottom = mix(t01[c], t11[c], fx);
        out[c] = mix(top, bottom, fy);
    }
    return out;
}

Rgb sample(const CompositeConstants& k, const HostDisplayImage& image, float u, float v) {
    if (k.zoom >= 1.0F) {
        return texel(image, static_cast<int>(std::floor(u)), static_cast<int>(std::floor(v)));
    }
    std::uint32_t n = 1;  // min(COMPOSITE_MAX_TAPS, ceil(0.5 / zoom)), as the shader
    while (n < COMPOSITE_MAX_TAPS && static_cast<float>(n) * k.zoom < 0.5F) {
        ++n;
    }
    const float nf = static_cast<float>(n);
    Rgb sum{0.0F, 0.0F, 0.0F};
    for (std::uint32_t b = 0; b < n; ++b) {
        const float vb = v + ((static_cast<float>(b) + 0.5F) / nf - 0.5F) / k.zoom;
        for (std::uint32_t a = 0; a < n; ++a) {
            const float ua = u + ((static_cast<float>(a) + 0.5F) / nf - 0.5F) / k.zoom;
            const Rgb tap = bilinear(image, ua, vb);
            for (std::size_t c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {
                sum[c] = sum[c] + tap[c];
            }
        }
    }
    const float count = static_cast<float>(n * n);
    return {sum[0] / count, sum[1] / count, sum[2] / count};
}

std::uint8_t unorm8(float x) {
    return static_cast<std::uint8_t>(std::lround(255.0F * std::clamp(x, 0.0F, 1.0F)));
}

}  // namespace

std::array<std::uint8_t, 4> compositePixel(const CompositeConstants& k, std::uint32_t px,
                                           std::uint32_t py,
                                           const std::optional<HostDisplayImage>& image) noexcept {
    const float grey = dvv::media::srgbDecode((k.flags & COMPOSITE_DARK) != 0 ? BACKGROUND_DARK
                                                                              : BACKGROUND_LIGHT);
    Rgb colour{grey, grey, grey};
    if (image && k.imageW != 0 && k.imageH != 0 && k.zoom > 0.0F) {
        const float u = k.originX + (static_cast<float>(px) + 0.5F) / k.zoom;
        const float v = k.originY + (static_cast<float>(py) + 0.5F) / k.zoom;
        if (u >= 0.0F && v >= 0.0F && u < static_cast<float>(k.imageW) &&
            v < static_cast<float>(k.imageH)) {
            colour = sample(k, *image, u, v);
        }
    }
    const bool skip = (k.flags & COMPOSITE_SKIP_ENCODE) != 0;
    return {skip ? unorm8(colour[0]) : dvv::media::encodeSrgb8(colour[0]),
            skip ? unorm8(colour[1]) : dvv::media::encodeSrgb8(colour[1]),
            skip ? unorm8(colour[2]) : dvv::media::encodeSrgb8(colour[2]), 255};
}
```

`compositeConstants` and `compositeReference` are short and follow their header comments (adapt
`encodeSrgb8`'s return type to `std::uint8_t` with a `static_cast` if Task 2 returns another
integer type).

`src/render/display_state.cpp` (the binding part; `stats()` returns `stats_` under `mutex_`):

```cpp
void DisplayState::show(FrameSet frames) {
    FrameSet replaced;
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
        if (!read_ && frames_.count > 0) {
            ++stats_.overwritten;  // nobody read it (design E7, R13)
        }
        replaced = std::exchange(frames_, std::move(frames));
        read_ = false;
        ++version_;
    }
    replaced = FrameSet{};  // its leases are released here, outside every lock (design §5.2)
    notify();
}

void DisplayState::clear() {
    FrameSet dropped;
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
        dropped = std::exchange(frames_, FrameSet{});
        read_ = true;
        ++version_;
    }
    dropped = FrameSet{};
    notify();
}

void DisplayState::setParams(CompositeParams params) {
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
        params_ = params;
        ++version_;
    }
    notify();
}

DisplaySnapshot DisplayState::snapshot() const {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    read_ = true;
    return DisplaySnapshot{frames_, params_, version_};  // copies shared_ptrs under the lock
}

void DisplayState::notify() {
    // Never under mutex_; serialises callbacks against setOnChanged().
    std::lock_guard lock(callback_mutex_);
    if (on_changed_) {
        on_changed_();
    }
}

void DisplayState::setOnChanged(std::move_only_function<void()> callback) {
    std::move_only_function<void()> previous;
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(callback_mutex_);  // waits for a running callback
        previous = std::exchange(on_changed_, std::move(callback));
    }
}  // `previous` is destroyed here, outside the lock
```

`src/render/viewport_renderer.cpp` (the Vulkan path):

```cpp
namespace {

// The display image of a lease, or null when the lease is not a display view (design D3: one
// RGBA16F plane in GENERAL). The caller's epoch check is what separates stacks: an old and a new
// context on the same GPU share the DeviceId, and every generation is FIRST_GENERATION in v1.0
// (design v2 D7). The device/generation comparison only rejects an image of another physical device.
std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage> displayImageOf(
    const std::shared_ptr<const dvv::media::ReadLease>& lease,
    const dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext& context) {
    if (!lease || !*lease) {
        return nullptr;
    }
    const auto* binding = std::get_if<dvv::media::VkImageB>(&lease->binding());
    if (binding == nullptr || binding->planeCount != 1 || !binding->planes[0]) {
        return nullptr;
    }
    const std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage>& image = binding->planes[0];
    if (image->format() != VK_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT ||
        image->deviceId() != context.deviceId() || image->generation() != context.generation()) {
        return nullptr;
    }
    return image;
}

}  // namespace

dvv::Result<std::unique_ptr<ViewportRenderer>> ViewportRenderer::create(
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context, std::uint64_t epoch) {
    DVV_TRY(std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ComputePipeline> pipeline,
            context->createComputePipeline(
                rawreel_shaders::composite(),
                static_cast<std::uint32_t>(sizeof(CompositeConstants)),
                dvv::gpu::ComputeBindings{.storageImages = MAX_STREAMS + 1}));
    DVV_TRY(std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage> dummy,
            context->createImage({.width = 1,
                                  .height = 1,
                                  .format = VK_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT,
                                  .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT,
                                  .name = "composite dummy"}));
    return std::make_unique<ViewportRenderer>(Private{}, std::move(context), epoch,
                                              std::move(pipeline), std::move(dummy));
}

dvv::Result<std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage>> ViewportRenderer::makeOutput(
    dvv::platform::PixelSize extent) const {
    return context_->createImage(
        {.width = extent.width,
         .height = extent.height,
         .format = VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM,
         .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT | VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT,
         .name = "viewport composite"});
}

dvv::gpu::RecordFunction ViewportRenderer::recordComposite(
    const std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage>& output, dvv::platform::PixelSize extent,
    const FrameSet& frames, const CompositeParams& params, bool skip_encode) {
    std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage> image;
    if (frames.epoch == epoch_ && frames.count >= 1 && frames.count <= MAX_STREAMS) {
        image = displayImageOf(frames.views[0], *context_);  // null: background only
    }
    const dvv::platform::PixelSize image_size =
        image ? dvv::platform::PixelSize{image->width(), image->height()} : dvv::platform::PixelSize{};
    const CompositeConstants constants = compositeConstants(params, extent, image_size, skip_encode);
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(recorded_mutex_);
        recorded_ = RecordedInfo{.epoch = epoch_,
                                 .index = image ? frames.index : -1,
                                 .extent = extent,
                                 .zoom = ViewTransform::fit(extent, image_size).zoom()};
    }
    const std::uint32_t groups_x = (extent.width + 7) / 8;
    const std::uint32_t groups_y = (extent.height + 7) / 8;
    // Everything the GPU touches stays alive until the submission has completed (M1a resource
    // rule): pipeline, output, the bound image, and the FrameSet's leases, which keep the display
    // image in the store. The presenter releases the function on its own thread.
    return [pipeline = pipeline_, output, bound0 = image ? image : dummy_, leases = frames.views,
            constants, groups_x, groups_y](dvv::gpu::CommandRecorder& recorder) {
        const std::array<dvv::gpu::GpuImage*, MAX_STREAMS + 1> bound{bound0.get(), output.get()};
        recorder.dispatch(*pipeline, std::as_bytes(std::span(&constants, 1)), bound, groups_x,
                          groups_y);
    };
}

dvv::Result<dvv::gpu::PresentFrame> ViewportRenderer::frame(const dvv::gpu::PresentFrameInfo& info,
                                                            const DisplaySnapshot& snapshot) {
    const bool skip_encode = isSrgbFormat(info.format);
    if (skip_encode && !logged_srgb_) {
        logged_srgb_ = true;
        DVV_LOG_WARN("viewport: the swapchain format is sRGB; the compositor skips its own encode");
    }
    bool record = !info.previousFrameCompleted || snapshot.version != version_ ||
                  skip_encode != skip_encode_;
    if (!output_ || info.extent != extent_) {
        DVV_TRY(output_, makeOutput(info.extent));
        extent_ = info.extent;
        record = true;
    }
    if (!record) {
        return dvv::gpu::PresentFrame{.record = {}, .image = output_};
    }
    dvv::gpu::RecordFunction commands =
        recordComposite(output_, info.extent, snapshot.frames, snapshot.params, skip_encode);
    version_ = snapshot.version;
    skip_encode_ = skip_encode;
    return dvv::gpu::PresentFrame{.record = std::move(commands), .image = output_};
}

dvv::Result<dvv::gpu::PresentFrame> ViewportRenderer::render(dvv::platform::PixelSize extent,
                                                             const FrameSet& frames,
                                                             const CompositeParams& params) {
    DVV_TRY(std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::GpuImage> output, makeOutput(extent));
    dvv::gpu::RecordFunction commands = recordComposite(output, extent, frames, params, false);
    return dvv::gpu::PresentFrame{.record = std::move(commands), .image = std::move(output)};
}

RecordedInfo ViewportRenderer::lastRecorded() const {
    std::lock_guard lock(recorded_mutex_);
    return recorded_;
}
```

**Tests first:**

    `clear` each increase `snapshot().version` by 1; after `show(FrameSet{.index = 7, .count =
    1})` and `setParams({.dark = true})`, `snapshot().frames.index == 7` and
    `snapshot().params.dark`.
  - `display state: overwritten`: `show(1)`, `show(2)` → `stats().overwritten == 1`; `snapshot()`,
    `show(3)` → still 1; `clear()`, `show(4)` → still 1 (FrameSets with `count = 1`).
  - `display state: leases are released outside the state`: a FrameSet holding a
    `std::make_shared<const dvv::media::ReadLease>()` (default lease) kept only through a
    `weak_ptr`; after `show(set)` and `show(other)` the weak pointer is expired; the same after
    `show(set)` and `clear()`.
  - `display state: no callback after setOnChanged({})` (race case: Step 4's Debug ×10 and the
    Task 9 checkpoint's Windows ASan ×10 now; a TSan target once Linux returns): the callback increments an

    `show()` 20,000 times; the test thread calls `setOnChanged({})` after the counter passes 100,
    then sets `cleared`; after the join: violations 0.
- `tests/test_composite_reference.cpp` (`rawreel_tests`). Helpers in the file: `white(w, h)` and

  every RGB 1.0; columnImage: RGB 1.0 in `column`, 0.0 elsewhere; alpha 1.0); `pattern37x23()`
  sets texel (x, y) channel c to `floatToHalf(float((x·7 + y·13 + c·5) % 17) / 16)`;
  `bg(dark) = encodeSrgb8(srgbDecode(dark ? 0.12F : 0.85F))` as RGBA with alpha 255.
  - `composite constants: layout`: `offsetof` `viewportW 0, viewportH 4, imageW 8, imageH 12,
    zoom 16, originX 20, originY 24, flags 28`; `sizeof == 32`.
  - `composite shader: the push block matches CompositeConstants` (the reflection check): parse
    `rawreel_shaders::composite().reflectionJson` with `dvv::json`; the parameter with
    `binding.kind == "pushConstantBuffer"` has `type.elementVarLayout.binding.size == 32`; its
    fields (`type.elementType.fields[i].binding.offset` by name) are `viewportW 0, viewportH 4,
    imageW 8, imageH 12, zoom 16, originX 20, originY 24, flags 28`. If Slang names the JSON keys
    differently, print the reflection once and adapt the key names, never the expected offsets.
  - `composite constants: mapping`: `c = compositeConstants({.dark = true}, {211, 97}, {37, 23},

    `c.originX == float(ViewTransform::fit({211, 97}, {37, 23}).origin().x)`, `c.originY == 0.0F`,
    `std::abs(-double(c.originX) * double(c.zoom) - 27.0) < 1e-4` (the whole-pixel offset 27 of

  - `composite reference: background`: no image → pixels (0, 0) and (210, 96) of 211×97 equal

    217, 217, 255}`; with `compositePixel(compositeConstants({.dark = true}, {4, 4}, {0, 0}, true),
    0, 0, std::nullopt)` → each RGB `== lround(255 · srgbDecode(0.12F))`.
  - `composite reference: fit placement` (`white(37, 23)`): viewport 211×97, row 48: px 26 →

    column 37: py 16 → `bg(true)`, py 17 → white, py 62 → white, py 63 → `bg(true)`.
  - `composite reference: 1:1 and integer zoom` (`pattern37x23()`): viewport 37×23 → every pixel

    `columnImage(16, 16, 1)` in viewport 1×1 → RGB `== encodeSrgb8(0.125F)`.

  store->acquire({origin, 0}, displayView(*store->deviceDomain()), *env.cpu))` → `lease` as

  image, 8)` into a `std::vector<std::uint16_t>` (memcpy) → `HostDisplayImage{37, 23, host}`.
  Images: **G** = `synthetic:gradient?w=37&h=23&fmt=yuv420p&frames=1`, **N** =
  `synthetic:noise?w=37&h=23&fmt=gbrp&frames=1` (noise makes a wrong tap count fail). Helper

  for every viewport below 200 pixels). Renderer: `ViewportRenderer::create(context, 1)`;
  FrameSets `{.index = 0, .count = 1, .views = {lease}, .epoch = 1}`.
  - `compositor: matches the CPU reference` (design §8.3 goldens), each with `INFO` naming image,

    - images G and N × viewports 37×23, 211×97, 74×80, 30×19, 11×7, 1×1, dark → mismatches within

    - image N, light background, viewports 211×97 and 11×7 → within the allowance;
    - no frame (`FrameSet{}`), 211×97, dark and light → every pixel `bg(dark)` exactly;

      block at `(2x, 17 + 2y)` has four byte-identical pixels.
    - Note on the exact background bytes: Vulkan only says float→UNORM *should* round to nearest.
      NVIDIA does, so the checks stay exact. A ±1 difference on another driver is the
      spec's allowed rounding, not a defect. The 2×2 block-identity check stays exact everywhere.

**Notes:**
- Design references (m3-design-v2): D3 (display path), D23 (fit computed by the renderer from the
  extent and the image size), §4.3 (`ViewTransform::fit`, integer offsets), §4.4 (push constants,
  binding pixel rule, `RecordedInfo`/`lastRecorded()`), §5.2 (lease release outside locks), §5.3
  GUI side (provider lambda, `setOnChanged` then `requestFrame()`: Task 8), §8.3 (fit goldens incl.
  11×7), §8.4 (`lastRecorded()` checks in `player` and `lost`), R3, R8, R10, R11. v2 arbiter FIX 6
  (`lastRecorded`), FIX 7 (multi-tap), FIX 10 (skip encode); v1 arbiter LOG 13; LOG 20 (below 1:8
  still undersamples → v1.x).
- **Note (plan choice):** `n = min(4, ceil(0.5/zoom))` is computed as the smallest `n ≤ 4` with
  `n · zoom ≥ 0.5`. It is the same number, but a multiplication by a small integer is correctly
  rounded on every Vulkan device, while a GPU division may be off by an ulp and give n = 2 at
  exactly zoom 0.5 (a common 3840 → 1920 fit). Shader and reference use the same loop.
- **Note (plan choice):** background only (no frame, another epoch, an unusable view) is sent as
  `imageW = imageH = 0`, `zoom = 0`, with the 1×1 dummy at binding 0 (a pushed descriptor must
  name a valid image). `lastRecorded()` then reports `index -1`, `zoom 0`.
- **Note (plan choice):** `lastRecorded()` reports the renderer's epoch (a drawn FrameSet always
  has it), is updated by `render()` as well, and is `RecordedInfo{}` (epoch 0, index -1, extent
  0×0, zoom 0) before the first record. `RecordedInfo` gets default initialisers and a defaulted
  `operator==` for the tests. The renderer had no lock, so `recorded_mutex_` is new; it guards only
  `recorded_`.
- **Note (plan choice):** the background is defined by its sRGB-encoded grey (0.12 / 0.85), so
  the shader decodes it first and the final encode returns 31 / 217 (design §4.4 literally).
- **Note (plan choice):** the DisplayState and reference unit cases live in `rawreel_tests`
  (`rawreel.unit`), the unit executable that links `rawreel_render`, because `rawreel_core_tests`
  does not link it; design §8.1 lists the DisplayState TSan case in the core row. `rawreel.unit`
  runs in `vs2026` and `vs2026-asan` (U-WIN; it also runs under TSan once Linux returns).
- **Note (plan choice):** the goldens use two store-made 37×23 images: the gradient of design §8.3
  and a gbrp noise image, because a smooth gradient could hide a wrong tap count within ±1. The
  CPU case `multi-tap downscale` fails exactly for a plain bilinear.
- The display lease is delivered after its converter's submission completed (M2 D10, kept by
  Task 3), so the composite needs no extra wait; the M2 dispatch moves bound images to `GENERAL`.
  If `sync` validation reports a hazard on the display image, report it to the arbiter: the fix
  belongs in the dvv-gpu recorder's storage-image barriers, not in the renderer.
- Tie robustness: the test viewports put no pixel centre or tap near an integer texel boundary
  (211×97: the nearest `u` is 1/194 away from an integer; 74×80 uses zoom 2 with exact floats),
  so only driver `pow`/division differences remain; the 0.5 % allowance covers those (0 pixels for
  viewports below 200 pixels).
- The record function captures the FrameSet's leases; after a device loss the presenter thread
  may release the last lease of the old store (R10). `ViewportRenderer` lives in namespace
  `rawreel` and is not a managed `dvv::` type (spec §9.4 does not apply); it keeps the
  `Private`-key constructor of the old plan so it is made only through `create()`.

---

### Task 8: GUI core: App, Session bridge, viewport lifecycle

**Goal:** Turn the M1b `rawreel-gui` into the Session's front end: the new argument parser
(`gui_arguments`, wx-free, in `rawreel_core`; at most one URI), App startup with appearance,
rotating log file, GPU choice and the Session, the `SessionBridge` (`ISessionEvents` →
`CallAfter`), the viewport's `DisplayState`/renderer/presenter lifecycle of design v2 §5.3 (GUI
side: a lost device is reported to the Session, any other presenter failure goes to a failure hook
for the Reopen path of D25, nothing is recreated automatically) and the shutdown order of §5.5,
the selftest runner with its `smoke` scenario, and the removal of the M1b test pattern.
`rawreel-gui --selftest smoke` passes on Windows (NVIDIA). On Linux, rawreel-gui builds only
through the M1b stub (`src/native_window_stub.cpp`, moved to `src/gui/viewport_stub.cpp`) and the
plain `#else` `main` of M1b Task 12a; nothing is run there (U-WIN, U-WIN-M1b). The GTK viewport,
`XInitThreads`, `GDK_BACKEND=x11` and the `DISPLAY` check are v1.x (future.md). The Task 9
checkpoint's `ninja-clang` build compiles the stub and the Linux `main` (U-PROC).
