- Produces (`src/core/gui_arguments.h`, complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

// rawreel-gui's command line (design M3 §4.5), wx-free so the unit tests and sanitizer builds use
// it. main() parses before wxEntry().

#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

#include "dvv/base/error.h"

namespace rawreel {

namespace exit_code {
inline constexpr int SUCCESS = 0;
inline constexpr int BAD_ARGUMENTS = 1;    // also: the GUI could not start, an unexpected exception
inline constexpr int GPU_ERROR = 4;        // no loader, no usable device, presenter failure
inline constexpr int SELFTEST_FAILED = 6;  // a --selftest check failed (spec §10.2)
inline constexpr int SKIPPED = 77;         // --selftest only: no usable device or no display
}  // namespace exit_code

enum class Appearance : std::uint8_t { System, Light, Dark };
enum class Scenario : std::uint8_t { None, Smoke, Player, Lost, Perf };
[[nodiscard]] std::string_view scenarioName(Scenario scenario) noexcept;  // "smoke", ...; "" for None

struct GuiArguments {
    Appearance appearance = Appearance::System;
    std::optional<std::uint32_t> gpu;  // index into GpuRegistry::devices() (dvv-gpu-info order)
    Scenario selftest = Scenario::None;
    std::uint32_t inflight = 4;        // --inflight K, 1..64, only with --selftest perf
    std::optional<std::string> uri;    // positional, at most one (opened as the stream, frame 0)
    bool help = false;
};
// Options take their value in the next argument. Every error is Errc::Format with one of:
// "unknown argument '<a>'" (an unknown --option), "option --x needs a value", "option --x given
// twice", "bad value '<v>' for --appearance (system, light, dark)", "bad value '<v>' for --selftest
// (smoke, player, lost, perf)", "bad number '<v>' for --gpu", "bad number '<v>' for
// --inflight (1 to 64)", "--inflight is valid only with --selftest perf", "at most one URI".
// "--help" anywhere sets help and stops checking.
[[nodiscard]] dvv::Result<GuiArguments> parseGuiArguments(std::span<const std::string_view> args);
// 77, or 4 when DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1 turns a skip into a failure (M1b D12).
[[nodiscard]] int skipExitCode();

inline constexpr char USAGE[] =
    R"(Usage: rawreel-gui [--appearance system|light|dark] [--gpu N] [--selftest SCENARIO]
                   [--inflight K] [URI]

  --appearance  light or dark windows, or the system setting (default)
  --gpu N       use GPU N (the index dvv-gpu-info lists) instead of the best one
  --selftest S  run a scripted check and exit: smoke, player, lost or perf
  --inflight K  frames in flight for --selftest perf (default 4)
  --help        print this text
  URI           a source to open (a path, file:..., synthetic:...)

Exit codes: 0 success; 1 bad arguments, or the GUI could not start; 4 GPU error (no Vulkan
loader, no usable device, presenter failure); 6 a --selftest check failed; 77 (--selftest only)
no usable device or no display, unless DVV_REQUIRE_GPU=1 (then 4).
)";

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Produces (`src/gui/session_bridge.h`, complete):

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <wx/wx.h>  // first (M1b R11)

#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "core/session_types.h"

namespace rawreel {

class MainFrame;

// ISessionEvents for the GUI (design D10): every event is queued onto the frame with CallAfter and
// handled on the GUI thread. Called on the Session executor; never blocks. Event lambdas carry
// values and weak_ptrs only, never a strong reference to a managed object.
class SessionBridge final : public ISessionEvents {
public:
    explicit SessionBridge(MainFrame& frame) noexcept;
    // GUI thread, before the frame dies: afterwards no event is queued (already queued ones die
    // with the frame's pending events).
    void detach();

    void streamsChanged(std::vector<StreamInfo> streams) override;
    void formatNeeded(std::uint32_t slot, dvv::media::SourceRequest request,
                      dvv::media::Ambiguous candidates, std::string reason) override;
    void stackChanged(std::weak_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context,
                      std::uint64_t epoch) override;
    void error(dvv::Error error) override;

private:
    template <class F>
    void forward(F handler);  // F: copyable callable taking MainFrame& (wx copies the functor)

    std::mutex mutex_;
    MainFrame* frame_;
};

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Produces (`src/gui/viewport.h`, the public part; private members are the implementer's):

```cpp
namespace rawreel {
// The viewport (M1b §5.6 plus design M3 v2 §5.3 GUI side): a native child window that shows the
// DisplayState through one ViewportRenderer and one Presenter of the current stack.
class Viewport final : public wxWindow {
public:
    // Sets display->setParams(CompositeParams{.dark = wxSystemSettings::GetAppearance().IsDark()})
    // once (D23: the renderer fits; the GUI never refits).
    Viewport(wxWindow* parent, std::shared_ptr<DisplayState> display);
    ~Viewport() override;  // detachStack() first (binding, M1b §5.6)

    // ISessionEvents::stackChanged on the GUI thread: drops presenter and renderer, keeps the
    // context of a live pointer and starts presenting when ready.
    void setStack(std::weak_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context, std::uint64_t epoch);
    void markReady();      // CallAfter once the top-level frame is shown (M1b startPresenting)
    void stopPresenting(); // DisplayState::setOnChanged({}), presenter, renderer: in this order
    void detachStack();    // stopPresenting() and release the context (frame close, §5.5)
    // Called with the presenter's epoch when it fails with DeviceLost (MainFrame: reportDeviceLost).
    void setDeviceLostHandler(std::function<void(std::uint64_t)> handler);
    // D25: called once with the failure when the presenter fails with an error other than
    // DeviceLost, or when renderer or presenter creation fails with such an error. Nothing is
    // recreated; MainFrame::onPresenterFailed logs it (Task 9: infobar with Reopen).
    void setFailureHandler(std::function<void(const dvv::Error&)> handler);
    void requestFrame();   // presenter->requestFrame() when there is one
    void topLevelIconized(bool iconized);
    void topLevelShown(bool shown);

    [[nodiscard]] dvv::gpu::Presenter* presenter() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t epoch() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] dvv::platform::PixelSize physicalClientSize() const;
    [[nodiscard]] const std::shared_ptr<DisplayState>& display() const noexcept;
};
}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Produces (`src/gui/selftest.h`, the runner used by Tasks 9-11a and M3.1 Task 11b):

```cpp
namespace rawreel {
class App;
class MainFrame;

struct SelfTestStep {
    std::string name;
    std::move_only_function<void()> start;                    // once, when the step begins (may be empty)
    std::move_only_function<bool(std::string& seen)> passed;  // polled every tick; `seen` explains a miss
    std::chrono::milliseconds deadline{5000};
};
struct SelfTestScript {
    std::vector<SelfTestStep> steps;
    bool presenterFailureFails = true;  // lost: false
};
class SelfTest {
public:
    SelfTest(MainFrame& frame, App& app);
    // selfTestScript(scenario, *this); nullopt -> App::finish(6, "rawreel-gui --selftest: scenario
    // <name> is not available").
    void start(Scenario scenario);
    void stop() noexcept;  // stops the timer; the object lives until ~MainFrame
    [[nodiscard]] MainFrame& frame() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] App& app() const noexcept;
};
// The dispatcher: Smoke -> {smokeScenario(test)}; Tasks 9 and 10 add Lost
// ({lostScenario(test), false}) and Player, M3.1 Task 11b adds Perf; every other scenario -> nullopt.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<SelfTestScript> selfTestScript(Scenario scenario, SelfTest& test);
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<SelfTestStep> smokeScenario(SelfTest& test);
}  // namespace rawreel
```

`MainFrame::startSelfTest(Scenario)` creates `selftest_ = std::make_unique<SelfTest>(*this,
wxGetApp())` and calls `start(scenario)`.

- App (M1b class, extended): `setArguments(GuiArguments)`, `arguments()`, `exitCode()`,
  `finish(code, message)`, `session()`, `registry()`, `display()`, `appearanceResult()`,
  `shutdownSession()`, `components()`, `cpu()`, `io()` (`const std::shared_ptr<…>&` of the objects

  `onStackChanged`, `onStreamsChanged`, `onFormatNeeded`, `onError` and `onPresenterFailed(dvv::Error)`
  (GUI thread; Tasks 9 and 10 fill them in).
- CMake: `rawreel_core` gains `src/core/gui_arguments.cpp`; `rawreel_render` loses
  `src/arguments.cpp`, `src/test_pattern*.cpp`; `dvv_add_shaders(rawreel_render NAMESPACE

  (`viewport_msw.cpp` + `.rc` on Windows, the stub `viewport_stub.cpp` elsewhere), linked to `rawreel_render

```cmake
# Windows only (U-WIN, M1b Task 12a): outside Windows the viewport is the stub.
if(TARGET rawreel_gui AND WIN32)
    add_test(NAME rawreel.gui_selftest.smoke
             COMMAND rawreel_gui --selftest smoke --appearance dark)
    set_tests_properties(rawreel.gui_selftest.smoke PROPERTIES
        LABELS "gpu;display;gui"
```

**Behaviour:**
- **main** (M1b, changed): parses with `parseGuiArguments` (bad → `rawreel-gui: <message>` +
  `USAGE` on stderr, exit 1; `--help` → `USAGE` on stdout, exit 0); the Linux `#else` branch stays
  M1b's plain `main(int, char**)` calling the same code with the new parser (compiling, not run;
  `XInitThreads`, `GDK_BACKEND=x11` and the `DISPLAY` check are v1.x, U-WIN).
- **App::OnInit** (binding order; M1b failures and exit codes unchanged):
  1. `SetAppearance(System|Light|Dark)` from `--appearance` before any window; the result is kept
     (`appearanceResult()`) and logged at Info (`appearance: dark -> Ok`); `Failure` and
     `CannotChange` are not errors (design §7).
  2. Log file: `paths::userDataDir("rawreel")`, `ensureDirectory`, `RotatingFileSink::open(dir /
     "rawreel.log", 4 MiB, 3)`, `Logger::addSink`; a failure is a warning (spec §11).
  3. Pools: `cpu = makeThreadPool(0)`, `io = makeThreadPool(4)`.
  4. GPU (M1b `startGpu`, changed): `GpuRegistry::create({.applicationName = "rawreel",
     .windowSystem = defaultWindowSystem()})`. `--gpu N`: `N >= devices().size()` → stderr
     `rawreel-gui: GPU N does not exist (<m> devices)`, exit 1; else device `DeviceId(N)`. Without
     `--gpu`: no ranked usable device with `optional.swapchain` → "no usable device" (selftest
     `skipExitCode()`, else 4 with the M1b dialog). Other registry failures → 4.
  5. `components = ComponentRegistry::create(Capabilities{})` (shared).
  6. `display = make_shared<DisplayState>()`; `new MainFrame(display)` (not shown yet); `bridge =
     make_shared<SessionBridge>(*frame)`.
  7. `Session::create({components, cpu, io, gpus = registry, device, presentation = true, inflight
     = arguments.inflight, steadyClock(), makeThreadTimer(), sink = display, events = bridge})`;
     a failure → `Vulkan could not start: <describe>` (stderr; dialog unless selftest), the frame
     is destroyed, exit 4 (`--gpu N` that cannot present: also 4).
  8. `frame->viewport().setDeviceLostHandler([](std::uint64_t epoch) { if (auto* s =
     wxGetApp().session()) s->reportDeviceLost(epoch); })`;
     `frame->viewport().setFailureHandler([frame](const dvv::Error& e) { frame->onPresenterFailed(e); })`;
     `frame->Show()`; `frame->viewport().CallAfter([v = &frame->viewport()] { v->markReady(); })`.
  9. URI: `parseUri(*uri)` → `session->open(0, SourceRequest{*uri})` (no start: frame 0); a parse
     error goes to the frame's `onError` (log; Task 9: infobar).
  10. `selftest != None` → `frame->startSelfTest(scenario)`; a scenario without a script yet →
      `finish(6, "rawreel-gui --selftest: scenario <name> is not available")`.
- **App::shutdownSession()** (called by `MainFrame::onClose`): `bridge->detach()`, then
  `syncWait(session->shutdown())`, `session.reset()`. Nothing it waits for needs the GUI thread
  (no presenter exists any more, events are only queued).
- **App::OnExit:** `shutdownSession()` if a Session still exists; `bridge`, `display`,
  `components` released; with `--selftest` and no code set yet: `validationMessageCount() != 0` →
  code 6 `rawreel-gui --selftest: <scenario> (close) failed: <n> validation messages`, else 0 and
  `rawreel-gui --selftest: passed`; then `registry`, `io`, `cpu` released, `Logger::flush()`,
  `wxApp::OnExit()`. `main` returns `finishWithLeakCheck(code)` as in M1b.
- **MainFrame (Task 8 part):** M1b frame (title `rawreel`, client `FromDIP(960, 540)`, one child
  `Viewport`, ICONIZE/SHOW forwarded to the viewport). Event handlers: `onStackChanged` →
  `viewport->setStack`; `onStreamsChanged` → one Info log line per stream (`stream <k>:
  <resolvedUri>`; no viewport call: the renderer fits from the display image, D23); `onError` →
  `DVV_LOG_WARN("{}", describe)`; `onFormatNeeded` → one log line (Task 10 adds the dialog);
  `onPresenterFailed(error)` → `DVV_LOG_ERROR("viewport: {}", describe)` (Task 9 adds the infobar
  with Reopen, D25). `onClose`: Code below. `MainFrame` gets `bool closing_ = false;` (binding):
  every `on*` Session handler, `onPresenterFailed`, the status-timer handler (Task 9), `execute`,
  `onCharHook` and `openFiles` (Task 10) return at once when `closing_` is set. `selftest_`
  (unique_ptr) is destroyed in `~MainFrame` (after `aui_.UnInit()` in Task 9); the close step is
  terminal: the runner sets `finished_ = true` and stops its timer before it calls
  `frame().Close()`, and touches no member after `Close()` returns.
- **Viewport** (Code below): the constructor sets `CompositeParams{.dark =
  wxSystemSettings::GetAppearance().IsDark()}` once; size changes only resize the presenter (M1b)
  and never touch the params (D23). Without a presenter (no stack, a lost device, a failed
  presenter, before `markReady`), the paint handler fills the client area with the compositor's
  background grey: `wxPaintDC dc(this); dc.SetBackground(wxBrush(dark ? wxColour(31, 31, 31) :
  wxColour(217, 217, 217))); dc.Clear();` (31 and 217 are Task 7's dark 0.12 and light 0.85
  sRGB-encoded greys in 8 bits); with a presenter the M1b paint rule stays. After
  `display_->setOnChanged(...)` the viewport calls `presenter_->requestFrame()` once, so a paused
  stream (e.g. after Reopen) is presented without waiting for a change. Presenter `onError`
  (presenter thread) → `CallAfter` (try/catch as M1b) → `onPresenterError(serial, error)`: a report
  of an older presenter (serial) is ignored; without `failure()` (a skipped frame) it is only
  logged; with `failure()` set: `DeviceLost` (in the error or in `failure()`) → the device-lost
  handler with the presenter's epoch; any other failure → `stopPresenting()`, `Refresh()` and the
  failure handler once (D25: no automatic recreation; Reopen rebuilds the stack). A renderer or
  presenter creation error: `DeviceLost` → the device-lost handler with the current epoch; any
  other → the failure handler.
- **SessionBridge:** each event → `forward([values](MainFrame& f) { f.onX(values); })`;
  `forward` takes the mutex, returns when detached, else `frame_->CallAfter([frame = frame_,
  handler] { handler(*frame); })` inside `try` (a `CallAfter` failure is logged).
- **Selftest runner:** A 50 ms `wxTimer`. Each tick: `busy_` or `finished_` → return.
  `viewport().requestFrame()`. When `presenterFailureFails` and the presenter has `failure()` →
  `finish(4, "rawreel-gui --selftest: the presenter failed: <describe>")`. Entering a step:
  `busy_ = true; start(); busy_ = false;` and record its start time. Every tick: `busy_ = true; ok =
  passed(seen); busy_ = false;`; pass → next step; a deadline miss → `finish(6, "rawreel-gui
  --selftest: <scenario> step <k> (<name>) failed: <seen>")`. After the last step the runner's own
  close step runs once: `finished_ = true`, stop the timer; when `presenterFailureFails` and
  `failure()` is set → `finish(4, …)`; else `frame().Close()` and return at once (no member access
  afterwards). `OnExit` checks validation, `main` the leak check (as now).
- **smoke script** (design §8.4; M1b steps kept):
  1. `first frames` (empty Session): a presenter exists, `framesPresented >= 2`,
     `presentedExtent == physicalClientSize()`.
  2. `resize 800x600`, 3. `resize 333x177`: `SetClientSize` in logical pixels; pass as M1b
     (extent equal, `swapchainsCreated` grew).
  4. `hide` (Windows `Iconize(true)`, Linux `Hide()`), 5. `restore`: as M1b.
  6. `open stream`: start `session->open(0, synthetic:gradient?w=64&h=48&fmt=yuv420p10le&frames=40)`;
     pass when `status().streams == 1`, the DisplayState snapshot has `frames.count == 1`,
     `frames.index == 0`, `frames.epoch == viewport.epoch()`, and `framesPresented` grew after that
     snapshot was first seen.
  7. `resize with a stream`: `SetClientSize(640, 360)` → presented extent equals the new physical
     size.
  8. `appearance`: pass at once for `System`; else `appearanceResult()` is set and, on Windows,
     `Ok` implies `wxSystemSettings::GetAppearance().IsDark() == (appearance == Dark)`.

**Code** (binding: the GUI side of §5.3 and the order of §5.5).

`src/gui/viewport.cpp`, the lifecycle (the M1b size/iconize handlers stay; `onSize` only resizes
the presenter; the paint handler adds the background fill without a presenter):

```cpp
Viewport::~Viewport() {
    detachStack();  // the presenter thread is joined while the native window still exists
}

void Viewport::setStack(std::weak_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context, std::uint64_t epoch) {
    stopPresenting();  // the old presenter and renderer hold the old context
    context_ = context.lock();  // GUI-held while current; released here on the next change
    epoch_ = epoch;
    if (!context_) {
        Refresh();  // no presenter: the paint handler fills the background
        return;
    }
    startPresenting();
}

void Viewport::markReady() {
    ready_ = true;
    startPresenting();
}

void Viewport::startPresenting() {
    if (!ready_ || !context_ || presenter_) {
        return;
    }
    const dvv::platform::NativeWindow window = nativeWindowOf(*this);
    if (std::holds_alternative<std::monostate>(window)) {
        reportFailure(dvv::makeError(dvv::Errc::Internal,
                                     "the viewport has no native Win32 or X11 window").error());
        return;
    }
    auto renderer = ViewportRenderer::create(context_, epoch_);
    if (!renderer) {
        reportFailure(renderer.error());
        return;
    }
    renderer_ = std::move(*renderer);
    const std::uint64_t serial = ++serial_;
    auto presenter = context_->createPresenter(
        {.window = window,
         .extent = physicalClientSize(),
         .visible = IsShownOnScreen(),
         .frames = [display = display_, renderer = renderer_.get()](
                       const dvv::gpu::PresentFrameInfo& info) {
             // Presenter thread: one consistent read of the state, then record (design §5.3).
             const DisplaySnapshot snapshot = display->snapshot();
             return renderer->frame(info, snapshot);
         },
         .onError =
             [this, serial](const dvv::Error& error) noexcept {
                 // Presenter thread. CallAfter allocates, so it may throw; the handler may not.
                 try {
                     CallAfter([this, serial, error] { onPresenterError(serial, error); });
                 } catch (...) {
                     std::fputs("rawreel-gui: a presenter error could not reach the GUI\n", stderr);
                 }
             },
         .name = "viewport"});
    if (!presenter) {
        renderer_.reset();
        reportFailure(presenter.error());
        return;
    }
    presenter_ = std::move(*presenter);
    // Safe with a raw pointer: stopPresenting() clears the callback before the presenter dies,
    // and after setOnChanged({}) returns no callback runs (DisplayState binding rule).
    dvv::gpu::Presenter* raw = presenter_.get();
    display_->setOnChanged([raw] { raw->requestFrame(); });
    presenter_->requestFrame();  // once: a paused frame is presented without a change (§5.3)
}

// Creation errors: DeviceLost goes to the Session, anything else to the D25 failure hook.
void Viewport::reportFailure(const dvv::Error& error) {
    DVV_LOG_ERROR("viewport: {}", error.describe());
    if (error.code == dvv::Errc::DeviceLost) {
        if (device_lost_) {
            device_lost_(epoch_);
        }
    } else if (failure_) {
        failure_(error);
    }
    Refresh();
}

void Viewport::stopPresenting() {
    display_->setOnChanged({});  // first (M1b order): no callback can reach the presenter after this
    presenter_.reset();          // joins the presenter thread (about one frame)
    renderer_.reset();           // after the presenter: its provider used the renderer
}

void Viewport::detachStack() {
    stopPresenting();
    context_.reset();
}

void Viewport::onPresenterError(std::uint64_t serial, const dvv::Error& error) {
    if (serial != serial_) {
        return;  // an older presenter's report
    }
    DVV_LOG_ERROR("viewport: {}", error.describe());
    const std::optional<dvv::Error> failure = presenter_ ? presenter_->failure() : std::nullopt;
    if (!failure) {
        return;  // a skipped frame; the presenter goes on
    }
    if (failure->code == dvv::Errc::DeviceLost || error.code == dvv::Errc::DeviceLost) {
        if (device_lost_) {
            device_lost_(epoch_);  // the Session releases the stack and sends stackChanged({})
        }
        return;
    }
    // D25: M1b Failed is terminal and is not recreated; the user's Reopen rebuilds the stack.
    stopPresenting();
    Refresh();
    if (failure_) {
        failure_(*failure);
    }
}
```

`src/gui/main_frame.cpp`, close (design §5.5, binding order):

```cpp
void MainFrame::onClose(wxCloseEvent& event) {
    if (closing_) {
        event.Skip();
        return;
    }
    closing_ = true;
    if (selftest_) {
        selftest_->stop();  // the runner lives until ~MainFrame
    }
    if (viewport_ != nullptr) {
        viewport_->detachStack();  // setOnChanged({}), presenter, renderer, context
    }
    if (const std::shared_ptr<DisplayState>& display = wxGetApp().display()) {
        display->clear();  // drops the last FrameSet's leases on the GUI thread
    }
    wxGetApp().shutdownSession();  // bridge detached, syncWait(session->shutdown()), Session gone
    if (viewport_ != nullptr) {
        viewport_->Destroy();  // a child's Destroy() deletes it now
        viewport_ = nullptr;
    }
    event.Skip();  // wx destroys the frame
}
```

`src/gui/session_bridge.cpp`, the forwarder:

```cpp
template <class F>
void SessionBridge::forward(F handler) {
    std::lock_guard lock(mutex_);
    if (frame_ == nullptr) {
        return;  // detached: the frame is closing
    }
    try {
        MainFrame* frame = frame_;
        // wx copies the functor; F holds values and weak_ptrs only (design D10).
        frame->CallAfter([frame, handler] { handler(*frame); });
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        DVV_LOG_ERROR("session event lost: {}", error.what());
    }
}
```

### Task 9: GUI shell: AUI frame, menus, status bar, Reopen

**Goal:** Give `rawreel-gui` its shell (design D17, D18, D20): a wxAUI frame with the fixed centre
player panel (infobar above the viewport) and the Info and Log panes, menus and accelerators
generated from the reduced `commandTable()`, `MainFrame::execute(Command)` for the transport and
help commands, the 50 ms status timer with the three D20 fields, the infobar for Session errors,
Help → GPU info and Diagnostics, and the GUI end of the device-lost path: `execute(Command::Reopen)`,
the infobar's Reopen button and the `lost` selftest (design v2 D7, D25, §8.4). The `smoke`
scenario grows checks for the shell; `smoke` and `lost` pass on Windows (U-WIN: no WSLg run).

```cpp
#pragma once

// The status bar fields of design D20, wx-free.

#include <string>

#include "core/session_types.h"

namespace rawreel {

struct StatusFields {
    std::string position;    // "frame 123 / 1000  00:00:04.920"; unbounded: "frame 123  00:00:04.920"
    std::string fps;         // "60.0 / 59.8 fps" (target / actual)
    std::string bottleneck;  // "bottleneck: disk" when the Session names one, else ""
};
// Without streams every field is empty. The time is position / targetFps as HH:MM:SS.mmm (left
// out when targetFps <= 0).
[[nodiscard]] StatusFields statusFields(const SessionStatus& status);

}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Produces (`src/gui/main_frame.h`, the public part used by Tasks 10-11b and the selftests; the
  Reopen section below adds `INFOBAR_REOPEN_ID`, `offerReopen`, `reopenShown`, `currentContext`):

```cpp
namespace rawreel {
struct InfoButton {
    int id;          // wx id of the infobar button (wxEVT_BUTTON on the infobar)
    wxString label;  // e.g. "Reopen" (the Reopen section), "Format…" (Task 10)
};
inline constexpr int COMMAND_ID_BASE = wxID_HIGHEST + 100;  // menu id = base + int(command)

class MainFrame final : public wxFrame {
public:
    explicit MainFrame(std::shared_ptr<DisplayState> display);
    ~MainFrame() override;  // aui_.UnInit(); selftest_ is destroyed after it

    void execute(Command command);                  // the menu/hotkey path (design §8.4)
    [[nodiscard]] bool isAvailable(Command command) const;
    // Infobar (design §7): the latest message, an icon (wxICON_*), optional buttons.
    void showMessage(const wxString& text, int icon, std::span<const InfoButton> buttons = {});
    void dismissMessage();
    [[nodiscard]] const wxString& lastMessage() const noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] std::array<wxString, 3> statusTexts() const;

    [[nodiscard]] Viewport& viewport() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] PlayerPanel& playerPanel() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] InfoPanel& infoPanel() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] LogPanel& logPanel() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] wxAuiManager& aui() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] Session& session() noexcept;  // valid until onClose; closing_ guards the rest
    void startSelfTest(Scenario scenario);

    // Session events on the GUI thread (SessionBridge), as Task 8.
    void onStackChanged(std::weak_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context, std::uint64_t epoch);
    void onStreamsChanged(std::vector<StreamInfo> streams);
    void onFormatNeeded(std::uint32_t slot, dvv::media::SourceRequest request,
                        dvv::media::Ambiguous candidates, std::string reason);
    void onError(dvv::Error error);
    void onPresenterFailed(dvv::Error error);  // Task 8's D25 hook (Viewport::setFailureHandler)
};
}  // namespace rawreel
```

- Produces (`src/gui/log_panel.h`):

```cpp
namespace rawreel {
// Keeps the formatted log lines in memory for the Log pane (design D18). It never refers to the
// panel, so it stays valid in the Logger after the panel is gone; it keeps at most 5000 lines.
class LogPanelSink final : public dvv::ILogSink {
public:
    void write(const dvv::LogRecord& record, std::string_view line) override;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> takeLines();  // and empties the buffer
private:
    std::mutex mutex_;
    std::deque<std::string> lines_;
};
// A read-only wxTextCtrl fed from the sink by a 100 ms timer, capped at 5000 lines.
class LogPanel final : public wxPanel {
public:
    LogPanel(wxWindow* parent, std::shared_ptr<LogPanelSink> sink);
    [[nodiscard]] wxString text() const;  // selftests
};
}  // namespace rawreel
```

- `InfoPanel`: read-only multi-line `wxTextCtrl`; `void setText(const std::string& text);
  [[nodiscard]] wxString text() const;`. `MainFrame` sets the streams text; Task 10 replaces it
  with `formatInfo`.
- `PlayerPanel`: `wxPanel` with a vertical `wxBoxSizer`: the `wxInfoBarGeneric` (top), the
  `Viewport` (proportion 1, `wxEXPAND`); accessors `wxInfoBarGeneric& infoBar() noexcept;
  Viewport& viewport() noexcept;`; the constructor creates `new wxInfoBarGeneric(this)`
  (`<wx/generic/infobar.h>`); `MainFrame::showMessage` uses it; Task 10 appends the transport bar.
- `App` (Task 8) gains `logSink()` (the `shared_ptr<LogPanelSink>`).

**Behaviour:**
- **Layout (D18):** `wxAuiManager aui_` manages the frame. Panes: `player` =
  `CenterPane().PaneBorder(false)` (never floats, so the viewport's native child is never
  re-parented; no border, arbiter LOG 21); `info` = `Caption("Info").Right()
  .BestSize(FromDIP(wxSize(320, 400))).CloseButton(false)`; `log` = `Caption("Log").Bottom()
  .BestSize(FromDIP(wxSize(800, 160))).CloseButton(false)`. `aui_.Update()` after adding them;
  `aui_.UnInit()` in `~MainFrame`. The frame's client size stays `FromDIP(960, 540)` plus the panes
  (`SetClientSize(FromDIP(wxSize(1280, 720)))`).
- **Infobar:** `infoBar->SetShowHideEffects(wxSHOW_EFFECT_NONE, wxSHOW_EFFECT_NONE)` (no slide
  animation: it would resize the swapchain on every step, arbiter LOG 27). `showMessage` removes the
  previous custom buttons, adds `buttons` (`AddButton(id, label)`), shows the text with the icon,
  and stores it in `lastMessage()`; `dismissMessage()` hides it.
- **Menus (D17):** for each `CommandInfo` of `commandTable()` in table order: the menu named
  `info.menu` (created on first use, in first-use order, appended to the menu bar), item id
  `COMMAND_ID_BASE + int(command)`, label `wxString::FromUTF8(menuLabel(info))` (an Accelerator
  command's `\t<key>` makes wx register the accelerator; CharHook commands show their key without
  `\t`), help `info.help`. `wxEVT_MENU` over the id range → `execute(command)`;
  `wxEVT_UPDATE_UI` over the range → `event.Enable(isAvailable(command))`. The CHAR_HOOK dispatch
  of bare keys is Task 10.
- **execute / isAvailable (this task):** available and executed here:
  - `Quit` → `Close()`.
  - With at least one stream (`session->status().streams > 0`): `PlayPause` → `togglePlay()`,
    `Stop` → `stop()`, `StepBack`/`StepForward` → `step(∓1)`, `StepBack10`/`StepForward10` →
    `step(∓10)`, `First` → `seek(0)`, `Last` → `seek(length − 1)` (only with a length), `Loop` →
    toggles the frame's loop flag and calls `setLoop(flag)`. This is the only definition (Task 10
    refers to it). The flag mirrors the Session's `loop_`, which also survives stream changes, and
    does not depend on the 50 ms status snapshot or on whether the stream is bounded.
  - `CloseStreams` (with a stream), `GpuInfo`, `Diagnostics` (`session->logDiagnostics()`).
  - `Reopen`: the Reopen section below.
  - Every other command (`Open`, `OpenUri`, `Format`): `isAvailable` false (disabled menu item)
    and `execute` logs at Debug and returns; Task 10 extends both switches.
- **GPU info:** `toJson(context->deviceInfo())` as 2-space indented JSON text (as `dvv-gpu-info
  --json` prints it) of the current stack's context (the frame keeps the `weak_ptr` from
  `onStackChanged` in member `context_`; the Reopen section uses it); no context → "no GPU device
  is in use". Shown in a modal `wxDialog` with a
  read-only multi-line `wxTextCtrl` and OK; with `--selftest` the text goes to the log at Info
  instead (no modal dialog in scripts).
- **Status bar (D20):** `CreateStatusBar(3)`, widths `{-3, -2, -2}`; a 50 ms `wxTimer` sets
  the fields from `statusFields(session->status())` (only when a text changed).
- **Session events:** `onError` → `DVV_LOG_WARN("{}", describe)` + `showMessage(describe,
  wxICON_WARNING)` (the Reopen section refines it: `DeviceLost`, or any error while no stack is
  installed, offers Reopen); `onStreamsChanged` → Task 8's log lines, Info pane streams text (one
  line per stream: `<k>: <resolvedUri>`; Task 10 replaces it with `formatInfo`), and each source
  warning → `showMessage("<resolvedUri>: <warning>", wxICON_INFORMATION)`; `onFormatNeeded` →
  `showMessage(reason, wxICON_INFORMATION)` (Task 10 opens the format dialog);
  `onPresenterFailed` → the Reopen section (D25).
- **Log pane:** `LogPanelSink` added to the Logger in `App::OnInit` step 2 (before the Session
  exists); the panel's timer appends `takeLines()` each 100 ms; more than 5000 lines → the oldest
  are removed (`Remove(0, XYToPosition(0, excess))`).
- **smoke additions** (after step 7 `resize with a stream`, before `appearance`):
  - `panes`: `aui().GetPane("info").IsShown()`, `GetPane("log").IsShown()`,
    `GetPane("player").IsOk()` and it is the centre pane; the viewport is a child of the player
    panel.
  - `menu transport`: start `execute(Command::PlayPause)`; pass when the position ≥ 3; then
    `execute(Command::PlayPause)` → not playing (next step `pause`, 5 s).
  - `status bar`: `statusTexts()[0]` starts with `frame `, `[1]` ends with `fps`.
  - `diagnostics`: `execute(Command::Diagnostics)` → `logPanel().text()` contains `store: frames`.
  - `menus` (design §8.4 "menus" and "Help items"; arbiter plan-v2 FIX 10):
    - `GetMenuBar()->GetMenuCount() == 4`, and `GetMenuLabelText(0..3)` are `File`, `Playback`,
      `View`, `Help`;
    - `FindItem(COMMAND_ID_BASE + int(Command::Open))` has `GetItemLabel() ==
      wxString::FromUTF8(menuLabel(<Open's CommandInfo>))`;
    - the View menu (`GetMenu(2)`) holds `COMMAND_ID_BASE + int(Command::Reopen)`;
    - `execute(Command::GpuInfo)` in selftest mode → `logPanel().text()` grows and contains the
      second line (trimmed; its first key) of `toJson(ctx->deviceInfo())`, with `ctx` taken from
      `currentContext()` in the check.
    (No enabled/disabled check: Task 10 enables Open, OpenUri and Format.)
  - `infobar`: start `session->open(1, synthetic:gradient?w=64&h=48&fmt=yuv420p&frames=4)` (slot
    1, refused in v1.0); pass when the infobar `IsShown()` and `lastMessage()` contains
    `one stream in v1.0`; then `dismissMessage()` → not shown; still one stream.

**Code** (the AUI and infobar setup, binding for the layout rules above):

```cpp
MainFrame::MainFrame(std::shared_ptr<DisplayState> display)
    : wxFrame(nullptr, wxID_ANY, "rawreel") {
    aui_.SetManagedWindow(this);
    player_ = new PlayerPanel(this, std::move(display));
    info_ = new InfoPanel(this);
    log_ = new LogPanel(this, wxGetApp().logSink());
    // The centre pane never floats, so the viewport's native child window is never re-parented
    // (M1b F14); no border, so the presented extent equals the pane (arbiter LOG 21).
    aui_.AddPane(player_, wxAuiPaneInfo().Name("player").CenterPane().PaneBorder(false));
    aui_.AddPane(info_, wxAuiPaneInfo().Name("info").Caption("Info").Right()
                            .BestSize(FromDIP(wxSize(320, 400))).CloseButton(false));
    aui_.AddPane(log_, wxAuiPaneInfo().Name("log").Caption("Log").Bottom()
                           .BestSize(FromDIP(wxSize(800, 160))).CloseButton(false));
    aui_.Update();
    buildMenus();      // from commandTable()
    buildStatusBar();  // 3 fields (D20) + the 50 ms timer
    SetClientSize(FromDIP(wxSize(1280, 720)));
    Bind(wxEVT_ICONIZE, &MainFrame::onIconize, this);
    Bind(wxEVT_SHOW, &MainFrame::onShow, this);
    Bind(wxEVT_CLOSE_WINDOW, &MainFrame::onClose, this);
}

MainFrame::~MainFrame() {
    aui_.UnInit();
}

// PlayerPanel:
PlayerPanel::PlayerPanel(wxWindow* parent, std::shared_ptr<DisplayState> display)
    : wxPanel(parent) {
    info_bar_ = new wxInfoBarGeneric(this);  // <wx/generic/infobar.h>
    // No slide animation: each animation step would resize the viewport's swapchain.
    info_bar_->SetShowHideEffects(wxSHOW_EFFECT_NONE, wxSHOW_EFFECT_NONE);
    viewport_ = new Viewport(this, std::move(display));
    auto* sizer = new wxBoxSizer(wxVERTICAL);
    sizer->Add(info_bar_, wxSizerFlags().Expand());
    sizer->Add(viewport_, wxSizerFlags(1).Expand());
    SetSizer(sizer);
}
```

Task 8's `onClose` order is unchanged; the status timer is stopped right after `closing_ = true`.

**Tests first:**
- `tests/test_status_text.cpp` (`rawreel_core_tests`): position 123, length 1000, 25 fps →
  `frame 123 / 1000  00:00:04.920`; unbounded → `frame 123  00:00:04.920`; position 90000 at 25 fps
  → time `01:00:00.000`; target 60, actual 59.83 → `60.0 / 59.8 fps`; bottleneck `disk` →
  `bottleneck: disk`; no streams → all three fields empty.

- The `lost` selftest: the Reopen section below (registered first; fails with exit 6 `scenario

The Reopen command and the `lost` selftest (files, interfaces, behaviour, code, tests and steps
R1-R4; its steps run inside Step 3 below):


#### Reopen command and the `lost` selftest (design D7, D25, §5.3 GUI side, §8.4 `lost`, E3)

**Goal of this section:** the GUI end of the device-lost path: `MainFrame::execute(Command::Reopen)`
(menu item and infobar button) calls `Session::reopen()`, a Session `error` with `DeviceLost` (or any
error while no stack is installed) shows the infobar with a Reopen button, and the `lost` selftest
proves on real windows that an injected loss gives the infobar within 5 s without a hang, and that
Reopen presents the kept frame again on the new stack. Its checks use `ShowHistory` and the
renderer's `lastRecorded()`, so a Reopen that only presents the background fails.

**Files** (rawreel):
- Modify `src/gui/main_frame.{h,cpp}`: `INFOBAR_REOPEN_ID`, `offerReopen`, `reopenShown`,
  `currentContext`, the `Reopen` cases of `execute`/`isAvailable`, the infobar button binding, the
  `onError` and `onStackChanged` rules below.
- Modify `src/gui/viewport.{h,cpp}`: `lastRecorded()`.
- Modify `src/gui/selftest.{h,cpp}`: `ShownSet`, `ShowHistory` (moved here from old Task 10), the
  helpers `step` and `presented`, `lostScenario`, the dispatcher line.
- Modify `src/gui/app.{h,cpp}`: the `ShowHistory` sink wrap in selftest mode and `showHistory()`.
- Modify `tests/CMakeLists.txt`: CTest `rawreel.gui_selftest.lost` inside `if(TARGET rawreel_gui AND WIN32)`,
  next to `smoke`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 1 (dvv-gpu): `dvv::gpu::testing::injectDeviceLost(ExecutionContext&)`
    (`dvv/gpu/testing/fault_injection.h`, main library, D8).
  - Task 4: `Command::Reopen` (menu-only, no key), `Clock`.
  - Task 6: `Session::reopen()` (enqueues `OpenJob{rebuild = true}`; coalesced), `status()` with
    `epoch` (`stackEpoch`, first = 1) and `gpuAvailable` (a stack is installed), the loss rule
    (pause, `stackChanged({}, 0)`, `error(DeviceLost "GPU device lost: <message>. Reopen to
    continue.")`, `lastStream` position, reopen lands paused at that position).
  - Task 7: `rawreel::RecordedInfo { std::uint64_t epoch; std::int64_t index;
    dvv::platform::PixelSize extent; double zoom; }` and `ViewportRenderer::lastRecorded()` (safe on
    the GUI thread).
  - Task 8: `Scenario::Lost` and the runner contract (`SelfTestStep`, `SelfTestScript`
    with `presenterFailureFails`, `SelfTest::frame()/app()`, `selfTestScript`, 50 ms ticks, 5 s step
    deadline, exit 6 naming the step, `viewport().requestFrame()` every tick); the viewport lifecycle
    (`setStack`, `presenter()`, the device-lost handler → `reportDeviceLost(epoch)`, the D25 hook for
    a presenter that fails with another error); App OnInit step 7 (`SessionOptions::sink`).
  - This task's shell: `showMessage(text, icon, buttons)`, `dismissMessage()`, `InfoButton`,
    `playerPanel().infoBar()` (`wxInfoBarGeneric`, no show/hide effects), `context_` (the `weak_ptr`
    kept by `onStackChanged`), `closing_`.
- Produces (additions to `src/gui/main_frame.h`):

```cpp
inline constexpr int INFOBAR_REOPEN_ID = wxID_HIGHEST + 60;  // below COMMAND_ID_BASE (+100)

class MainFrame /* this task */ {
public:
    // Infobar with `text`, wxICON_ERROR and one button "Reopen" (INFOBAR_REOPEN_ID) (§5.3, D25).
    void offerReopen(const wxString& text);
    // The infobar is shown and its current message carries the Reopen button.
    [[nodiscard]] bool reopenShown() const;
    // The context of the last stackChanged (expired: no stack). GUI thread.
    [[nodiscard]] std::weak_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> currentContext() const;
};
```

- Produces (addition to `src/gui/viewport.h`; Task 10's `player` resize step uses it too):

```cpp
// The renderer's last recorded composite; nullopt without a renderer (no stack). GUI thread.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<RecordedInfo> lastRecorded() const;
```

- Produces (`src/gui/selftest.h`; Tasks 10 and 11b consume them; complete):

```cpp
struct ShownSet {
    std::int64_t index;
    std::uint32_t count;
    bool viewsMatch;  // every view's key().index == index
    std::uint64_t epoch;
    Clock::time_point at;
};

// Selftest only: records every FrameSet the Session shows (no leases kept) and forwards it.
class ShowHistory final : public IDisplaySink {
public:
    explicit ShowHistory(std::shared_ptr<IDisplaySink> inner) : inner_(std::move(inner)) {}
    void show(FrameSet frames) override {
        ShownSet record{.index = frames.index, .count = frames.count, .viewsMatch = true,
                        .epoch = frames.epoch, .at = Clock::now()};
        for (std::uint32_t k = 0; k < frames.count; ++k) {
            const auto& view = frames.views[k];
            record.viewsMatch = record.viewsMatch && view && view->key().index == frames.index;
        }
        {
            const std::scoped_lock lock(mutex_);
            shown_.push_back(record);
        }
        inner_->show(std::move(frames));
    }
    void clear() override { inner_->clear(); }
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t size() const {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(mutex_);
        return shown_.size();
    }
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<ShownSet> since(std::size_t first) const {
        const std::scoped_lock lock(mutex_);
        return {shown_.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(std::min(first, shown_.size())),
                shown_.end()};
    }

private:
    std::shared_ptr<IDisplaySink> inner_;
    mutable std::mutex mutex_;
    std::vector<ShownSet> shown_;
};

[[nodiscard]] std::vector<SelfTestStep> lostScenario(SelfTest& test);
```

  `App` gains `[[nodiscard]] const std::shared_ptr<ShowHistory>& showHistory() const noexcept;`
  (null outside `--selftest`).
- CTest `rawreel.gui_selftest.lost` (labels `gpu;display;gui`).

**Behaviour:**
- **`execute(Command::Reopen)`**: returns at once when `closing_`; else `dismissMessage()` and
  `session().reopen()`. `isAvailable(Command::Reopen)` is `true` (D25: a Reopen with a healthy
  stack rebuilds it and reopens the attached stream; with no stack and no stream it builds a stack
  only). Repeated clicks are coalesced by the Session (one `DVV_LOG_INFO` line).
- **Infobar button:** in the constructor, after the infobar exists:
  `playerPanel().infoBar().Bind(wxEVT_BUTTON, [this](wxCommandEvent&) { execute(Command::Reopen); },
  INFOBAR_REOPEN_ID);`. The handler does not `Skip()`, so `wxInfoBarGeneric`'s own button handler
  (which would hide the bar) does not run; `execute` dismisses it.
- **`offerReopen(text)`**: `showMessage(text, wxICON_ERROR, {{INFOBAR_REOPEN_ID, "Reopen"}})` and
  `reopenOffered_ = true`. Every other `showMessage` call and `dismissMessage()` set
  `reopenOffered_ = false`. `reopenShown()` = `reopenOffered_ && playerPanel().infoBar().IsShown()`.
- **`onStackChanged(context, epoch)`** (adds to the Task 8/9 handler, which forwards to
  `viewport().setStack` and keeps `context_`): `stackLost_ = context.expired()`; a live context
  while `reopenOffered_` → `dismissMessage()` (a File → Open after a loss built a new stack).
- **`onError(error)`** (replaces the warning-only rule): returns when `closing_`;
  `DVV_LOG_WARN("{}", error.describe())`; then `error.code == dvv::Errc::DeviceLost || stackLost_` →
  `offerReopen(wxString::FromUTF8(error.describe()))` (a loss, or a failed `makeStack` while no
  stack is installed: §7 "infobar keeps Reopen"); otherwise `showMessage(…, wxICON_WARNING)`. Session
  events reach the frame in the Session's order (one `CallAfter` queue), and §5.3 sends
  `stackChanged({}, 0)` before `error(DeviceLost)`, so `stackLost_` is current.
- **D25:** the viewport hook Task 8 added for a presenter that fails with an error other than
  `DeviceLost` is connected here to `offerReopen(wxString::FromUTF8(failure.describe()))`.
- **`Viewport::lastRecorded()`**: `renderer_ ? std::optional(renderer_->lastRecorded()) :
  std::nullopt` (GUI thread; the renderer exists only while a stack is current).
- **`ShowHistory` wiring** (App `OnInit` step 7): with `arguments().selftest != Scenario::None`,
  `showHistory_ = std::make_shared<ShowHistory>(display)` and `SessionOptions::sink =
  showHistory_`; otherwise `sink = display`. `OnExit` releases `showHistory_` together with
  `display`.
- **Dispatcher:** `case Scenario::Lost: return SelfTestScript{lostScenario(test),
  /*presenterFailureFails=*/false};` (the presenter fails with `DeviceLost` on purpose).
- **`lost` scenario** (one stream from the command line, Code below). Steps, each with the runner's
  5 s deadline:
  1. `play with loop` — `execute(Loop)`, `execute(PlayPause)`; passes when a presenter exists,
     `framesPresented >= 5`, playing, position `>= 3`, one stream, and `status().epoch == 1`.
  2. `loss offers Reopen` — injects a loss into `currentContext()`; passes when `reopenShown()`,
     `!status().gpuAvailable`, `viewport().presenter() == nullptr`, at least 10 polls ran since the
     injection and no two polls were 1 s or more apart (the GUI thread was never blocked: R10), and
     `ShowHistory` has a show of epoch 1. The kept index is the index of the **last** show of epoch 1
     (the player position at the loss, which `lastStream` keeps).
  3. `Reopen presents the kept frame` — sends `wxEVT_BUTTON` with `INFOBAR_REOPEN_ID` to the
     infobar (the user's path; it runs `execute(Command::Reopen)`). Passes when `status().epoch ==
     2`, `gpuAvailable`, not playing, `position == kept`, `!reopenShown()`, `ShowHistory` has (since
     the click) a show of epoch 2 at `kept` with `count == 1` and `viewsMatch`, `lastRecorded()` has
     epoch 2 and index `kept`, and `framesPresented` is larger than the value seen on the first poll
     that met all the other conditions.
  4. `play continues` — `execute(PlayPause)`; passes when playing, the position differs from
     `kept`, and at least 3 shows arrived since the step started, all of epoch 2 with `count == 1`
     and `viewsMatch`.
  5. `second loss offers Reopen again` — the same injection and the same pass rule as step 2.
  The runner's close step follows: validation 0 (`OnExit`) and the leak check (`main`).

**Code** (complete; `src/gui/selftest.cpp`, anonymous namespace for the helpers):

```cpp
SelfTestStep step(std::string name, std::move_only_function<void()> start,
                  std::move_only_function<bool(std::string&)> passed,
                  std::chrono::milliseconds deadline = std::chrono::seconds(5)) {
    return {.name = std::move(name), .start = std::move(start), .passed = std::move(passed),
            .deadline = deadline};
}

std::uint64_t presented(Viewport& viewport) {
    const dvv::gpu::Presenter* presenter = viewport.presenter();
    return presenter != nullptr ? presenter->stats().framesPresented : 0;
}
```

```cpp
std::vector<SelfTestStep> lostScenario(SelfTest& test) {
    MainFrame& frame = test.frame();
    ShowHistory& history = *test.app().showHistory();
    struct State {
        std::size_t mark = 0;           // history.size() when the step started
        std::int64_t kept = -1;         // the position at the first loss
        std::optional<std::uint64_t> presentedAtShow;
        Clock::time_point lastPoll{};
        std::int64_t longestGapMs = 0;  // between two polls since the injection
        std::uint32_t polls = 0;
    };
    auto st = std::make_shared<State>();
    auto status = [&frame] { return frame.session().status(); };

    // Injects a loss into the current stack's context. The strong reference lives only inside this
    // function, on the GUI thread (allowed by the §5.3 release rule).
    auto inject = [&frame, &history, st] {
        st->mark = history.size();
        st->lastPoll = Clock::now();
        st->longestGapMs = 0;
        st->polls = 0;
        if (const std::shared_ptr<dvv::gpu::ExecutionContext> context =
                frame.currentContext().lock()) {
            dvv::gpu::testing::injectDeviceLost(*context);
        }
    };
    // The loss is reported and the GUI thread kept ticking (R10).
    auto reported = [&frame, status, st](std::string& seen) {
        const Clock::time_point now = Clock::now();
        st->longestGapMs = std::max<std::int64_t>(
            st->longestGapMs,
            std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(now - st->lastPoll).count());
        st->lastPoll = now;
        ++st->polls;
        const SessionStatus s = status();
        const bool presenter = frame.viewport().presenter() != nullptr;
        seen = std::format("reopen offered {}, gpuAvailable {}, presenter {}, polls {}, "
                           "longest poll gap {} ms",
                           frame.reopenShown(), s.gpuAvailable, presenter, st->polls,
                           st->longestGapMs);
        return frame.reopenShown() && !s.gpuAvailable && !presenter && st->polls >= 10 &&
               st->longestGapMs < 1000;
    };
    std::vector<SelfTestStep> steps;

    steps.push_back(step("play with loop",
        [&frame] {
            frame.execute(Command::Loop);
            frame.execute(Command::PlayPause);
        },
        [&frame, status](std::string& seen) {
            const SessionStatus s = status();
            seen = std::format("streams {}, epoch {}, playing {}, position {}, presented {}",
                               s.streams, s.epoch, s.player.playing, s.player.position,
                               presented(frame.viewport()));
            return s.streams == 1 && s.epoch == 1 && s.player.playing && s.player.position >= 3 &&
                   presented(frame.viewport()) >= 5;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("loss offers Reopen", inject,
        [&history, reported, st](std::string& seen) {
            if (!reported(seen)) return false;
            const std::vector<ShownSet> shows = history.since(0);
            const auto last = std::ranges::find_if(shows.rbegin(), shows.rend(),
                                                   [](const ShownSet& s) { return s.epoch == 1; });
            if (last == shows.rend()) {
                seen = "no show of epoch 1";
                return false;
            }
            st->kept = last->index;
            return true;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("Reopen presents the kept frame",
        [&frame, &history, st] {
            st->mark = history.size();
            st->presentedAtShow.reset();
            wxInfoBarGeneric& bar = frame.playerPanel().infoBar();
            wxCommandEvent click(wxEVT_BUTTON, INFOBAR_REOPEN_ID);
            click.SetEventObject(&bar);
            bar.GetEventHandler()->ProcessEvent(click);  // -> execute(Command::Reopen)
        },
        [&frame, &history, status, st](std::string& seen) {
            const SessionStatus s = status();
            const std::vector<ShownSet> shows = history.since(st->mark);
            const bool shown = std::ranges::any_of(shows, [st](const ShownSet& set) {
                return set.epoch == 2 && set.index == st->kept && set.count == 1 && set.viewsMatch;
            });
            const std::optional<RecordedInfo> recorded = frame.viewport().lastRecorded();
            seen = std::format("epoch {}, gpu {}, playing {}, position {} (kept {}), reopen shown "
                               "{}, show of epoch 2 {}, recorded {}/{}",
                               s.epoch, s.gpuAvailable, s.player.playing, s.player.position,
                               st->kept, frame.reopenShown(), shown,
                               recorded ? std::to_string(recorded->epoch) : "-",
                               recorded ? std::to_string(recorded->index) : "-");
            if (s.epoch != 2 || !s.gpuAvailable || s.player.playing ||
                s.player.position != st->kept || frame.reopenShown() || !shown || !recorded ||
                recorded->epoch != 2 || recorded->index != st->kept) {
                return false;
            }
            const std::uint64_t now = presented(frame.viewport());
            if (!st->presentedAtShow) {
                st->presentedAtShow = now;
                seen = "waiting for a present after the recording";
                return false;
            }
            seen = std::format("framesPresented {} (at the show {})", now, *st->presentedAtShow);
            return now > *st->presentedAtShow;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("play continues",
        [&frame, &history, st] {
            st->mark = history.size();
            frame.execute(Command::PlayPause);
        },
        [&history, status, st](std::string& seen) {
            const SessionStatus s = status();
            const std::vector<ShownSet> shows = history.since(st->mark);
            const bool epoch2 = std::ranges::all_of(shows, [](const ShownSet& set) {
                return set.epoch == 2 && set.count == 1 && set.viewsMatch;
            });
            seen = std::format("playing {}, position {} (kept {}), shows {}, all of epoch 2 {}",
                               s.player.playing, s.player.position, st->kept, shows.size(), epoch2);
            return s.player.playing && s.player.position != st->kept && shows.size() >= 3 &&
                   epoch2;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("second loss offers Reopen again", inject, reported));
    return steps;  // the runner's close step: validation 0, leak check (§8.4 lost)
}
```

(`selftest.cpp` includes `dvv/gpu/testing/fault_injection.h` and `<wx/generic/infobar.h>`;
`rawreel_gui` already links `dvv::gpu`.)

CTest (`tests/CMakeLists.txt`, inside `if(TARGET rawreel_gui AND WIN32)`, next to `smoke`; reuse Task 8's
registration helper if it has one, with these properties):

```cmake
add_test(NAME rawreel.gui_selftest.lost
         COMMAND rawreel_gui --selftest lost "synthetic:gradient?fmt=yuv420p10le&size=64x48&frames=40")
set_tests_properties(rawreel.gui_selftest.lost PROPERTIES
    LABELS "gpu;display;gui"
    SKIP_RETURN_CODE 77
    TIMEOUT 180
    ENVIRONMENT "${gpu_test_environment};NO_AT_BRIDGE=1"
    ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION "${DVV_TEST_ENVIRONMENT}")
```

**Tests first:**
- `rawreel.gui_selftest.lost` is registered first. Before `lostScenario` exists the dispatcher
  returns nullopt for `Scenario::Lost` (Task 8), so the test fails with exit 6 and
  `rawreel-gui --selftest: scenario lost is not available`.
- The exact pass conditions are the five steps above. Each one can fail: step 2 without the
  `onError` → `offerReopen` rule (no Reopen button) or with a blocked GUI thread; step 3 when Reopen
  does not reach `Session::reopen()`, when the Session reopens at another position or playing, or
  when the new presenter shows only the background (`lastRecorded()` is not epoch 2 at `kept`).

**Expected results:** `vs2026` gains the CTest entry `rawreel.gui_selftest.lost` (Windows only;
`ninja-clang` registers no GUI selftest, U-WIN). It passes on NVIDIA
(Debug; Release at the checkpoint) with 0 validation messages and no leak. The epochs go 1 → (loss) → 2 → (loss); R3's mutation fails
at step 3. `vs2026-asan` has no GUI entries.

**Notes:**
- Design D7 (detect → error → user reopens), D8 (injection in the shipped binary), D25 (one Reopen
  path), §5.3 GUI side (`error` with `DeviceLost` → infobar with Reopen; Reopen →
  `session.reopen()`), §7 rows "Device lost seen by the Session", "`makeStack` fails" and "Presenter
  Failed with another error", §8.4 `lost` (arbiter v2 FIX 5 and FIX 6), E3, R10, R14.
- Note (plan choice): `ShowHistory`, `ShownSet` and the `step`/`presented` helpers move from old
  Task 10 into this task, because `lost` needs them and runs before Task 10. Task 10 and Task 11b
  consume them and must not define them again.
- Note (plan choice): the Reopen button gets the fixed id `wxID_HIGHEST + 60`. If this task's shell
  part already defines an infobar Reopen id, keep one definition under the name
  `INFOBAR_REOPEN_ID`.
- Note (plan choice): `onError` offers Reopen also for errors that arrive while no stack is installed
  (`stackLost_`), so a failed `makeStack` during Reopen keeps the button (§7); a live `stackChanged`
  dismisses the Reopen message.
- Note (plan choice): the injection uses the frame's `currentContext()` (the `weak_ptr` of the last
  `stackChanged`) rather than the presenter, so no Presenter accessor is assumed; the temporary
  strong reference is released on the GUI thread, which §5.3 allows.
- Note (plan choice): "the GUI stays responsive" is measured as at least 10 polls after the
  injection with no gap of 1 s or more between two polls (nominal 50 ms).
- Note (plan choice): the kept index is the last show of epoch 1 in `ShowHistory`. The Session's
  position is the last shown index (Task 4 `PlayerStatus`), and after the loss no player exists to
  query. Task 6's `releaseStack()` flushes a show the player has already counted before it releases
  the stack (arbiter plan-v2 FIX 1), so the kept position and the last sunk show agree even when
  the loss is found by the `pump()` check right after a tick.
- Risk: under a heavily loaded CI runner a 1 s poll gap could be scheduling, not a GUI-thread
  block. The failure message prints the gap; treat a gap in `vs2026` Debug as an R10 finding
  first and only then consider a longer bound, recorded in the decisions log.

### Task 10: Transport, keys, drop and dialogs

**Goal:** Make the one-stream player usable with mouse and keyboard: bare keys through
`wxEVT_CHAR_HOOK`, the transport bar, drag-and-drop of one file onto the viewport or the player
panel, the Info pane text (`formatInfo`), the format dialog (catalogue or JSON format, size, fps,
colour including a custom Kr/Kb matrix, candidates) for `formatNeeded` and View → Format…, *Open…*
and *Open URI…*, with a selftest dialog hook. Prove it with the `player` selftest on Windows
(design v2 §8.4: transport, keys, slider, loop, the resize fit check, View → Format…, and the
drop and dialog steps). U-WIN: no WSLg run; the GUI code stays portable wx code and compiles in
the `ninja-clang` build of the Task 11a checkpoint (U-PROC).
