**Behaviour:**
- **Ownership of `MainFrame::execute` cases** (plan choice): this task implements `Open`,
  `OpenUri` and `Format` and makes them available (`Format` only with a stream). Task 9 keeps
  Quit, CloseStreams, the transport cases, Reopen, GpuInfo and Diagnostics; a case Task 9 already
  implemented stays. For reference, the transport cases: PlayPause → `session.togglePlay()`; Stop →
  `stop()`; StepBack/StepForward → `step(∓1)`; StepBack10/StepForward10 → `step(∓10)`; First →
  `seek(0)`; Last → `seek(length − 1)` (no-op when the length is unbounded or there is no stream);
  Loop → Task 9's definition (the frame's loop flag is toggled, then `setLoop(flag)`).
- **Keys (D17).** `MainFrame` binds `wxEVT_CHAR_HOOK` (code below): `normaliseKey` →
  `commandForKey(chord, text_focus)`; a match is executed and the event is consumed (so arrows never
  reach the slider and Space never clicks a button); else `Skip()`. `text_focus` is true only for an
  editable `wxTextEntry` with focus. `main_frame.cpp` holds `static_assert`s that the wx key codes
  Task 4's normaliser names equal wx's: `WXK_ESCAPE == 27`, `WXK_SPACE == 32`, `WXK_END == 312`,
  `WXK_HOME == 313`, `WXK_LEFT == 314`, `WXK_UP == 315`, `WXK_RIGHT == 316`, `WXK_DOWN == 317`,
  `WXK_F11 == 350`, `WXK_ADD == 335`, `WXK_SUBTRACT == 337`, `WXK_NUMPAD0 == 324`, `WXK_NUMPAD9 ==
  333`, `WXK_NUMPAD_ADD == 388`, `WXK_NUMPAD_SUBTRACT == 390`.
- **Viewport focus (R5).** The viewport is created with `wxWANTS_CHARS` and calls `SetFocus()` on
  every mouse button press; no other mouse handling (zoom and pan are v1.x).
- **Transport bar.** Buttons First `|<`, StepBack `<`, PlayPause (`Play`/`Pause`), StepForward `>`,
  Last `>|`, a `wxToggleButton` Loop, a `wxSlider` and a frame label `frame <pos> / <length>`
  (an unbounded stream shows `frame <pos>`). Buttons call `execute(...)`; every button and
  the toggle get `SetCanFocus(false)`. The slider (range `[0, length − 1]`, disabled when the length
  is unbounded or there is no stream) binds `wxEVT_SCROLL_THUMBTRACK` and `wxEVT_SCROLL_CHANGED` →
  `seek(event.GetPosition())`. `update(status)` sets the range when it changed, the value unless a
  scroll event arrived in the last 250 ms, the play label, the toggle and the label. The status
  timer (Task 9) also calls `transportBar().update(status.player)`.
- **Drag and drop (D21, R5).** One `DropTarget` on the viewport and one on the player panel (GTK may
  deliver a drop on the native child to the parent). File names convert with
  `dvv::media::pathFromUtf8(name.utf8_string())`. `MainFrame::openFiles(files)`: returns when
  `closing_`; `plan = routeDrop(files)`; `plan.uri` → `dvv::parseUri(*plan.uri)` (an error → log +
  infobar) → `session.open(0, {.uri = *uri})` with no start (frame 0, design v2 §4.3);
  `plan.ignored > 0` → `DVV_LOG_INFO("{} files ignored: one stream in v1.0", plan.ignored)`.
- **Open (Ctrl+O):** `wxFileDialog` (`wxFD_OPEN | wxFD_FILE_MUST_EXIST`, wildcard
  `Raw video (*.yuv;*.raw;*.rgb;*.bin;*.y4m)|*.yuv;*.raw;*.rgb;*.bin;*.y4m|All files (*.*)|*.*`) →
  `openFiles({path})`. **OpenUri (Ctrl+U):** `wxTextEntryDialog` prefilled
  `synthetic:gradient?fmt=yuv420p&size=1920x1080&frames=250` (X15) → `parseUri` (error → infobar) →
  `session.open(0, …)` (no start).
- **`formatNeeded(slot, request, candidates, reason)`** (D21): queued in a FIFO; the GUI shows one
  `FormatDialog` at a time (the next after the current one closes). Prefill: the best candidate
  (`choiceFromCandidate(candidates.candidates[0])`), else the last accepted choice, else
  `FormatChoice{.format = "yuv420p"}` (1920×1080, 25 fps). OK → remember the choice →
  `applyChoice(dvv::formatUri(request.uri), choice)` → `parseUri` → `session.open(slot, {.uri})`
  (no start); Cancel → log "open of <uri> cancelled". `formatDialogsShown()` counts dialogs.
- **`FormatDialog` controls:** a reason label (the event's `reason`); format `wxChoice` with every
  `catalogue()` name, plus the current `@<path>` entry when one is loaded; "Load JSON…" → `wxFileDialog`
  (`*.json`) → `"@" + utf8 path`; width and height `wxSpinCtrl` [1, 65535]; fps `wxTextCtrl`
  (`parseFpsText`); matrix `wxChoice` (Unspecified, BT.601, BT.709, BT.2020 NCL, RGB, Custom) with Kr
  and Kb `wxTextCtrl`s enabled only for Custom (`std::from_chars` floats); range (Unspecified, Limited,
  Full); transfer (Unspecified, sRGB, BT.1886, Linear, PQ, HLG); primaries (Unspecified, BT.601-525,
  BT.601-625, BT.709, BT.2020); a candidates `wxListBox` (`<confidence> % <component>: <why>`) whose
  selection calls `setChoice(choiceFromCandidate(c))` keeping the colour fields; an error label. After
  every change: `validateChoice(choice())` (unparseable fps → `Format` "fps must be N, N/D or a
  decimal"); an error disables OK and shows the message (§7 "OK stays disabled").
- **View → Format… (Command::Format):** with a stream (slot 0): prefill `choiceFromInfo(info)`; OK →
  `applyChoice(resolvedUri, choice)` → `session.open(0, {.uri}, status().player.position)` (a new
  origin; the position is kept through `start`, design v2 §4.3, D21).
- **Infobar action (§7):** a stream warning containing "trailing bytes ignored" → message
  "<file name>: <warning>" with a "Format…" button (`InfoButton`; it runs `execute(Command::Format)`).
  The button handler, bound on the infobar (`wxEVT_BUTTON`, by id), calls `Dismiss()` itself.
- **Info pane.** On `streamsChanged`: keep the list (`streams()`),
  `infoPanel.setText(formatInfo(streams))`.
- **`formatInfo` text (binding for the unit cases).** Lines joined with `\n`:
  - No streams: `No stream. Open a file (Ctrl+O) or a URI (Ctrl+U), or drop a file here.`
  - Per stream (one in v1.0): `Stream <slot+1>: <resolvedUri>`;
    `  <format name>, <W>x<H>, <fps> fps, <N> frames` (`<fps>` is `num` when `den == 1`, else
    `{:.3f}` of `num/den`; `unbounded` instead of `<N> frames` when `frameCount` is empty);
    `  colour: matrix <m>[ (assumed)], range <r>[ (assumed)], transfer <t>[ (assumed)], primaries
    <p>[ (assumed)]` from `StreamInfo::colour` (`(assumed)` per `AssumedColour` bit; names as M2's
    CLI: `bt601 bt709 bt2020 rgb`, `custom kr=<{:.4f}> kb=<{:.4f}>`; `limited full`; `srgb bt1886 pq
    hlg linear`; `bt601-525 bt601-625 bt709 bt2020`);
    `  tone-mapped (BT.2390, 1000 → 100 nits)` only for transfer PQ or HLG (D4);
    `  warning: <text>` per warning.
- **Dialog hook** (code below; §8.4): every `ShowModal` of `FormatDialog` goes through `showModal`;
  with a hook set, a `CallAfter` queued before `ShowModal` runs inside the modal loop, calls the hook
  (which uses `choice`/`setChoice`) and `EndModal(wxID_OK)`. The hook is moved out first (one shot).
  The runner's `busy` guard keeps ticks out while the step's `start` is inside `ShowModal`.

**Code**

`MainFrame::onCharHook` (binding; D17):

```cpp
void MainFrame::onCharHook(wxKeyEvent& event) {
    if (closing_) {
        event.Skip();
        return;
    }
    const KeyChord chord = normaliseKey(event.GetKeyCode(), event.ControlDown(), event.ShiftDown(),
                                        event.AltDown());
    // Only an editable text entry keeps bare keys (the read-only Info and Log panes do not, D17).
    const auto* entry = dynamic_cast<const wxTextEntry*>(wxWindow::FindFocus());
    const bool text_focus = entry != nullptr && entry->IsEditable();
    if (const std::optional<Command> command = commandForKey(chord, text_focus)) {
        execute(*command);
        return;  // consumed: the slider and the buttons never see bare keys
    }
    event.Skip();
}
```

`src/gui/drop_target.cpp` (complete):

```cpp
#include <wx/wx.h>  // first (M1b R11)

#include "drop_target.h"

#include <utility>

#include "dvv/media/types.h"

namespace rawreel {

DropTarget::DropTarget(Handler handler) : handler_(std::move(handler)) {}

bool DropTarget::OnDropFiles(wxCoord /*x*/, wxCoord /*y*/, const wxArrayString& names) {
    std::vector<std::filesystem::path> files;
    files.reserve(names.size());
    for (const wxString& name : names) {
        files.push_back(dvv::media::pathFromUtf8(name.utf8_string()));  // never the ANSI code page
    }
    handler_(std::move(files));
    return true;
}

}  // namespace rawreel
```

(`window.SetDropTarget(new DropTarget(...))` transfers ownership to wx; mark the `new` with
`// NOLINT(cppcoreguidelines-owning-memory): wx takes ownership`. The handler is
`[frame](std::vector<std::filesystem::path> files) { frame->openFiles(std::move(files)); }`.)

`showModal` (complete, `src/gui/selftest.cpp`):

```cpp
DialogHooks& dialogHooks() {
    static DialogHooks hooks;  // GUI thread only
    return hooks;
}

int showModal(FormatDialog& dialog) {
    std::move_only_function<void(FormatDialog&)>& slot = dialogHooks().format;
    if (slot) {
        // One shot; CallAfter needs a copyable functor, so the hook is shared.
        auto hook = std::make_shared<std::move_only_function<void(FormatDialog&)>>(std::move(slot));
        slot = nullptr;
        dialog.CallAfter([&dialog, hook] {
            (*hook)(dialog);
            dialog.EndModal(wxID_OK);
        });
    }
    return dialog.ShowModal();
}
```

Event synthesis helpers (complete, `src/gui/selftest.cpp`, anonymous namespace; §8.4: events go
straight to the target's handler, no OS input injection; `step` and `presented` are Task 9's):

```cpp
// Through the frame's wxEVT_CHAR_HOOK handler, as a real key press reaches it first.
void sendKey(wxFrame& frame, int keycode, bool shift = false) {
    wxKeyEvent event(wxEVT_CHAR_HOOK);
    event.SetEventObject(&frame);
    event.m_keyCode = keycode;
    if (keycode > 0 && keycode < 128) {
        event.m_uniChar = static_cast<wxChar>(keycode);
    }
    event.SetShiftDown(shift);
    frame.GetEventHandler()->ProcessEvent(event);
}

void sendSliderTrack(wxSlider& slider, int value) {
    slider.SetValue(value);
    wxScrollEvent event(wxEVT_SCROLL_THUMBTRACK, slider.GetId(), value, wxHORIZONTAL);
    event.SetEventObject(&slider);
    slider.GetEventHandler()->ProcessEvent(event);
}

// Drops `files` on the viewport's DropTarget (the wx callback a real drop calls).
void dropFiles(Viewport& viewport, const std::vector<std::filesystem::path>& files) {
    auto* target = static_cast<DropTarget*>(viewport.GetDropTarget());
    const wxSize size = viewport.GetClientSize();
    wxArrayString names;
    for (const auto& file : files) {
        names.Add(wxString::FromUTF8(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(file.u8string().c_str())));
    }
    target->OnDropFiles(size.x / 2, size.y / 2, names);
}

// `bytes` bytes of `value`: a raw file for the drop steps.
void writeFile(const std::filesystem::path& file, std::size_t bytes, char value) {
    std::ofstream(file, std::ios::binary).write(std::string(bytes, value).data(),
                                                static_cast<std::streamsize>(bytes));
}

// Every set holds `count` views of its own index, and each index follows the previous one
// (0 after length - 1 when looping).
bool consecutive(std::span<const ShownSet> shows, std::uint32_t count,
                 std::optional<std::int64_t> wrap_length, std::string& seen) {
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < shows.size(); ++i) {
        if (shows[i].count != count || !shows[i].viewsMatch) {
            seen = std::format("set {} (index {}) has {} views, match {}", i, shows[i].index,
                               shows[i].count, shows[i].viewsMatch);
            return false;
        }
        if (i == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        const std::int64_t expected =
            wrap_length && shows[i - 1].index == *wrap_length - 1 ? 0 : shows[i - 1].index + 1;
        if (shows[i].index != expected) {
            seen = std::format("index {} followed {} (expected {})", shows[i].index,
                               shows[i - 1].index, expected);
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

// The Info line of a colour field without "(assumed)" right after it, e.g. "matrix bt601,".
bool infoHas(MainFrame& frame, std::string_view field) {
    const std::string text = frame.infoPanel().text().utf8_string();
    const std::size_t at = text.find(field);
    return at != std::string::npos &&
           text.compare(at + field.size(), 10, " (assumed)") != 0;
}
```

`player` scenario (complete; one stream from the command line, see CTest below):

```cpp
std::vector<SelfTestStep> playerScenario(SelfTest& test) {
    MainFrame& frame = test.frame();
    ShowHistory& history = *test.app().showHistory();
    struct State {
        std::uint64_t presented0 = 0;
        std::size_t mark = 0;
        std::int64_t position = 0;
        dvv::platform::PixelSize extent0{};
        std::filesystem::path dir;
        std::uint64_t dialogs0 = 0;
        bool formatHookRan = false;
        State() = default;
        State(const State&) = delete;
        State& operator=(const State&) = delete;
        State(State&&) = delete;
        State& operator=(State&&) = delete;
        // A failed step (or "player without a URI") ends the script early: the scratch folder is
        // removed when the steps are destroyed as well (no-op after the last step removed it).
        ~State() {
            if (!dir.empty()) {
                std::error_code error;
                std::filesystem::remove_all(dir, error);
            }
        }
    };
    auto st = std::make_shared<State>();
    auto status = [&frame] { return frame.session().status().player; };
    auto streamIs = [&frame](std::uint32_t w, std::uint32_t h, std::string_view format) {
        const auto& streams = frame.streams();
        return streams.size() == 1 && streams[0].info.desc.width == w &&
               streams[0].info.desc.height == h &&
               dvv::media::descriptor(streams[0].info.desc.format).name == format;
    };
    std::vector<SelfTestStep> steps;

    steps.push_back(step("open one stream",
        [st] {
            st->dir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() /
                      std::format("rawreel-selftest-{}", wxGetProcessId());
            std::filesystem::create_directories(st->dir);
        },
        [&frame, status](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("streams {}, presented {}, position {}", frame.streams().size(),
                               presented(frame.viewport()), status().position);
            return frame.streams().size() == 1 && presented(frame.viewport()) >= 2 &&
                   status().position == 0;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("play 30 frames",
        [&frame, &history, st] {
            st->presented0 = presented(frame.viewport());
            st->mark = history.size();
            frame.execute(Command::PlayPause);
        },
        [&frame, &history, st, status](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("position {}", status().position);
            if (status().position < 30) return false;
            if (presented(frame.viewport()) <= st->presented0) {
                seen = "framesPresented did not grow";
                return false;
            }
            return consecutive(history.since(st->mark), 1, std::nullopt, seen);
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("Space pauses (CHAR_HOOK)",
        [&frame, status] {
            if (status().playing) sendKey(frame, WXK_SPACE);
        },
        [status](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("playing {}, position {}", status().playing, status().position);
            return !status().playing && status().position < 59;  // paused by the key, not at the end
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("step back",
        [&frame, st, status] {
            st->position = status().position;
            frame.execute(Command::StepBack);
        },
        [st, status](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("position {} (expected {})", status().position, st->position - 1);
            return status().position == st->position - 1;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("Right steps forward (CHAR_HOOK)",
        [&frame] { sendKey(frame, WXK_RIGHT); },
        [st, status](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("position {} (expected {})", status().position, st->position);
            return status().position == st->position;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("slider to 5",
        [&frame] { sendSliderTrack(frame.transportBar().slider(), 5); },
        [&history, status](std::string& seen) {
            const auto shows = history.since(0);
            seen = std::format("position {}", status().position);
            return status().position == 5 && !shows.empty() && shows.back().index == 5;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("Home (CHAR_HOOK)",
        [&frame] { sendKey(frame, WXK_HOME); },
        [status](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("position {}", status().position);
            return status().position == 0;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("End (CHAR_HOOK)",
        [&frame] { sendKey(frame, WXK_END); },
        [status](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("position {}", status().position);
            return status().position == 59;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("loop wraps",
        [&frame, &history, st] {
            frame.execute(Command::Loop);
            st->mark = history.size();
            frame.execute(Command::PlayPause);
        },
        [&history, st, status](std::string& seen) {
            const auto shows = history.since(st->mark);
            const bool wrapped = std::ranges::any_of(shows, [](const ShownSet& s) { return s.index == 0; });
            seen = std::format("loop {}, shows {}", status().loop, shows.size());
            return status().loop && wrapped && consecutive(shows, 1, 60, seen);
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("pause",
        [&frame] {
            frame.execute(Command::PlayPause);
            frame.execute(Command::Loop);  // loop off again
        },
        [status](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("playing {}, loop {}", status().playing, status().loop);
            return !status().playing && !status().loop;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("resize keeps the fit",
        [&frame, st] {
            st->extent0 = frame.viewport().physicalClientSize();
            st->presented0 = presented(frame.viewport());
            // DIP-scaled and large enough that the centre pane stays non-empty next to the Info
            // and Log panes at 150 % scaling (a 400x300 client could collapse the viewport).
            frame.SetClientSize(frame.FromDIP(wxSize(900, 640)));
        },
        [&frame, st](std::string& seen) {
            const dvv::platform::PixelSize extent = frame.viewport().physicalClientSize();
            const std::optional<RecordedInfo> recorded = frame.viewport().lastRecorded();
            const double fit = ViewTransform::fit(extent, {64, 48}).zoom();
            seen = std::format("extent {}x{} (was {}x{}), recorded {}x{} zoom {} (fit {})",
                               extent.width, extent.height, st->extent0.width, st->extent0.height,
                               recorded ? recorded->extent.width : 0,
                               recorded ? recorded->extent.height : 0,
                               recorded ? recorded->zoom : 0.0, fit);
            return !(extent == st->extent0) && extent.width > 0 && extent.height > 0 && recorded &&
                   recorded->extent == extent && recorded->zoom == fit &&
                   presented(frame.viewport()) > st->presented0;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("View > Format... sets BT.601 and full range",
        [&frame, st, status] {
            st->position = status().position;
            dialogHooks().format = [](FormatDialog& dialog) {
                FormatChoice choice = dialog.choice();
                choice.colour.matrix = dvv::media::ColourMatrix::Bt601;
                choice.colour.range = dvv::media::ColourRange::Full;
                dialog.setChoice(choice);
            };
            frame.execute(Command::Format);  // ShowModal; the hook fills and closes the dialog
        },
        [&frame, st, status](std::string& seen) {
            const bool info = infoHas(frame, "matrix bt601,") && infoHas(frame, "range full,");
            seen = std::format("Info bt601/full {}, position {} (kept {}), playing {}", info,
                               status().position, st->position, status().playing);
            return info && status().position == st->position && !status().playing;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("drop a named file",
        [&frame, st] {
            const auto file = st->dir / "drop_16x16_gray.yuv";
            writeFile(file, 768, '\x40');  // 3 frames of gray 16x16
            st->presented0 = presented(frame.viewport());
            st->dialogs0 = frame.formatDialogsShown();
            dropFiles(frame.viewport(), {file});
        },
        [&frame, st, streamIs](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("gray 16x16 {}, dialogs {}", streamIs(16, 16, "gray"),
                               frame.formatDialogsShown() - st->dialogs0);
            return streamIs(16, 16, "gray") && frame.formatDialogsShown() == st->dialogs0 &&
                   presented(frame.viewport()) > st->presented0;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("drop clip.yuv without a size",
        [&frame, st] {
            const auto file = st->dir / "clip.yuv";
            writeFile(file, 768, '\x80');  // no size in the name: the format dialog asks
            st->dialogs0 = frame.formatDialogsShown();
            dialogHooks().format = [st](FormatDialog& dialog) {
                FormatChoice choice;
                choice.format = "gray";
                choice.width = 16;
                choice.height = 16;
                choice.fps = {25, 1};
                dialog.setChoice(choice);
                st->formatHookRan = true;
            };
            dropFiles(frame.viewport(), {file});
        },
        [&frame, st, streamIs](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("dialogs {}, hook {}, gray 16x16 {}",
                               frame.formatDialogsShown() - st->dialogs0, st->formatHookRan,
                               streamIs(16, 16, "gray"));
            return st->formatHookRan && frame.formatDialogsShown() > st->dialogs0 &&
                   streamIs(16, 16, "gray") &&
                   frame.streams()[0].resolvedUri.find("clip.yuv") != std::string::npos;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("drop two files",
        [&frame, st] {
            const auto first = st->dir / "first_32x16_gray.yuv";
            const auto second = st->dir / "second_16x16_gray.yuv";
            writeFile(first, 1536, '\x20');
            writeFile(second, 768, '\x60');
            dropFiles(frame.viewport(), {first, second});
        },
        [&frame, streamIs](std::string& seen) {
            const bool logged = frame.logPanel().text().Contains("1 files ignored: one stream in v1.0");
            seen = std::format("gray 32x16 {}, log line {}", streamIs(32, 16, "gray"), logged);
            return streamIs(32, 16, "gray") && logged;
        }));
    steps.push_back(step("close stream and clean up",
        [&frame] { frame.execute(Command::CloseStreams); },
        [&frame, st](std::string& seen) {
            seen = std::format("streams {}", frame.streams().size());
            if (!frame.streams().empty()) return false;
            std::error_code error;
            std::filesystem::remove_all(st->dir, error);  // a failure only logs
            if (error) DVV_LOG_WARN("selftest: could not remove the scratch folder: {}", error.message());
            return true;
        }));
    return steps;  // the runner appends the close step (validation 0, leak check)
}
```

(`wxGetProcessId()` keeps parallel runs apart. The Log pane shows the line after its 100 ms timer;
the step's 5 s deadline covers it.)

```cmake
         COMMAND rawreel_gui --selftest player "synthetic:gradient?fmt=yuv420p10le&size=64x48&frames=60")

    LABELS "gpu;display;gui"
    SKIP_RETURN_CODE 77
```

**Tests first:**
- `rawreel_core_tests`, `tests/test_info_text.cpp`:
  - `info: no stream` — `formatInfo({})` equals the "No stream. …" sentence.
  - `info: assumed defaults` — a `StreamInfo{0, "synthetic:gradient?fmt=yuv420p&size=64x48&fps=25&frames=40&seed=1", info, resolveColour(info.desc)}`
    with `yuv420p` 64×48, fps 25/1, 40 frames, colour Unspecified: the text contains
    `Stream 1: synthetic:gradient?fmt=yuv420p&size=64x48&fps=25&frames=40&seed=1`,
    `yuv420p, 64x48, 25 fps, 40 frames` and
    `colour: matrix bt601 (assumed), range limited (assumed), transfer srgb (assumed), primaries bt709 (assumed)`.
  - `info: fps and length` — fps 30000/1001 → `29.970 fps`; `frameCount` empty → `unbounded`.
  - `info: tone mapping line` — transfer PQ → contains `tone-mapped (BT.2390, 1000 → 100 nits)`;
    HLG → contains it; sRGB → does not.
  - `info: custom matrix` — `ColourMatrix::Custom`, kr 0.212, kb 0.087 → contains
    `matrix custom kr=0.2120 kb=0.0870`.
  - `info: warnings` — warning `5 trailing bytes ignored` → contains `  warning: 5 trailing bytes ignored`.
- Format-choice unit file, `format choice: fps text` — `"25"` → 25/1; `"30000/1001"` → 30000/1001;
  `"29.97"` → 2997/100; `"50.000"` → 50/1; `""`, `"0"`, `"-25"`, `"1/0"`, `"25fps"`, `"2,5"` → nullopt.
- `format choice: custom matrix` — `applyChoice("synthetic:gradient?fmt=yuv420p&size=8x8", {format "yuv420p", 8×8, 25/1, colour {Custom, Unspecified, Unspecified, Unspecified, 0.212f, 0.087f}})`
  → the query contains `matrix=custom`, `kr=0.212` and `kb=0.087` (add if Task 4 lacks it); and

- GUI: CTest `rawreel.gui_selftest.player`. It fails first: exit 6 `rawreel-gui --selftest:

  ```powershell
  Bare keys through CHAR_HOOK (editable text entries keep them), the transport bar with the seek
  ```

**Expected results:**
- `rawreel_core_tests` gains 8 cases (6 info, 2 format choice); green in `vs2026` Debug.
- `vs2026` gains 1 CTest entry (`gui_selftest.player`), passing on NVIDIA (Windows, Debug); smoke
  and lost still pass.
- FORMAT-OK; privacy lint clean. (Release with the three GUI scenarios, `vs2026-asan` (no GUI
  entries), clang-tidy and the `ninja-clang` build that compiles the GUI: Task 11a checkpoint.)

**Commit:** `feat: add the transport bar, hotkeys, drag-and-drop and the format dialog to rawreel-gui`.

**Notes:**
- Design references: D2, D17, D18, D20, D21 (opening rules, View → Format…), D23 (fit; the resize
  check), X7 (JSON load instead of an editor), X8 (Kr/Kb), X15 (*Open URI…*), §4.3 (commands,
  drop, format choice, start position), §7 rows, §8.4 (`player`), R5, R11, R12; arbiter v2 FIX 2
  and FIX 6.
- Note (plan choice): `formatInfo` lives in `rawreel_core` (`src/core/info_text.{h,cpp}`) because
  §8.4 tests it as a pure unit and the GUI is not built under sanitizers.
- Note (plan choice): the scenario URI uses `frames=60` (the design example says 40) so the "Space
  pauses" step has ≥ 1 s of slack on slow CI runners; `size=64x48` is M2's synthetic spelling of
  `w=64&h=48`.
- Note (plan choice): the design's "Info shows BT.601 and full without assumed" is checked on
  `formatInfo`'s names: `matrix bt601,` and `range full,` without `(assumed)` after them (transfer
  and primaries stay assumed for a synthetic stream).
- Note (plan choice): the resize step sets the frame's client size to `FromDIP(900, 640)` instead
  of the design's 400×300 example (arbiter plan-v2 FIX 18). On MSW `SetClientSize` takes physical
  pixels, and at 150 % the Info pane alone is wider than 400 px, so the viewport could collapse.
  The step requires the viewport extent to change and stay non-empty; the fit
  itself is the renderer's (D23), so the check reads `lastRecorded()`.
- Note (plan choice): `clip.yuv` is 16×16 gray like the named file before it, so the step also
  checks that the stream's URI is `clip.yuv`.
- Note (plan choice): the drop of several files logs "N files ignored: one stream in v1.0" (design
  §7 "log"), no infobar; File → Open selects one file.
- Note (plan choice): `formatNeeded` events queue and show one dialog at a time (no nested modal
  loops); `parseFpsText` lives in `format_choice` (wx-free, unit-tested).
- Risk: `CallAfter` inside `ShowModal` relies on the modal loop processing pending events; it does on
  MSW and GTK. If a platform runs the hook before the dialog is modal, queue the hook with a second
  `CallAfter` from inside the first and record it.
- Risk R12: synthesised events skip OS focus and GTK quirks; E6 (Task 11a, Windows) covers real
  input. GTK-only failures of the `player` steps are not looked for in M3 (U-WIN; v1.x).
- Cut by U-SCOPE (design v2 §13): mouse zoom at the cursor, pan, fit/100 %/ladder commands,
  fullscreen, the pan/pipette/select/grid tools, zoom to selection, `selectionFromImagePoints`,
  probe refresh, the 2-tile and fullscreen steps of `player`, the `tools` scenario, AddStream,
  `ExportDialog`, the GPU menu, the "Choose GPU…/Retry" infobar buttons, the `recovery` scenario
  (replaced by Task 9's `lost`), `ReferenceSource`.

---

### Task 11a: GUI completion

**Goal:** The three GUI scenarios (`smoke`, `player`, `lost`) pass five times in a row on Windows
(Debug and Release, local NVIDIA); the E6 manual checklist is walked through with the user on
Windows, with screenshots; the G2 workspace workflow runs (`vs2026`, `vs2026-asan`). Defects found
here are fixed with tests where they can be automated. U-WIN: the WSLg and Xvfb ×5 runs, the E6
walk-through on WSLg and the GTK checks (CHAR_HOOK focus, drop target on the native child) are not
part of M3; they are listed in future.md for v1.x.

**Files:**
- rawreel: fixes only (any file of Tasks 8–10; typically `viewport.cpp`, `main_frame.cpp`,
  `drop_target.cpp`, `selftest.cpp`), each with a unit case or a selftest step when the defect can
  be reproduced without real input. A fix in shared GUI code stays portable; a fix that touches a
  Linux-only file keeps it compiling; the checkpoint build of Step 8 compiles it (Linux sanity build).
- rawreel-workspace: the M3 plan file's `## Changes during execution` section (find the file with
  `Get-ChildItem rawreel-workspace/docs/superpowers/plans -Filter '*m3*'`).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `rawreel-gui` of Tasks 8–10 and M2's `rawreel-cli` (`dump`, `info`); the selftest
  scenarios `smoke`, `player`, `lost` and their CTest entries `rawreel.gui_selftest.<name>`; the
  M2 custom-format JSON schema (`formatFromJson`, described in dvv-media's README).
- Produces: the E5/E6 record for Windows (the user's confirmation per item), a flakiness record
  (×5), the fixes and the G2 workspace-workflow result. Nothing in Task 11a depends on Task 12.
  M3.1 Task 11b (U-M31) runs after M3 and includes 11a's fixes.

**Behaviour:**
- **E6 checklist** (design v2 §1 E6 as narrowed by U-WIN; on Windows only; every item is done by
  the user with real input; the agent prepares the files, starts the program, takes a screenshot
  when the user says an item is on screen, shows it, and records the user's verdict):
  1. Drag `clip.yuv` (no size in its name) onto the window; the format dialog appears; the user
     enters 640×360, `yuv420p`, 25 fps; the stream plays. Format dialog: OK is disabled for width
     641 with `yuv420p` (the error names "multiple of 2"; the width control's range starts at 1).
  2. Drop `clip.y4m`; it plays without a dialog.
  3. Open `clip.yuv` again; in the format dialog, *Load JSON…* with `my_yuv420p.json` (a custom
     format, size 640×360, 25 fps); the stream plays and looks as in item 1.
  4. Play/pause (Space), step (Left/Right, Shift+Left/Right), seek (slider; Home/End), loop (L),
     with the hotkeys and with the transport bar.
  5. Resize the window (drag the border, maximise, restore): the frame stays fitted, centred, with
     its aspect kept.
  6. View → Format…: matrix BT.601 (assumed for 640×360) → BT.709 and range limited → full: a
     visible change; the Info pane shows `matrix bt709,` without `(assumed)`; the frame position is
     kept.
  7. View → Format…: matrix Custom with Kr 0.212, Kb 0.087: the Info pane shows
     `matrix custom kr=0.2120 kb=0.0870`.
  8. *Open URI…* `synthetic:gradient?fmt=yuv420p10le&size=1280x720&frames=100&transfer=pq&primaries=bt2020`:
     the Info pane shows `tone-mapped (BT.2390, 1000 → 100 nits)`.
  9. Dark theme: restart with `--appearance dark`; the frame, menus and panes are dark (R6: some
     controls such as AUI captions or the infobar may stay light; record which).
  10. Only if the user's display uses scaling (e.g. 150 %): items 4 and 5 again; the presented
      frame fills the viewport correctly (R11: the fit uses the physical extent).
  Then read the redirected stderr file: no validation message (except
  `VUID-VkSwapchainCreateInfoKHR-imageExtent-01274` during a drag-resize, the known false positive of
  M1b) and no leak report.
- **Not in M3 (U-WIN, future.md):** the WSLg walk-through (incl. its drag-and-drop limits), the
  Xvfb runs (no xvfb question is asked) and the GTK checks of R5 (real keys reach
  `wxEVT_CHAR_HOOK` after a click into the viewport; a drop on the native child reaches a
  `DropTarget`). There is no GTK viewport in v1.0: Linux builds the M1b stub only (Task 8), and the
  GTK viewport with these checks is v1.x.
- **The custom format file** `my_yuv420p.json`: an 8-bit 4:2:0 planar layout (Y, U, V planes) written
  in M2's custom-format JSON schema (copy the schema's yuv420p-like example from dvv-media's README
  or M2's `formatFromJson` test fixtures and rename it `my_yuv420p`); before the user step, the agent
  checks it with `rawreel-cli info "file:<path>/clip.yuv?fmt=@<path>/my_yuv420p.json&size=640x360"`
  (expected: 100 frames, the custom name).

**Tests first:** this task adds no feature. Its tests are the ×5 runs and the checklist. Every defect
gets a failing unit case or selftest step first (when it can be reproduced without real input), then
the fix.

**Checkpoint task (U-PROC):** Task 11a is a checkpoint and the last task of M3 alpha (U-M3A); after
it the controller stops for the user's review of alpha. It covers Tasks 10 and 11a: `rawreel`
(`<cp-base>` = its head at the Task 9 checkpoint) and the docs-only rawreel-workspace. Its Release
builds and runs (Steps 1, 2, 3, 5) are part of the task's subject (E5 ×5 in Debug and Release) and
of its checkpoint.

**Steps:**
- [ ] **Step 1: build Debug and Release** (Windows):
  `Push-Location rawreel; cmake --preset vs2026; cmake --build --preset vs2026; cmake --build --preset vs2026-release; Pop-Location`.
- [ ] **Step 2: Windows ×5** (background; a GUI run takes about 10–30 s):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location rawreel; ctest --preset vs2026 -R "rawreel\.gui_selftest\." --repeat until-fail:5 --output-on-failure; ctest --preset vs2026-release -R "rawreel\.gui_selftest\." --repeat until-fail:5 --output-on-failure; Pop-Location`.
  Expected: `100% tests passed, 0 tests failed out of 3` for each configuration (every repeat passes;
  a failure stops the repeat and is investigated with superpowers:systematic-debugging, never retried
  away).
- [ ] **Step 3: exit codes.** `& _build/rawreel/vs2026/Release/rawreel-gui.exe --selftest bogus 2>&1 | Out-Host; $LASTEXITCODE` → 1;
  `--selftest player` without a URI → 6 with `step 1 (open one stream) failed: streams 0`; two URIs
  → 1 with `at most one URI`.
- [ ] **Step 4: prepare the E6 files on Windows** in a scratch folder (never in a repository):
  ```powershell
  $cli = (Get-ChildItem -Recurse _build/rawreel/vs2026 -Filter rawreel-cli.exe | Where-Object FullName -Match '\\Release\\' | Select-Object -First 1).FullName
  $e6 = Join-Path $env:TEMP 'rawreel-e6'; New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $e6 | Out-Null
  & $cli dump "synthetic:gradient?fmt=yuv420p&size=640x360&frames=100" --view original --via cpu --count 100 --out (Join-Path $e6 'clip.yuv')
  # clip.y4m: the same 100 frames with a YUV4MPEG2 header and FRAME markers.
  $raw = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllBytes((Join-Path $e6 'clip.yuv')); $frame = 640 * 360 * 3 / 2
  $out = [System.IO.File]::Create((Join-Path $e6 'clip.y4m'))
  $header = [System.Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetBytes("YUV4MPEG2 W640 H360 F25:1 Ip A1:1 C420jpeg`n"); $out.Write($header, 0, $header.Length)
  $tag = [System.Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetBytes("FRAME`n")
  for ($i = 0; $i -lt 100; $i++) { $out.Write($tag, 0, $tag.Length); $out.Write($raw, $i * $frame, $frame) }
  $out.Close()
  & $cli info (Join-Path $e6 'clip.y4m')
  ```
  Expected: `info` shows 640×360, `yuv420p`, 25 fps, 100 frames. Write `my_yuv420p.json` into `$e6`
  (Behaviour) and check it with `& $cli info "file:$e6/clip.yuv?fmt=@$e6/my_yuv420p.json&size=640x360"`
  (the path stays in the tool call; it is never committed).
- [ ] **Step 5: the E6 checklist on Windows with the user.** Start
  `$env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; Start-Process _build/rawreel/vs2026/Release/rawreel-gui.exe -RedirectStandardError (Join-Path $e6 'gui.err.txt')`
  and open Explorer on `$e6` (`Start-Process explorer.exe $e6`). Walk through items 1–10. Screenshots:
  .NET capture of the primary screen (`System.Windows.Forms.Screen`,
  `System.Drawing.Graphics.CopyFromScreen`) into the session scratchpad, shown to the user. Read the
  stderr file at the end (Behaviour). Delete `$e6` (`Remove-Item -Recurse $e6`) when the
  walk-through is done.
- [ ] **Step 6: fixes.** For each defect: failing test first (when automatable), fix, rerun Step 2
  for the affected scenario, commit `fix: <what>` in rawreel with the trailer.
- [ ] **Step 7: Per-task checks for rawreel** (only when rawreel changed in this task, on its final
  commit, before Step 8; `<base>` = the rawreel head before this task):
  1. clang-format on the changed files (expected `FORMAT-OK`; fixes in a new `style: …` commit
     first):
     `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc '. _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd rawreel && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <base> -- "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r clang-format --dry-run --Werror && echo FORMAT-OK'`
  2. One background call (Windows Debug):
     `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location rawreel; cmake --preset vs2026 | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026; ctest --preset vs2026 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error'; Pop-Location`.
     Expected: `100% tests passed`, no `Skipped` for `gpu`-labelled tests.
- [ ] **Step 8: Checkpoint checks (Tasks 10, 11a; Global Constraints) with the G2 workspace
  workflow** (the whole workspace at Tasks 0–11a, so G1 is covered too). The workspace build holds
  every repository, so its `vs2026` workflow
  (Debug and Release) and `vs2026-asan` workflow are the checkpoint's Windows Release and ASan runs
  for rawreel too. Tasks 10 and 11a have no ×10 race checks. Two background calls in parallel:
  - `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location rawreel-workspace; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error|AddressSanitizer'; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026-asan 2>&1 | Select-String 'tests passed|tests failed|Skipped|error|AddressSanitizer'; Pop-Location`
  - clang-tidy on the rawreel sources changed since `<cp-base>`, after its `ninja-clang` configure +
    build with no tests (also the Linux sanity build of Task 10's GUI code and any Linux-only fix
    of Step 6):
    `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc 'set -o pipefail && . _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd rawreel && cmake --preset ninja-clang >/dev/null && cmake --build --preset ninja-clang 2>&1 | tail -n 30 && git diff --name-only -z --diff-filter=d <cp-base> HEAD -- ":(glob)src/**/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/*.cpp" ":(glob)tools/*.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_win32.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_msw.cpp" | xargs -0 -r -P "$(nproc)" -n 1 clang-tidy -p ../_build/rawreel/ninja-clang --quiet && echo TIDY-OK'`
  Expected: `100% tests passed` everywhere; no `Skipped` among `gpu`-labelled tests; no
  AddressSanitizer report; `TIDY-OK`. A compile error in our code is fixed; a toolchain or
  third-party failure is logged in the task record and reported. A failure is fixed (a `fix:`
  commit, then the Per-task checks and the failed check again) before the user's review of M3
  alpha (U-M3A).
- [ ] **Step 9: record and commit.** In the M3 plan file's `## Changes during execution`, add "Task
  11a: GUI completion": the ×5 results per configuration on Windows, each E6 item with the user's
  verdict, the R6 notes, the fixes (commit subjects), the G2 workflow result, and the line "WSLg,
  Xvfb and GTK checks: not run (U-WIN, v1.x)". No machine names, user names or paths.
  ```powershell
  git -C rawreel-workspace add docs/superpowers/plans
  @'
  docs: record the M3 GUI check

  Selftests five times in a row in Debug and Release on Windows, the manual checklist with the
  user's verdicts, and the workspace workflow for Tasks 8 to 11a. Linux GUI checks are not part
  of v1.0 (Windows only).

  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  '@ | git -C rawreel-workspace commit -F -
  ```
  Privacy lint for each changed repository, rawreel-workspace always:
  `cmake -DREPO=<repo> -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake`
  → `-- privacy-lint: clean (commits)`.

**Expected results:** 3 scenarios × 5 runs pass in Debug and Release on NVIDIA; E6 confirmed by

Debug green and FORMAT-OK when it changed; checkpoint: G2 workflow green (`vs2026` Debug and
Release, `vs2026-asan`), TIDY-OK; privacy lint clean.

(short sentences, no history). The text M2's final task left may differ from older wording, so find
each item's current sentence (design v2 §10 "Spec updates").
- **§1.1 and §15:** U-SCOPE: the v1.0 list (one stream of any supported format, full colour mapping
  incl. custom Kr/Kb and PQ/HLG tone mapping, play/pause/step/seek/loop, fit to window, raw + Y4M +

  table and key normaliser, format choice, drop routing, GUI argument parsing, status and info
  text), `rawreel_render` (fit compositor), `rawreel_gui`, `rawreel_cli`.
- **§4:** the Session executor; the timer thread (`ThreadTimer`, Windows high-resolution waitable
  timer); frames to the viewport through `IDisplaySink` (`DisplayState`), status polled every 50 ms;
  the in-flight cap K = 4 and stale results dropped instead of cancellation (`stop_token`
  cancellation is planned for M3.1 Task C, if triggered); the bottleneck from converter stats.

  `STORAGE_READ_WITHOUT_FORMAT` (or Task 0's R1 outcome).
- **§5.2:** device lost: the lost state, fail-fast submissions, bounded waits, safe destruction,
  `testing::injectDeviceLost`; the Session releases the stack and reports an error, and the user
  reopens (Reopen rebuilds the stack and reopens the stream at its frame); the first stack is built
  synchronously in `Session::create` (a startup GPU failure exits 4), and every later stack (Reopen,
  an open after a loss) is built on the I/O pool as a job of the open FIFO; automatic recovery and
  the GPU menu are v1.x; `--gpu N` keeps "one active GPU, selectable"; the device generation stays 1
  in v1.0; automatic recovery (v1.x) recreates the context's device with the next generation.

  ```powershell
  docs: describe the display path, colour defaults and tone mapping

  ```
**Checkpoint task (U-PROC):** Task 12 is the milestone's final checkpoint and covers everything:
Debug (Per-task), Release and ASan of every repository through the G3 workspace workflows (Step 4),
the ASan ×10 race checks of every risky task of the milestone (Step 4b), clang-tidy on all sources
of every repository with M3 code changes after its `ninja-clang` build (Step 6), the workspace
Linux sanity build (Step 5) and the privacy lint on all seven repositories (Step 7). Its docs
commits change no C++ file, so its Per-task clang-format is covered by Step 6.

  NVIDIA; two background calls):
  `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location rawreel-workspace; cmake --workflow --preset vs2026; Pop-Location`
  and the same with `vs2026-asan`. Expected: `100% tests passed` in every step; no `***Skipped` among
  `gpu`-labelled tests (list them with `ctest --test-dir _build/rawreel-workspace/vs2026 -C Release -L gpu -N`);
  the three `rawreel.gui_selftest.*` entries (`smoke`, `player`, `lost`) ran in `vs2026` (Debug and
  Release); no AddressSanitizer report.
- [ ] **Step 4b: the Windows race checks of every risky task** (final checkpoint; ×10 under

  - Task 1 (dvv-gpu loss tests, through CTest so that `VK_ADD_LAYER_PATH`, `DVV_LEAK_CHECK=fail`
    and the ASan runtime PATH of the entries apply):
    `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location dvv-gpu; cmake --preset vs2026-asan | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026-asan; ctest --preset vs2026-asan -R "^dvv_gpu\.(gpu|present)$" --repeat until-fail:10 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 5; Pop-Location`
  - Tasks 5, 6 and 7 (rawreel):
    `$env:DVV_REQUIRE_GPU='1'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION='sync'; $env:DVV_VK_VALIDATION_REQUIRED='1'; Push-Location rawreel; cmake --preset vs2026-asan | Out-Null; cmake --build --preset vs2026-asan; ctest --preset vs2026-asan -R "rawreel\.(core_session|unit)" --repeat until-fail:10 --output-on-failure 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 5; Pop-Location`
  Expected: `100% tests passed` after 10 runs each, no AddressSanitizer report.
- [ ] **Step 5: Linux sanity build of the workspace** (the milestone's one Linux sanity build,
  U-WIN; WSL, background, in parallel with Step 4; a fresh configure + build, no tests):
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc 'set -o pipefail && . _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd rawreel-workspace && cmake --preset ninja-clang --fresh 2>&1 | tail -n 20 && cmake --build --preset ninja-clang 2>&1 | tail -n 40 && echo SANITY-OK'`.
  Expected: `SANITY-OK`, no warning or error in our code; no `ctest` is run. A compile error in our
  code is a portability bug: fix it in the owning repository (`fix: …` commit with the trailer,
  portable code or a compiling stub, never a deletion), then rerun this step and Step 4 for the
  workspace. A toolchain or third-party failure is not fixed: record it (Step 9 and the plan's
  `## Changes during execution`) and report it to the controller.
- [ ] **Step 6: checkpoint lint** of every repository with M3 code changes (the full clang-format
  and clang-tidy on all sources in parallel; each builds its repository's `ninja-clang` tree first,
  no tests), one call per `<repo>` in `dvv-gpu`, `dvv-media` and `rawreel`:
  `wsl -d Ubuntu-24.04 -e bash -lc 'set -o pipefail && . _build/tools/linux/env.sh && cd <repo> && cmake --preset ninja-clang >/dev/null && cmake --build --preset ninja-clang 2>&1 | tail -n 30 && git ls-files -z "*.h" "*.cpp" | xargs -0 -r -P "$(nproc)" -n 8 clang-format --dry-run --Werror && git ls-files -z ":(glob)src/**/*.cpp" ":(glob)tests/*.cpp" ":(glob)tools/*.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_win32.cpp" ":(exclude,glob)**/*_msw.cpp" | xargs -0 -r -P "$(nproc)" -n 1 clang-tidy -p ../_build/<repo>/ninja-clang --quiet && echo LINT-OK'`.
  Expected `LINT-OK` each.

  `foreach ($r in 'rawreel-workspace','dvv-base','dvv-registry','dvv-platform','dvv-gpu','dvv-media','rawreel') { cmake -DREPO=$r -DMODE=commits "-DEXTRA_TERMS=$env:USERNAME;$env:COMPUTERNAME" -P rawreel-workspace/tools/privacy-lint.cmake }`.

## U-WIN changes

Source: the user decision U-WIN (2026-10-23, binding; decisions log): v1.0 supports Windows only,
the code stays portable, and nothing is tested on Linux. Per task, the full matrix is `vs2026` +
`vs2026-asan` (GPU tests on NVIDIA) + clang-format + clang-tidy + the privacy lint; one Linux sanity
build (WSL `ninja-clang` configure + build, no tests) runs in Task 12 and in tasks that change
Linux-only code or build files. No task was dropped; no task was added. Linux-only GUI work moved
to future.md as `[M3->v1.x] … (reason: U-WIN)` lines.

| Task | U-WIN changes |
|---|---|
| Header, Global Constraints | Goal names the Windows narrowing and the sanity build. New bullets: Platform (U-WIN), "the code stays portable", per-task full matrix, Linux sanity build rule (Task 12; Tasks 5, 8 and a Linux-touching 11a fix, through the lint build), accepted risk (no TSan). Toolchains: `ninja-clang` only, for the lint and the sanity build; `-asan`/`-tsan` not run. Shells: WSL only for lint and sanity build; the WSLg/xvfb line removed. GPU tests: lavapipe line removed; GUI selftests (`smoke`, `player`, `lost`, `perf`) and the 4K60 run are Windows only. Long runs and workflow groups without WSL/WSLg/xvfb runs. New block "Exit criteria under U-WIN" (E1–E7 and portability). |
| Part 1 conventions | WSL only for lint and sanity build; GPU env Windows only; full matrix = `vs2026`, `vs2026-asan` + lints (WSL preset loop removed); the lint recipe configures and builds `ninja-clang` first (clang-tidy needs the compile database and generated headers), no tests; a Linux sanity build recipe; failure policy (our compile errors fixed, toolchain/third-party failures logged). |

Notes:
- The clang-tidy lint needs a `ninja-clang` build tree, so every task's lint configures and builds
  `ninja-clang` in WSL (no tests), as CI's lint job does. That build is the lint's input; the
  milestone's named Linux sanity build is the fresh workspace build of Task 12 (plus Tasks 5, 8 and
  a Linux-touching 11a fix, where the lint build counts as it).
- Race-target cases (dvv-gpu injection, DisplayState, `lastRecorded`, Session, Task C cancel) stay
  in the suites and run in every Windows preset; their ×10 repeats run under `vs2026-asan`.
- Existing CI Linux jobs are left as they are unless they break; M3 adds no Linux test job.

  `yuv420p10le`, 60 frames) at `--inflight 1` and `--inflight 8`, three runs each, medians by

Recorded at Task 12 (changes made during Tasks 0-11a and the process decisions that applied):
- Fix 21b0260 (rawreel, Task 4 code found in Task 5 Step 2): the player dropped its readiness for
  frames that left the keep range only when it showed a frame, so after `seek(5)` from 20 and
  `seek(25)`, or loop off and on, it could show a frame whose leases the Session had released. It
  now prunes after every change of the keep range; new case "player: a frame that leaves the keep
  range is no longer ready".
- Session case 4 wording: without loop, playback pauses at the last index; play at the end
  restarts from frame 0 ("session: step, seek, home, end and loop", "player: the end pauses or
  loops").
- `PlayerLogic::target()` (plan addition): the pending seek, step, start or stall index, else the
  position; the Session's out-of-memory retry restarts there.
- The checkpoint ASan x10 regex is `rawreel\.(core_session|core_timer|unit|gpu)$` (controller
  decision from the Task 7 review), so it covers the Session, the timer and the renderer's
  `lastRecorded` race case; it supersedes the narrower `rawreel\.(core_session|unit)` of Task 12
  Step 4b.
- User decisions applied during execution:
  - U-WIN: Windows only for v1.0; no Linux test runs; one Linux sanity build per milestone.
  - U-PROC and U-PROC-tidy: lighter per-task checks; Release, ASan and clang-tidy at the
    checkpoints (Tasks 3, 6, 9, 11a, 12).
  - U-TEST: the sanity cycle (labels `sanity` and `package`, presets `vs2026-sanity` and
    `vs2026-small`, `tools/sanity.ps1`); package tests only in the full cycle.
  - U-BATCH: a stage's failures are collected, fixed together and re-run once.
  - U-M31: Tasks 11b, B and C, exit criterion E7 and its risk move to M3.1 (`m3.1-plan.md`, kept
    with the plans outside the repositories); their text above is kept for reference.
  - U-M3A: M3 alpha = Tasks 0-11a, ending with the Task 11a checkpoint and the user review; Task
    12 is M3 beta / release.
- Task S (sanity cycle, between alpha and Task 12): the `sanity` and `package` labels in every

Task B: F1 not built: drive-limited (M3.1 Task B Step 0, 2026-10-24):
- Branch: F1 (M2 Task 14 named "M3 item: F1", bottleneck Produce at 0.46 GB/s; M3 Task 0 recorded
  "Task B: needed").
- Setup: an NVIDIA desktop GPU (the NVIDIA driver of the time), Windows 11, rawreel
  `vs2026-release` build of the m3 heads, local NVMe SSD, no validation layer, 60 frames of
  3840x2160 yuv420p10le (24883200 bytes each, a 1.49 GB file written by `dump --via cpu` in the
  temp folder and deleted afterwards). Reader: `unbuffered` in all six runs.
- Six runs, `bench --frames 60 --inflight K --json` (Produce GB/s = bytes / summed per-read
  nanoseconds, as the JSON reports it; frames/s end to end):
  - K = 1: Produce 1.823, 1.756, 1.826 GB/s (27.9, 39.5, 40.0 frames/s). Median 1.823 GB/s.
  - K = 8: Produce 0.455, 0.405, 0.387 GB/s (56.3, 62.1, 62.0 frames/s). Median 0.405 GB/s.
- Gate (K = 8 median ≥ 1.2 × K = 1 median, i.e. ≥ 2.19 GB/s): 0.405 GB/s, 0.22 × K = 1. Failed;
  F1 (IOCP) is not built and Steps 1-3 did not run. No code changed.
- Cross-check by wall clock: the bench's I/O pool has 4 threads, so at K = 8 at most 4 reads run
  at once; the median K = 8 run's reads therefore took at least 3.684 s / 4 = 0.921 s of its
  0.967 s wall, an aggregate drive rate of 1.54 to 1.62 GB/s, not above the 1.82 GB/s that one
  read at a time reaches. More reads in flight do not raise the drive's throughput, so the reads
  are bandwidth-bound, not latency-bound, and a deeper queue (IOCP) has nothing to gain.
- Reading of the M2 number: Produce GB/s divides bytes by the summed time of reads that overlap,
  so with K > 1 it falls with K (per-read time grows as reads share the drive). M2's 0.46 GB/s at
  K = 4 is this effect, not a slow drive: one read at a time moves 1.82 GB/s, about 73 frames/s of
  4K yuv420p10le, above the 1.49 GB/s that 60 frames/s need.
- Next R6 items (upload + unpack in one submission, reads into staging F3): not built; the plan
  takes them only on a controller decision after a measurement. The numbers go to the controller
  for the arbiter; Task 11b measures E7 without Task B code.

Task B2: buffered reads with a sequential hint (M3.1, 2026-10-24; user decisions U-HW, U-IO,
U-SIMPLE):
- Replaces F1 (IOCP) and the Task B gate rule. U-HW: target typical modern consumer PCs (NVMe or
  SATA SSD), prefer simple solutions. U-IO: parallel reads issued from one thread with an
  overlapped read-ahead window, no reader or IOCP pool. U-SIMPLE (later, binding): measure first;
  build the overlapped read-ahead only if plain buffered reads with the OS read-ahead are more
  than about 25 % slower than the best option and below the stream rate (4K60 yuv420p10le needs
  1.49 GB/s); otherwise use buffered reads with the sequential hint, remove the NO_BUFFERING path
  if it is not significantly faster, and add no overlapped API.
- Measured before any code (spec §16 has the table): `rawreel-cli bench --read-only` (new in this
  task), 120 frames of 4K yuv420p10le, an NVMe SSD (PCIe 3), Release, three runs, medians, cold
  cache purged before each run. Unbuffered: 1.74 / 1.77 / 1.92 GB/s at K = 1 / 2 / 4, warm the
  same. Buffered + `FILE_FLAG_SEQUENTIAL_SCAN`: 1.22 / 1.52 / 1.82 GB/s cold, 2.41 / 5.10 / 6.85
  GB/s warm. Full pipeline at K = 4: 56.7 → 59.3 fps cold, 59.6 → 72.4 fps warm.
- Decision: buffered reads with the sequential hint. At the player's K = 4 reads in flight (D11,
  4-thread I/O pool) they are 5.5 % below unbuffered from a cold cache and above the stream rate,
  and much faster from a warm cache while the OS keeps the file cached (full GPU pipeline runs
  evicted part of it). An interleaved cold A/B series with the committed code confirmed the
  numbers (K = 4: 1.83 against 1.91 GB/s; full pipeline 54.6 against 51.4 fps). At K = 1 they are 30 % below and under the stream rate, but
  the player never reads one frame at a time. The overlapped read-ahead (option c) was not built:
  2 to 4 reads from one thread could at best match the 2 to 4 parallel reads that the existing
  pool already issues, and the brief's "limit produce to 1 per file source" was only needed for
  it, so the store is unchanged.
- Code: dvv-platform `FileReader` keeps `open(path)`, `size()`, `readAt()`, `path()` and
  `allocateAligned`; `FileReadMode`, `alignment()`, `unbuffered()`, the probe, the fallback and
  the opener seam are removed. Windows opens with `FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED | FILE_FLAG_SEQUENTIAL_SCAN`
  (overlapped keeps concurrent reads on one handle parallel); Linux with `posix_fadvise`
  (`POSIX_FADV_SEQUENTIAL`). dvv-media raw and Y4M read exactly the frame (alignment 1, host frames
  64-byte aligned); `SourceInfo::reader` is always "buffered". rawreel-cli `bench --read-only`
  reports the read stage's wall clock ("read wall clock:", JSON `readOnly`); `--inflight` is the
  read depth.

Task B3: file reads per U-IO2 (M3.1, 2026-10-25; user decision U-IO2, which supersedes U-IO, the
Task B gate and B2's "remove NO_BUFFERING"):
- dvv-platform `FileReader` has two modes behind one interface (`FileReadMode`): `Buffered` (B2's
  path, the portable one) and `Unbuffered` (the restored M2 path: `FILE_FLAG_NO_BUFFERING` /
  `O_DIRECT`, sector-aligned offset, size and address, the aligned superset read into aligned
  memory), plus `Auto`. `Auto` on Windows runs the open-time check (the unbuffered open, the
  volume's sector size from `FileStorageInfo`, known, a power of two and at most 64 KiB, and the
  first aligned read); any failure opens that file buffered with one info line naming the reason.
  On other platforms `Auto` is buffered. An explicit `Unbuffered` that fails is `Errc::Io`, never a
  fallback. `modeText()` gives "unbuffered", "buffered" or "buffered (fallback: <reason>)".
  Only the open flags differ between the modes; the read call, the overlapped handle and the
  per-read event are shared. `open(path)` keeps `Buffered` as its default for header reads.
- One read at a time per file source. Measured first: there is no file-specific parallel-read
  code. The parallelism came from three generic parts: the player's K = 4 requests in flight (the
  window check in `PlayerLogic::takeRequests`, 6 lines, for every pipeline stage, not only reads),
  the 4-thread I/O pool (`IO_THREADS` in rawreel-gui, `makeThreadPool(4)` in rawreel-cli, 1 line
  each) onto which raw and Y4M `produce()` hop (`co_await resumeOn(ctx.io())`, 1 line each), and a
  thread-safe `FileReader::readAt` (the per-read `OVERLAPPED` and event, about 15 lines in
  `readNative`, kept so concurrent reads on one handle are not serialised). The store has no
  produce limit. No configuration limits only file reads: K = 1 would serialise the GPU stages
  too, one I/O thread would change the I/O pool (also used for the factory's head reads and the
  stack builds), and the store's existing `FrameStoreOptions::io` setting with a `SerialExecutor`
  over the pool would also run whatever follows the read on that lane until the next hop
  (HostToStaging, Upload and GpuUnpack start without a hop). So it is code: a mutex around the
  platform read in `FileReader::readAt` (one member, three lines). The I/O pool, the player's K
  and prefetch window and the thread pools are unchanged.
- dvv-media: `SourceRequest::fileReads` (default `Auto`) reaches `FileReader::open` for raw and
  Y4M; both plan the aligned superset again and allocate host frames aligned to max(alignment,
  64); `SourceInfo::reader` is `modeText()`.
- rawreel: the config file `rawreel.json` in the folder of the running executable
  (`dvv::paths::executablePath`; the user moved it there from `%APPDATA%` during the task),
  `--config <file>` on rawreel-gui and rawreel-cli (info, dump, bench). Key `io.fileReads`:
  `auto`, `unbuffered`, `buffered`. Missing: defaults; unreadable, invalid JSON or a bad value:
  one warning and the defaults; unknown keys ignored. `core/config` is a typed loader with its
  own tests. `SessionOptions::fileReads` sets every open's request (reopens included). `info`
  prints `reads: <mode>` (was `reader:`; the JSON key stays `reader`), the GUI Info pane
  `  reads: <mode>`. No `rawreel.json` is shipped (packaging, M4).
- Bench (one data point, U-HW): `rawreel-cli bench --read-only`, 120 frames of 4K yuv420p10le
  (2.99 GB), the NVMe SSD (PCIe 3) of Task B2, Release, no validation, cold (the file's cached
  pages purged before every run), unbuffered and buffered interleaved, three runs, medians. One
  read in flight: unbuffered 2.00 GB/s (80.3 fps), buffered 1.32 GB/s (53.2 fps); 4K60 of this
  format needs 1.49 GB/s. `--inflight 4` gives the same (1.95 and 1.28 GB/s), because the reader
  now keeps one read in flight. Full pipeline at K = 4: unbuffered 64.8 fps, buffered 44.5 fps (B2
  had 51 to 57 fps for both with 4 parallel reads). So the Windows default meets the stream rate
  with one read in flight; a file that falls back to buffered reads (network drive, unusual sector
  size) does not on this drive, which U-IO2 accepts.
- Review fix wave (2 reviewers, all Important items accepted): the overlapped handle and the
  per-read event are gone (reads are serialised, U-SIMPLE): both modes open synchronous handles
  and read with a positional `ReadFile`. One-read bench, the old and new builds interleaved on one
  new file, three runs, medians: unbuffered 1.93 → 1.94 GB/s (77.5 → 78.1 fps), buffered 1.16 →
  1.41 GB/s (46.5 → 56.7 fps). A missing `--config` file now gives one warning and the defaults (a
  missing default `rawreel.json` stays silent); the fallback log line reads "the unbuffered check
  failed (<reason>)"; bench text prints `reads <mode>`; the rawreel README and spec §16 are fixed.

Task 11b: perf (M3.1, 2026-10-25; E7):
- Code: rawreel 2f6706b, `rawreel-gui --selftest perf <uri> [--inflight K]` and `core/perf_stats`
  (4 unit cases); the `--inflight` plumbing already existed. The 640x360 smoke run exits 0 with
  one parseable JSON line.
- Setup (one data point, U-HW): an NVIDIA desktop GPU (the NVIDIA driver of the time, Vulkan 1.4),
  Windows 11, Release build, validation off, an NVMe SSD (PCIe 3), reads
  `unbuffered` (the default, no `rawreel.json`), a 120 Hz display, frame maximised,
  K = 4 (warm-up ended with readyAhead 8). File: 600 frames of 4K `yuv420p10le` noise,
  `perf_3840x2160_yuv420p10le_60.yuv`, 14,929,920,000 bytes (`info`: 3840x2160, 60/1, 600 frames,
  `reads: unbuffered`).
- The three runs (K = 4):
  - `{"targetFps":60.000,"actualFps":58.013,"stalls":4,"bottleneck":"disk","frames":600,"presented":745,"overwritten":9,"p99TickLatenessMs":1.887,"inflight":4,"seekMedianMs":50.0,"seekMaxMs":77.8}`
  - `{"targetFps":60.000,"actualFps":60.003,"stalls":0,"bottleneck":"disk","frames":600,"presented":764,"overwritten":0,"p99TickLatenessMs":0.045,"inflight":4,"seekMedianMs":57.6,"seekMaxMs":83.7}`
  - `{"targetFps":60.000,"actualFps":59.999,"stalls":0,"bottleneck":"disk","frames":600,"presented":765,"overwritten":0,"p99TickLatenessMs":0.246,"inflight":4,"seekMedianMs":59.0,"seekMaxMs":106.8}`
- Median by `actualFps` (run 3): 59.999 fps, 0 stalls, 765 presented, 0 overwritten, p99 tick
  lateness 0.246 ms, seek median 59.0 ms, max 106.8 ms. **E7 verdict: pass** (≥ 59.5 fps, 0 stalls,
  overwritten ≤ 6). The 120 Hz display is twice the stream rate, so no R13 beat.
- The margin is thin: run 1 missed (58.013 fps, 4 stalls, 9 overwritten). Further runs, same
  setup: a second K = 4 series 60.000, 60.002, 60.002 fps, 0 stalls, 0 overwritten each; K = 6
  60.001, 60.001, 59.999 fps, 0 stalls, 0 overwritten; K = 8 60.001, 60.000, 59.737 fps, the last
  with 1 stall and 4 overwritten. 10 of 12 runs pass the E7 rule; both misses are stalls. All 12
  runs (series, K, actualFps, stalls, presented, overwritten, p99 tick lateness ms, seek median /
  max ms):

  | Run | K | fps | stalls | presented | overwritten | p99 | seek |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | 1.1 | 4 | 58.013 | 4 | 745 | 9 | 1.887 | 50.0 / 77.8 |
  | 1.2 | 4 | 60.003 | 0 | 764 | 0 | 0.045 | 57.6 / 83.7 |
  | 1.3 | 4 | 59.999 | 0 | 765 | 0 | 0.246 | 59.0 / 106.8 |
  | 2.1 | 4 | 60.000 | 0 | 762 | 0 | 0.220 | 61.9 / 99.3 |
  | 2.2 | 4 | 60.002 | 0 | 765 | 0 | 0.183 | 55.8 / 85.2 |
  | 2.3 | 4 | 60.002 | 0 | 762 | 0 | 0.184 | 53.0 / 80.1 |
  | 3.1 | 6 | 60.001 | 0 | 765 | 0 | 0.152 | 60.5 / 118.8 |
  | 3.2 | 6 | 60.001 | 0 | 764 | 0 | 0.147 | 61.0 / 118.3 |
  | 3.3 | 6 | 59.999 | 0 | 764 | 0 | 0.230 | 60.5 / 122.5 |
  | 4.1 | 8 | 60.001 | 0 | 761 | 0 | 0.199 | 60.8 / 115.5 |
  | 4.2 | 8 | 60.000 | 0 | 765 | 0 | 0.373 | 62.8 / 113.3 |
  | 4.3 | 8 | 59.737 | 1 | 756 | 4 | 0.507 | 69.1 / 118.8 |

- `presented` is about 76 presents per second for 60 shows per second: something presents besides
  the new frames (not investigated; each extra present costs GPU time at full size). The play step
  is polled every 50 ms, so the counter deltas (stalls, presented, overwritten) can include up to
  about 3 shows after the 601st; `showTiming` uses exactly 601. The effect is negligible.
- Per stage (`rawreel-cli bench`, same file, 60 frames, K = 4, three runs, medians): up to unpack
  60.4, 61.6, 61.0 frames/s (median 61.0, 1.52 GB/s); summed stage time per frame Produce 36.6 ms
  (it includes the wait for the one read in flight with 4 requests queued, so it is not a read
  time), HostToStaging 3.36 ms, Upload 3.22 ms, GpuUnpack 1.37 ms (bench 2, the median run by
  frames/s: Produce 2,161,142,900 ns, HostToStaging 201,537,800, Upload 193,219,100, GpuUnpack
  82,079,200 over 60 runs each, 1.533 GB/s wall clock). `bench --read-only` (the read
  stage's wall clock): 72.2, 70.7, 68.4 frames/s (median 70.7, 1.76 GB/s). Upload + GpuUnpack, the
  M2 risk of 16.6 ms, is now 4.6 ms per frame. The read is the slowest stage (14.1 ms per frame
  alone); the pipeline up to unpack has about 1.7 % headroom over 60 fps, the read stage alone
  about 18 %.
- `bottleneck` is "disk" in every run, also in the passing ones and the 640x360 smoke run: the
  Session names a stage whenever the player's measured fps is below 95 % of the target, which is
  true right after PlayPause while the estimate settles, and the scenario keeps the last non-empty
  value. Here it agrees with the bench (Produce), but it is not a per-stall cause. Minor item.
- Seek latency: at most 122.5 ms over all 12 runs, so `seekMaxMs <= 250`: **Task C not
  triggered**. The 4K file was deleted after the runs.
- Candidate fixes for more margin, in the order the measurement gives (M3.1 final review; none
  started, none built in M3.1; candidates for v1.x or M4 Task P):
  1. The read path: the pipeline takes 16.4 ms per frame (61.0 fps) against 14.1 ms for the read
     alone, so about 2.3 ms of non-read work does not overlap the read. Name that stage first.
  2. Reads straight into staging (F3: drops the 3.36 ms HostToStaging copy and its memory traffic)
     and/or a larger prefetch window cap for 4K. `session.cpp` clamps W to 8 frames (133 ms at 60
     fps); with about 1.7 % refill headroom, a window drained by one read hiccup takes seconds to
     refill, so a second hiccup stalls. K = 6 and 8 did not help because W stays 8 and reads are
     serialised (U-IO2). The cap is one constant (U-SIMPLE).
  3. Last: upload + unpack in one submission (4.6 ms per frame, not limiting).

  More than one read in flight per file contradicts U-IO2 and needs the user's approval. **M4 Task P
  is not opened:** E7 passed, so there is no M3.1 miss to carry; a later E7 miss opens it with this
  order.
